</details>


### [170] [Mana: Dexterous Manipulation of Articulated Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13677)
*Zhao-Heng Yin,Guanya Shi,Pieter Abbeel,C. Karen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Mana是一个受计算机动画启发的通用仿真到现实框架，通过粗到细的流水线将程序化生成的关键帧转化为灵巧操作轨迹，实现了多种铰接工具的零样本仿真到现实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铰接工具操作因需要协调内部自由度与接触丰富的交互而极具挑战性，现有工作多集中于刚体物体，铰接工具操作因其物理复杂性和功能性抓取与操作策略的学习难度而未被充分探索。

Method: 受计算机动画启发，Mana采用粗到细流水线：首先通过程序化生成抓取关键帧（仅需少量鼠标点击指定功能可供性），然后结合运动规划和强化学习将这些关键帧转化为完整的操作轨迹。数据生成过程高度自动化。

Result: 在涵盖不同尺度和关节类型的四种铰接工具上，Mana实现了抓取和手内操作的零样本仿真到现实迁移。

Conclusion: Mana展示了将灵巧操作重新诠释为动画问题的有效性和可扩展性，为铰接工具操作提供了一种可规模化的方法。

Abstract: Articulated tool manipulation remains a major challenge in dexterous robotics due to the need to coordinate internal degrees of freedom and contact-rich interactions. While prior work has largely focused on rigid objects, articulated tool use remains underexplored because of its physical complexity and the difficulty of learning functional grasping and manipulation policies. We present Mana (Manipulation Animator), a general sim-to-real framework that reinterprets dexterous manipulation as an animation problem. Inspired by computer animation, Mana employs a coarse-to-fine pipeline that transforms procedurally-generated grasp keyframes into manipulation trajectories through motion planning and reinforcement learning. The data generation process is largely automatic, requiring only a few mouse clicks to specify functional affordances (<1 minute per tool). Across four articulated tools spanning different scales and joint types, Mana achieves zero-shot sim-to-real transfer for both grasping and in-hand manipulation, demonstrating a scalable approach to dexterous articulated tool use.

</details>


### [171] [Learning Robot Safety from Sparse Human Feedback using Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.04823)
*Aaron O. Feldman,Joseph A. Vincent,Maximilian Adang,JunEn Low,Mac Schwager*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于人类二元反馈和共形预测的机器人安全保证方法，通过识别策略可能出错的危险状态区域（潜在潜在空间），构建具有保证漏报率的预警系统，并在四旋翼飞行器视觉运动控制任务中验证了该方法能有效检测和改善策略安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户定义的约束可能遗漏边缘情况、从安全数据训练的策略可能变得不安全、安全性具有主观性，这些挑战使得确保机器人安全变得困难。

Method: 展示策略轨迹给人类标注者获取二元安全反馈；利用共形预测（conformal prediction）结合最近邻分类，在潜在空间中识别包含指定比例未来策略错误的状态区域；通过检测机器人是否进入可疑不安全区域，构建具有保证漏报率的预警系统。

Result: 在四旋翼飞行器视觉运动控制的门穿越任务中，该系统能有效检测策略失败；基于避免可疑不安全区域的策略改进方法，在30次飞行6个导航任务中提升了模型预测控制器的安全性。

Conclusion: 该方法具有样本效率高、无需像传统共形预测那样保留数据、能保证误报率可控等优势，为机器人安全提供了一种结合人类主观偏好与统计保证的实用框架。

Abstract: Ensuring robot safety can be challenging; user-defined constraints can miss edge cases, policies can become unsafe even when trained from safe data, and safety can be subjective. Thus, we learn about robot safety by showing policy trajectories to a human who flags unsafe behavior. From this binary feedback, we use the statistical method of conformal prediction to identify a region of states, potentially in learned latent space, guaranteed to contain a user-specified fraction of future policy errors. Our method is sample-efficient, as it builds on nearest neighbor classification and avoids withholding data as is common with conformal prediction. By alerting if the robot reaches the suspected unsafe region, we obtain a warning system that mimics the human's safety preferences with guaranteed miss rate. From video labeling, our system can detect when a quadcopter visuomotor policy will fail to steer through a designated gate. We present an approach for policy improvement by avoiding the suspected unsafe region. With it we improve a model predictive controller's safety, as shown in experimental testing with 30 quadcopter flights across 6 navigation tasks. Code and videos are provided.

</details>


### [172] [Data-Driven Soft Robot Control via Adiabatic Spectral Submanifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.10919)
*Roshan S. Kaundinya,John Irvin Alora,Jonas G. Matt,Luis A. Pabon,Marco Pavone,George Haller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于绝热谱子流形（aSSM）的数据驱动模型预测控制策略，用于软体机器人的轨迹跟踪。该方法利用软体机器人过阻尼振动的快慢时间尺度分离特性，从数据中提取低维吸引不变流形，在复杂非线性路径上实现了比传统线性数据驱动方法提升高达10倍的跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人的机械复杂性给基于模型的控制带来了巨大挑战，线性数据驱动模型在复杂空间路径上控制效果不佳，难以处理显著的非线性行为。

Method: 基于绝热谱子流形（aSSM）理论设计模型预测控制策略：利用过阻尼机器人内部振动衰减远快于期望运动速度的特性，从路径上提取低维吸引不变流形（aSSM）作为主导动力学模型，实现纯数据驱动的控制方案。

Result: 在高保真有限元软体躯干机器人模型和Cosserat杆弹性软臂模型上验证，5-6维aSSM降阶模型在所有闭环控制任务中的跟踪性能优于其他数据驱动方法，性能提升高达10倍，且对实验噪声具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: aSSM-based模型预测控制能有效处理软体机器人的非线性动力学，通过低维降阶模型实现高精度轨迹跟踪，为软体机器人的模型控制提供了一种高效的数据驱动解决方案。

Abstract: The mechanical complexity of soft robots creates significant challenges for their model-based control. Specifically, linear data-driven models have struggled to control soft robots on complex, spatially extended paths that explore regions with significant nonlinear behavior. To account for these nonlinearities, we develop here a model-predictive control strategy based on the recent theory of adiabatic spectral submanifolds (aSSMs). This theory is applicable because the internal vibrations of heavily overdamped robots decay at a speed that is much faster than the desired speed of the robot along its intended path. In that case, low-dimensional attracting invariant manifolds (aSSMs) emanate from the path and carry the dominant dynamics of the robot. Aided by this recent theory, we devise an aSSM-based model-predictive control scheme purely from data. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our data-driven model in tracking dynamic trajectories across diverse tasks. We validate on high-fidelity, high-dimensional finite-element models of a soft trunk robot and Cosserat-rod-based elastic soft arms, with additional experiments confirming robust performance even in the presence of experimental noise. Notably, we find that five- or six-dimensional aSSM-reduced models outperform the tracking performance of other data-driven modeling methods by a factor up to 10 across all closed-loop control tasks.

</details>


### [173] [Adaptive Model-Predictive Control of a Soft Continuum Robot Using a Physics-Informed Neural Network Based on Cosserat Rod Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.12681)
*Johann Licher,Max Bartholdt,Henrik Krauss,Tim-Lukas Habich,Thomas Seel,Moritz Schappler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于域解耦物理信息神经网络（DD-PINN）的实时非线性模型预测控制（MPC）框架，用于软连续体机器人（SCRs）的动态控制。DD-PINN作为动态Cosserat杆模型的替代模型，加速比高达44,000倍，并结合无迹卡尔曼滤波从末端执行器位置测量中估计模型状态和弯曲柔顺性。在GPU上运行的进化MPC在70 Hz下实现了仿真中末端执行器位置误差低于3 mm（作动器长度的2.3%），实际实验中达到类似精度和高达3.55 m/s²的加速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软连续体机器人（SCRs）的动态控制因精确动态模型的高计算需求而具有挑战性。现有的数据驱动方法（如Koopman算子方法）缺乏适应性且无法重构完整机器人形状，限制了其应用。

Method: 提出域解耦物理信息神经网络（DD-PINN）作为动态Cosserat杆模型的替代模型（加速44,000倍），结合无迹卡尔曼滤波（UKF）估计状态和弯曲柔顺性，并实现基于GPU的70 Hz非线性进化MPC控制框架。

Result: 仿真中末端执行器位置误差低于3 mm（作动器长度的2.3%），实际实验中达到类似精度和高达3.55 m/s²的加速度，控制器在GPU上以70 Hz运行。

Conclusion: 提出的DD-PINN结合进化MPC框架实现了SCRs的实时高精度动态控制，兼具计算效率和模型适应性，为软体机器人的实际应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Dynamic control of soft continuum robots (SCRs) holds great potential for expanding their applications, but remains a challenging problem due to the high computational demands of accurate dynamic models. While data-driven approaches like Koopman-operator-based methods have been proposed, they typically lack adaptability and cannot reconstruct the full robot shape, limiting their applicability. This work introduces a real-time-capable nonlinear model-predictive control (MPC) framework for SCRs based on a domain-decoupled physics-informed neural network (DD-PINN) with adaptable bending stiffness. The DD-PINN serves as a surrogate for the dynamic Cosserat rod model with a speed-up factor of up to 44,000. It is also used within an unscented Kalman filter for estimating the model states and bending compliance from end-effector position measurements. We implement a nonlinear evolutionary MPC running at 70 Hz on the GPU. In simulation, it demonstrates accurate tracking of dynamic trajectories and setpoint control with end-effector position errors below 3 mm (2.3\% of the actuator's length). In real-world experiments, the controller achieves similar accuracy and accelerations up to 3.55 m/s2.

</details>


### [174] [GLIDE: A Coordinated Aerial-Ground Framework for Search and Rescue in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.14210)
*Seth Farrell,Chenghao Li,Henrik I. Christensen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了GLIDE框架，通过两架无人机与一辆无人地面车辆协作，实现未知环境下的快速遇险者定位与障碍感知导航。无人机分工为"目标搜索"和"地形侦察"，地面车辆融合空中与本地感知进行A*路径规划与重规划，硬件实验与仿真验证了该方法可缩短到达时间并提升导航安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知环境的搜索救援任务中，单一地面车辆视野受限、感知范围不足，难以快速定位遇险者并规划安全路径；而无人机虽有全局视野，但续航和载荷有限。因此需要空地协同，充分利用各自优势实现高效、安全的搜救。

Method: 提出GLIDE框架：1) 目标搜索无人机实时进行机载遇险者检测与地理配准，为地面车辆提名目标点；2) 地形侦察无人机飞在地面车辆规划路线前方，提供中程可通行性更新；3) 地面车辆融合空中线索与本地感知，使用A*算法进行高效路径规划与持续重规划。硬件实验采用GEM e6高尔夫球车作为UGV、两台X500无人机作为UAV，并进行了仿真消融实验。

Result: 实验结果表明，无人机间明确角色分工，结合地形侦察与引导规划，能够缩短到达时间并提升导航安全性，在时间关键的搜救任务中表现更优。

Conclusion: GLIDE框架通过空地多机器人协同、无人机角色分离与地形预侦察，有效提升了未知环境搜救任务的速度与安全性，验证了该框架在实际硬件平台上的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: We present a cooperative aerial-ground search-and-rescue (SAR) framework that pairs two unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) with an unmanned ground vehicle (UGV) to achieve rapid victim localization and obstacle-aware navigation in unknown environments. We dub this framework Guided Long-horizon Integrated Drone Escort (GLIDE), highlighting the UGV's reliance on UAV guidance for long-horizon planning. In our framework, a goal-searching UAV executes real-time onboard victim detection and georeferencing to nominate goals for the ground platform, while a terrain-scouting UAV flies ahead of the UGV's planned route to provide mid-level traversability updates. The UGV fuses aerial cues with local sensing to perform time-efficient A* planning and continuous replanning as information arrives. Additionally, we present a hardware demonstration (using a GEM e6 golf cart as the UGV and two X500 UAVs) to evaluate end-to-end SAR mission performance and include simulation ablations to assess the planning stack in isolation from detection. Empirical results demonstrate that explicit role separation across UAVs, coupled with terrain scouting and guided planning, improves reach time and navigation safety in time-critical SAR missions.

</details>


### [175] [Active Semantic Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.05430)
*Huayi Tang,Pratik Chaudhari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多层级场景图和大语言模型的主动语义感知方法，用于机器人在未知室内环境中进行高效探索和语义理解，通过预测未观测区域的场景结构并计算信息增益来指导路径规划，在仿真和真实机器人上验证了方法优于现有方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动感知方法缺乏高层语义推理能力，无法有效利用语义信息指导探索，例如区分未知区域的不同可能性（如门后是厨房还是卧室）。

Method: 构建紧凑的多层场景图（rooms, objects, walls, windows等节点），利用LLM从未观测区域的部分观测中采样合理的场景图假设，并基于场景图计算候选航点的信息增益，从而实现空间语义推理驱动的主动探索。

Result: 在3D室内仿真环境和真实Unitree Go 2机器人上实验，定性和定量分析表明该方法能更快、更准确地获取环境中的高层和低层语义信息，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 将LLM与场景图结合用于主动语义感知是有效的，能够实现更智能的空间语义推理和探索决策。

Abstract: We develop an approach for active semantic perception, which refers to using the semantics of the scene for tasks such as exploration. We build a compact, multi-layer scene graph that can represent large, complex indoor environments at various levels of abstraction, e.g., nodes corresponding to rooms, objects, walls, windows etc., as well as fine-grained details of their geometry. We develop a procedure based on large language models (LLMs) to sample new plausible scene graphs of unobserved regions that are consistent with partial observations of the scene. We develop a procedure to compute the information gain of a potential waypoint upon this scene graph to enable sophisticated spatial reasoning: for example, of the two doors that lead out of the living room, one probably leads to the kitchen and the other to the bedroom. We evaluate our approach in realistic 3D indoor apartments in simulation and also on a Unitree Go 2 robot in the real world. Qualitative and quantitative analysis shows that our approach can pin down high-level and low-level semantic information in the environment quickly and more accurately than existing approaches.

</details>


### [176] [Miniature Testbed for Validating Multi-Agent Cooperative Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.11022)
*Hyunchul Bae,Eunjae Lee,Jehyeop Han,Minhee Kang,Jaehyeon Kim,Junggeun Seo,Minkyun Noh,Heejin Ahn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文设计并实现了一个1:15比例的微型测试平台CIVAT，用于验证协同自动驾驶，该平台集成了智能路侧基础设施（含感知、边缘计算和通信能力），并通过ROS2和Wi-Fi实现V2V/V2I通信，最后通过基础设施感知和交叉口管理实验验证了系统有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试平台均未采用具备感知、边缘计算和通信能力的智能基础设施，无法有效验证协同自动驾驶的关键功能。

Method: 设计1:15比例微型测试平台CIVAT，包含缩微城市地图、搭载车载传感器的自动驾驶车辆和智能基础设施；通过共享Wi-Fi和ROS2框架集成V2V和V2I通信（采用发布-订阅模式），实现车辆与基础设施间的信息交换。

Result: 通过基础设施感知和交叉口管理实验，验证了该测试平台能够支持协同自动驾驶功能的实现。

Conclusion: CIVAT测试平台填补了现有测试床缺乏智能基础设施的空白，为协同自动驾驶的验证提供了有效的实验工具。

Abstract: Cooperative autonomous driving, which extends vehicle autonomy by enabling real-time collaboration between vehicles and smart roadside infrastructure, remains a challenging yet essential problem. However, none of the existing testbeds employ smart infrastructure equipped with sensing, edge computing, and communication capabilities. To address this gap, we design and implement a 1:15-scale miniature testbed, CIVAT, for validating cooperative autonomous driving, consisting of a scaled urban map, autonomous vehicles with onboard sensors, and smart infrastructure. The proposed testbed integrates V2V and V2I communication with the publish-subscribe pattern through a shared Wi-Fi and ROS2 framework, enabling information exchange between vehicles and infrastructure to realize cooperative driving functionality. As a case study, we validate the system through infrastructure-based perception and intersection management experiments.

</details>


### [177] [Learning Visually Interpretable Oscillator Networks for Soft Continuum Robots from Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.18322)
*Henrik Krauss,Johann Licher,Naoya Takeishi,Annika Raatz,Takehisa Yairi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出ABCD模块和VON网络，通过注意力机制和2D潜振荡器网络实现软体连续体机器人动力学的可解释性学习，在双段机器人上预测误差降低5.8倍（Koopman算子）和3.5倍（振荡网络）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从视频学习软体连续体机器人动力学的方法缺乏可解释性或依赖先验假设，基于模型的方法需要先验知识和手动设计，作者旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出两个核心组件：(1) 注意力广播解码器（ABCD），作为自编码器潜动力学学习的即插即用模块，生成像素级注意力图，定位每个潜维度的贡献并过滤静态背景；(2) 视觉振荡器网络（VON），一种2D潜振荡器网络，与ABCD注意力图耦合，实现学习质量、耦合刚度和力的可视化。

Result: 在单段和双段SCR上验证，ABCD模型显著提升多步预测精度：Koopman算子误差降低5.8倍，振荡网络误差降低3.5倍；VON能自主发现振荡器的链式结构。

Conclusion: 这种全数据驱动方法产生了紧凑、具备机械可解释性的模型，对未来的控制应用具有潜在价值。

Abstract: Learning soft continuum robot (SCR) dynamics from video offers flexibility but existing methods lack interpretability or rely on prior assumptions. Model-based approaches require prior knowledge and manual design. We bridge this gap by introducing: (1) The Attention Broadcast Decoder (ABCD), a plug-and-play module for autoencoder-based latent dynamics learning that generates pixel-accurate attention maps localizing each latent dimension's contribution while filtering static backgrounds, enabling visual interpretability via spatially grounded latents and on-image overlays. (2) Visual Oscillator Networks (VONs), a 2D latent oscillator network coupled to ABCD attention maps for on-image visualization of learned masses, coupling stiffness, and forces, thereby enabling mechanical interpretability. We validate our approach on single- and double-segment SCRs, demonstrating that ABCD-based models significantly improve multi-step prediction accuracy with 5.8x error reduction for Koopman operators and 3.5x for oscillator networks on a two-segment robot. VONs autonomously discover a chain structure of oscillators. This fully data-driven approach yields compact, mechanically interpretable models with potential relevance for future control applications.

</details>


### [178] [DiskChunGS: Large-Scale 3D Gaussian SLAM Through Chunk-Based Memory Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.23030)
*Casimir Feldmann,Maximum Wilder-Smith,Vaishakh Patil,Michael Oechsle,Michael Niemeyer,Keisuke Tateno,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DiskChunGS，一种基于外存管理的可扩展3DGS SLAM系统，通过将场景划分为空间块并仅在GPU内存中保留活跃区域，解决了3DGS SLAM在内存容量上的可扩展性瓶颈，能够在Replica、TUM-RGBD、KITTI等数据集上实现大规模全局一致的3D重建，并首次完整跑完所有11个KITTI序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS SLAM系统受限于GPU内存容量，无法扩展到大规模场景，存在根本性的可扩展性限制。

Method: 采用外存(out-of-core)管理策略，将场景划分为空间块(chunks)，仅将活跃区域保留在GPU内存中，非活跃区域存储于磁盘，并与现有SLAM框架的位姿估计和闭环检测无缝集成。

Result: 在Replica、TUM-RGBD和KITTI数据集上验证，唯一完成所有11个KITTI序列且无内存故障，同时实现了更优的视觉质量。

Conclusion: 算法创新可以克服此前3DGS SLAM方法的内存限制，实现大规模场景下的高质量重建。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have demonstrated impressive results for novel view synthesis with real-time rendering capabilities. However, integrating 3DGS with SLAM systems faces a fundamental scalability limitation: methods are constrained by GPU memory capacity, restricting reconstruction to small-scale environments. We present DiskChunGS, a scalable 3DGS SLAM system that overcomes this bottleneck through an out-of-core approach that partitions scenes into spatial chunks and maintains only active regions in GPU memory while storing inactive areas on disk. Our architecture integrates seamlessly with existing SLAM frameworks for pose estimation and loop closure, enabling globally consistent reconstruction at scale. We validate DiskChunGS on indoor scenes (Replica, TUM-RGBD), urban driving scenarios (KITTI), and resource-constrained Nvidia Jetson platforms. Our method uniquely completes all 11 KITTI sequences without memory failures while achieving superior visual quality, demonstrating that algorithmic innovation can overcome the memory constraints that have limited previous 3DGS SLAM methods.

</details>


### [179] [ReactEMG Stroke: Healthy-to-Stroke Few-shot Adaptation for sEMG-Based Intent Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22090)
*Runsheng Wang,Katelyn Lee,Xinyue Zhu,Lauren Winterbottom,Dawn M. Nilsen,Joel Stein,Matei Ciocarlie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种健康人到脑卒中患者的sEMG意图检测迁移学习管道，利用大规模健康数据预训练模型，仅需少量卒中患者数据微调（头部微调、LoRA、全微调），在3名慢性卒中患者数据上验证，相比零样本迁移和仅用卒中数据训练，平均过渡准确率从0.42提升至0.61，原始准确率从0.69提升至0.78，显著降低校准负担并提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面肌电信号（sEMG）可作为脑卒中后手部康复的按需辅助控制信号，但从瘫痪肌肉中检测患者意图通常需要冗长的、针对受试者的校准，且对变异性脆弱不堪。现有方法缺乏泛化能力，难以应对个体差异和实际应用中的分布偏移。

Method: 提出健康人到卒中患者的迁移学习管道：首先在大规模健康人sEMG数据上预训练意图检测模型，然后使用少量卒中患者数据对模型进行微调。对比了三种微调策略：头部微调（仅调整分类头）、参数高效LoRA适配器、以及端到端全微调。在3名慢性卒中患者的新数据集上评估，测试集包含会话内漂移、姿势变化和臂带重定位等现实分布偏移。

Result: 健康预训练+微调一致优于零样本迁移和仅用卒中数据训练（相同数据预算下）。最佳方法将平均过渡准确率从0.42提升至0.61，原始准确率从0.69提升至0.78。

Conclusion: 迁移可复用的健康域sEMG表征能减少校准负担，同时提升实时卒中后意图检测的鲁棒性，为临床实用化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Surface electromyography (sEMG) is a promising control signal for assist-as-needed hand rehabilitation after stroke, but detecting intent from paretic muscles often requires lengthy, subject-specific calibration and remains brittle to variability. We propose a healthy-to-stroke adaptation pipeline that initializes an intent detector from a model pretrained on large-scale able-bodied sEMG, then fine-tunes it for each stroke participant using only a small amount of subject-specific data. Using a newly collected dataset from three individuals with chronic stroke, we compare adaptation strategies (head-only tuning, parameter-efficient LoRA adapters, and full end-to-end fine-tuning) and evaluate on held-out test sets that include realistic distribution shifts such as within-session drift, posture changes, and armband repositioning. Across conditions, healthy-pretrained adaptation consistently improves stroke intent detection relative to both zero-shot transfer and stroke-only training under the same data budget; the best adaptation methods improve average transition accuracy from 0.42 to 0.61 and raw accuracy from 0.69 to 0.78. These results suggest that transferring a reusable healthy-domain EMG representation can reduce calibration burden while improving robustness for real-time post-stroke intent detection. Our project website, video, code, and dataset are available at: https://roamlab.github.io/reactemg-stroke/.

</details>


### [180] [Extending the Law of Intersegmental Coordination: Implications for Powered Prosthetic Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02181)
*Elad Siman Tov,Nili E. Krausz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分析下肢截肢者步态中节段间协调性（ISC）的方法，并将其扩展到力矩协调的新定律（Elevation Space Moments, ESM）。研究发现截肢者使用假肢时力矩协调性受损，并基于健康ISC约束开发了动力假肢控制框架，同时发布了ISC3d工具箱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力假肢虽能提供正功，但降低截肢者行走代谢成本仍是未解难题。节段间协调性（ISC）已被观察与步行能耗相关，但极少在截肢者步态中分析或应用。作者旨在填补这一空白，并探索将ISC应用于假肢控制的可能性。

Method: 1) 开发了分析下肢3D运动学ISC的方法；2) 将ISC扩展到力矩协调新定律（Elevation Space Moments, ESM）；3) 分析健全步态和截肢者（使用动力/被动假肢）的ISC与ESM；4) 提出以健康ISC为约束的动力假肢控制框架，预测小腿角度/力矩以补偿被动足部带来的改变；5) 开发了ISC3d工具箱。

Result: 健全步态中抬升角和力矩均呈现平面协调性；截肢者使用假肢时抬升角仍保持平面性，但ESM缺乏平面协调性。

Conclusion: ISC驱动的控制框架可补偿被动假肢带来的步态改变；ISC3d工具箱为研究步态协调性及神经控制提供了工具支持。

Abstract: Powered prostheses are capable of providing net positive work to amputees and have advanced in the past two decades. However, reducing amputee metabolic cost of walking remains an open problem. The Law of Intersegmental Coordination (ISC) has been observed across gaits and previously implicated in energy expenditure of walking, yet it has rarely been analyzed or applied within the context of lower-limb amputee gait. This law states that the elevation angles of the thigh, shank and foot over the gait cycle covary. In this work, we developed a method to analyze intersegmental coordination for lower-limb 3D kinematic data, to simplify ISC analysis. Moreover, inspired by motor control, biomechanics and robotics literature, we used our method to extend ISC to a new law of coordination of moments. We find these Elevation Space Moments (ESM), and present results showing a moment-based coordination for able bodied gait. We also analyzed ISC for amputee gait with powered and passive prostheses, and found that while elevation angles remained planar, the ESM lacked planar coordination. We present an ISC-driven powered prosthetic control framework, using healthy coordination as a constraint to predict the shank angles/moments to compensate for alterations due to a passive foot. We developed the ISC3d toolbox that is freely available online, which may be used to compute kinematic and kinetic ISC in 3D. This provides a means to further study the role of coordination in gait and may help address fundamental questions of the neural control of human movement.

</details>


### [181] [SCALE: Self-uncertainty Conditioned Adaptive Looking and Execution for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.04208)
*Hyeonbeom Choi,Daechul Ahn,Youhan Lee,Taewook Kang,Seongwon Cho,Jonghyun Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCALE是一种无需额外训练、无需验证器、仅需单次前向传播的推理策略，通过基于"自不确定性"联合调节视觉感知和动作，在不确定性高时扩大探索、低时聚焦利用，从而提升VLA模型的鲁棒性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型的测试时扩展方法需要额外训练、验证器和多次前向传播，部署成本高；且仅在动作解码层干预，忽略了视觉感知不确定性带来的问题。

Method: 提出SCALE，基于Active Inference理论中的不确定性驱动探索，通过自不确定性联合调节视觉感知和动作输出，不确定性高时扩大感知与动作的探索空间，低时聚焦利用。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界基准测试中，SCALE提升了最先进VLA模型的性能，并优于现有TTS方法，同时保持单次前向传播的高效性。

Conclusion: SCALE通过简单的单次前向传播推理策略，有效解决了VLA模型在感知模糊场景下的鲁棒性问题，无需额外训练开销，具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising paradigm for general-purpose robotic control, with test-time scaling (TTS) gaining attention to enhance robustness beyond training. However, existing TTS methods for VLAs require additional training, verifiers, and multiple forward passes, making them impractical for deployment. Moreover, they intervene only at action decoding while keeping visual representations fixed-insufficient under perceptual ambiguity, where reconsidering how to perceive is as important as deciding what to do. To address these limitations, we propose SCALE, a simple inference strategy that jointly modulates visual perception and action based on 'self-uncertainty', inspired by uncertainty-driven exploration in Active Inference theory-requiring no additional training, no verifier, and only a single forward pass. SCALE broadens exploration in both perception and action under high uncertainty, while focusing on exploitation when confident-enabling adaptive execution across varying conditions. Experiments on simulated and real-world benchmarks demonstrate that SCALE improves state-of-the-art VLAs and outperforms existing TTS methods while maintaining single-pass efficiency.

</details>


### [182] [Adaptive-Horizon Conflict-Based Search for Closed-Loop Multi-Agent Path Finding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12024)
*Jiarui Li,Federico Pecora,Runyu Zhang,Gioele Zardini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ACCBS，一种基于有限视野CBS和迭代深化MPC思想的闭环算法，通过动态调整规划视野和复用单一约束树，在有限计算预算下快速生成高质量可行解，并具有渐近最优性和任意时刻行为，在保证鲁棒性的同时缩小了理论最优性与实际部署之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAPF方法要么是开环规划器（生成固定轨迹，难以应对干扰），要么是闭环启发式方法（缺乏可靠性能保证），限制了安全关键场景下的应用。需要一种既能处理动态扰动又能提供性能保证的闭环算法。

Method: 基于有限视野CBS（Conflict-Based Search）的闭环算法，引入类似MPC中迭代深化的视野改变机制，动态根据计算预算调整规划视野，并复用单一约束树实现视野间的无缝切换。

Result: 在有限计算预算下快速生成高质量可行解；具有渐近最优性（随预算增加）；表现出任意时刻行为（anytime behavior）；对扰动具有灵活性；同时具备强性能保证。

Conclusion: ACCBS有效弥合了理论最优性与实际鲁棒性之间的鸿沟，为大规棋机器人部署提供了一种兼具灵活性和性能保证的解决方案。

Abstract: MAPF is a core coordination problem for large robot fleets in automated warehouses and logistics. Existing approaches are typically either open-loop planners, which generate fixed trajectories and struggle to handle disturbances, or closed-loop heuristics without reliable performance guarantees, limiting their use in safety-critical deployments. This paper presents ACCBS, a closed-loop algorithm built on a finite-horizon variant of CBS with a horizon-changing mechanism inspired by iterative deepening in MPC. ACCBS dynamically adjusts the planning horizon based on the available computational budget, and reuses a single constraint tree to enable seamless transitions between horizons. As a result, it produces high-quality feasible solutions quickly while being asymptotically optimal as the budget increases, exhibiting anytime behavior. Extensive case studies demonstrate that ACCBS combines flexibility to disturbances with strong performance guarantees, effectively bridging the gap between theoretical optimality and practical robustness for large-scale robot deployment.

</details>


### [183] [Lyapunov-Based PI-Like Control for Robust Trajectory Tracking of a Four-Wheel Independently Driven and Steered Robot: Design and Experimental Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15424)
*Branimir Ćaran,Vladimir Milić,Marko Švaco,Bojan Jerbić*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于李雅普诺夫方法的类PI控制器，用于四轮独立驱动转向移动机器人的鲁棒轨迹跟踪，通过结合模型前馈补偿与积分误差动力学的实用稳定性分析，实验验证了其在水平和垂直工况下优于传统PI和滑模控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对四轮独立驱动转向移动机器人的鲁棒轨迹跟踪问题，现有方法缺乏兼具结构简单、实时性好和严格稳定性保证的控制器设计，需要一种可在嵌入式平台实现且对未建模动态具有鲁棒性的解决方案。

Method: 基于显式结构验证的数学模型，采用扩展李雅普诺夫实用稳定性分析方法处理速度误差与积分误差的耦合动力学，推导出实用稳定性和一致终极有界性的显式界与充分条件；控制律保持类PI结构并附加模型前馈补偿。

Result: 在四轮独立驱动转向移动机器人平台上进行实验，包括水平和垂直两种运行工况，与标准PI控制器和滑模控制器进行对比，验证了所提方法的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的类PI控制器在保持简单结构适用于嵌入式实现的同时，通过李雅普诺夫分析保证了严格的稳定性，并在实验中展现出优于传统PI和滑模控制器的鲁棒轨迹跟踪性能。

Abstract: In this paper, a Lyapunov-based synthesis of a PI-like controller is proposed for robust trajectory tracking of an independently driven and steered four-wheel mobile robot. For the robot considered in this work, an explicit structurally verified mathematical model is used to enable systematic controller design with rigorous stability guarantees suitable for real time implementation. An augmented Lyapunov-based practical stability analysis is developed for the combined velocity-error and integral-error dynamics of the inner loop, yielding explicit bounds and sufficient conditions for practical stability and uniform ultimate boundedness of the combined velocity-error and integral-error state. The resulting control law retains a PI-like structure with model-based feedforward compensation, making it suitable for implementation on standard embedded platforms while improving robustness against configuration dependent residual dynamics and unmodelled effects. The effectiveness and robustness of the proposed design are demonstrated experimentally on a four-wheel independently steered and independently driven mobile robot platform, under both horizontal and vertical operating conditions and benchmarked against a PI controller and a sliding-mode controller.

</details>


### [184] [EgoMoD: Predicting Global Maps of Dynamics from Local Egocentric Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00167)
*Iacopo Catalano,David Morilla-Cabello,Jorge Pena-Queralta,Eduardo Montijano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EgoMoD，首个从机器人短时自我中心视频片段学习预测全局运动动态图（MoDs）的方法，无需外部全局感知基础设施，实现零样本迁移到真实系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MoDs构建需要长时间全局环境观测，依赖外部感知基础设施，且无法在机器人局部视野内预测全局运动趋势。本文旨在利用机器人运行时采集的短时自我中心视频，直接预测未来全局运动动态，实现无需外部设施的预判式导航。

Method: 提出视频与位姿条件化的架构，以外部观测（鸟瞰图）计算的MoDs作为特权监督信号，学习从局部动态线索推断环境范围的运动倾向。模型通过短时自我中心视频片段预测未来全环境的MoDs，替代传统方法在推理时所需的外部全局感知基础设施。

Result: 在大规模仿真环境中，EgoMoD能在有限观测条件下准确预测未来MoDs；在真实图像上评估展示了零样本迁移能力，可直接应用于真实系统。

Conclusion: EgoMoD证明了仅靠机器人日常运行时采集的自我中心视频即可学习全局运动动态先验，为动态环境中的预判式导航提供了无需外部基础设施的实用方案，且具备从仿真到真实的零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Efficient navigation in dynamic environments requires anticipating how motion patterns evolve beyond the robot's immediate perceptual range, enabling preemptive rather than purely reactive planning in crowded scenes. Maps of Dynamics (MoDs) offer a structured representation of motion tendencies in space useful for long-term global planning, but constructing them traditionally requires global environment observations over extended periods of time. We introduce EgoMoD, the first approach that learns to predict future MoDs directly from short egocentric video clips collected during robot operation. Our method learns to infer environment-wide motion tendencies from local dynamic cues using a video- and pose-conditioned architecture trained with MoDs computed from external observations as privileged supervision, allowing local observations to serve as predictive signals of global motion structure. Thanks to this, we offer the capacity to forecast future motion dynamics over the whole environment rather than merely extend past patterns in the robot's field of view. As a site-specific dynamic prior, EgoMoD replaces the external global sensing infrastructure required by prior MoD methods at inference time with standard onboard sensors. Experiments in large simulated environments show that EgoMoD predicts future MoDs under limited observability, while evaluation with real images showcases its zero-shot transferability to real systems.

</details>


### [185] [PROBE: Probabilistic Occupancy BEV Encoding with Analytical Translation Robustness for 3D Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05965)
*Jinseop Lee,Byoungho Lee,Gichul Yoo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PROBE提出了一种无学习、基于概率占据BEV编码的LiDAR地点识别描述子，将每个BEV单元的占据建模为伯努利随机变量，通过极坐标雅可比矩阵解析地边缘化连续平移不确定性，在O(R·S)时间内实现距离自适应角不确定性σ_θ=σ_t/r。跨四类LiDAR数据集评估，在手工描述子中多会话评估准确率最高，单会话性能与有监督方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LiDAR地点识别方法通常依赖离散点云扰动或学习范式，前者对连续平移不确定性的建模不够精确，后者跨传感器泛化能力有限且需要大量调参。PROBE旨在设计一种无学习、解析建模平移不确定性、跨传感器泛化强的描述子。

Method: 将BEV网格中每个单元的占据概率建模为伯努利随机变量；通过极坐标雅可比矩阵解析边缘化连续笛卡尔平移，推导出距离自适应的角不确定性σ_θ=σ_t/r；相似度计算结合Bernoulli-KL Jaccard距离、指数不确定性门控和基于FFT的高度余弦相似度进行旋转对齐。

Result: 在四种LiDAR类型对应的四个数据集上评估，PROBE在手工描述子中多会话评估达到最高精度，单会话评估与有监督基线方法性能相当。

Conclusion: PROBE作为一种无学习的概率占据描述子，通过解析平移不确定性建模和物理可解释的参数σ_t，实现了跨传感器泛化能力和有竞争力的地点识别性能，减少了数据集调参需求。

Abstract: We present PROBE (PRobabilistic Occupancy BEV Encoding), a learning-free LiDAR place recognition descriptor that models each BEV cell's occupancy as a Bernoulli random variable. Rather than relying on discrete point-cloud perturbations, PROBE analytically marginalizes over continuous Cartesian translations via the polar Jacobian, yielding a distance-adaptive angular uncertainty $σ_θ= σ_t / r$ in $\mathcal{O}(R{\cdot}S)$ time. The primary parameter $σ_t$ represents the expected translational uncertainty in meters, a sensor-independent physical quantity that enhances cross-sensor generalization while reducing the need for extensive per-dataset tuning. Pairwise similarity combines a Bernoulli-KL Jaccard with exponential uncertainty gating and FFT-based height cosine similarity for rotation alignment. Evaluated on four datasets spanning four diverse LiDAR types, PROBE achieves the highest accuracy among handcrafted descriptors in multi-session evaluation and competitive single-session performance relative to both handcrafted and supervised baselines. The source code and supplementary materials are available at https://sites.google.com/view/probe-pr.

</details>


### [186] [Safety Case Patterns for VLA-based driving systems: Insights from SimLingo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16013)
*Gerhard Yu,Fuyuki Ishikawa,Oluwafemi Odu,Alvine Boaye Belle*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对VLA（视觉-语言-动作）自动驾驶系统，提出了一套名为RAISE的安全案例设计方法，包括专用模式、扩展的HARA分析以及安全案例构建技术，并通过SimLingo案例验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA自动驾驶系统融合了视觉理解、语言解释和动作生成，但开放式的自然语言输入（如用户或导航指令）可能引入不可预测的危险行为，威胁乘客和行人安全。现有安全方法难以覆盖这类新风险，亟需针对性的安全保证方案以建立公众信任。

Method: 提出RAISE方法，包含三个核心部分：（1）针对指令驱动驾驶系统（尤其是VLA系统）的新型安全案例模式；（2）扩展的HARA（危害分析与风险评估），细化安全场景及其结果；（3）用于构建VLA驾驶系统安全案例的设计技术。

Result: 通过SimLingo案例研究，展示了RAISE方法能够为VLA自动驾驶系统构建严谨的、基于证据的安全声明。

Conclusion: RAISE方法为新兴VLA自动驾驶系统的安全性保证提供了系统化的设计框架，有助于推动此类系统的可信部署。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA)-based driving systems represent a significant paradigm shift in autonomous driving since, by combining traffic scene understanding, linguistic interpretation, and action generation, these systems enable more flexible, adaptive, and instruction-responsive driving behaviors. However, despite their growing adoption and potential to support socially responsible autonomous driving as well as understanding high-level human instructions, VLA-based driving systems may exhibit new types of hazardous behaviors. For instance, the integration of open-ended natural language inputs (e.g., user or navigation instructions) into the multimodal control loop may lead to unpredictable and unsafe behaviors that could endanger vehicle occupants and pedestrians. Hence, assuring the safety of these systems is crucial to help build trust in their operations. To support this, we propose a novel safety case design approach called RAISE. Our approach introduces novel patterns tailored to instruction-based driving systems such as VLA-based driving systems, an extension of Hazard Analysis and Risk Assessment (HARA) detailing safe scenarios and their outcomes, and a design technique to create the safety cases of VLA-based driving systems. A case study on SimLingo illustrates how our approach can be used to construct rigorous, evidence-based safety claims for this emerging class of autonomous driving systems.

</details>


### [187] [AssemLM: A Spatial Reasoning Multimodal Large Language Model for Robotic Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08983)
*Zhi Jing,Jinbin Qiao,Ouyang Lu,Jicong Ao,Shuang Qiu,Huazhe Xu,Yu-Gang Jiang,Chenjia Bai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AssemLM，一种集成装配手册、点云和文本指令的空间多模态大语言模型，用于机器人装配中的6D位姿推理；同时构建了包含90万+样本的大规模基准AssemBench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于VLM的方法依赖粗粒度2D感知，难以对复杂3D几何进行精确推理，无法满足机器人装配等精细操作任务的需求。

Method: 设计专门的点云编码器提取细粒度几何和旋转特征，将原始3D感知与高层语言推理桥接；融合装配手册、点云和文本指令等多模态输入，预测6D装配位姿。

Result: 在AssemBench基准和真实机器人评估中，AssemLM在6D位姿推理任务上达到SOTA性能，有效支持真实世界中的精细多步装配任务。

Conclusion: 将3D几何理解显式融入多模态大语言模型是实现机器人装配中精确空间推理的有效途径，AssemBench为相关研究提供了全面的评估平台。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning is a fundamental capability for embodied intelligence, especially for fine-grained manipulation tasks such as robotic assembly. Recent methods based on vision-language models (VLMs) largely rely on coarse 2D perception and struggle to perform accurate reasoning over complex 3D geometry. To address this limitation, we propose AssemLM, a spatial multimodal large language model for robotic assembly that integrates assembly manuals, point clouds, and textual instructions to predict task-critical 6D assembly poses with explicit geometric understanding. To bridge raw 3D perception and high-level linguistic reasoning, AssemLM employs a specialized point cloud encoder to extract fine-grained geometric and rotational features for accurate 3D spatial reasoning in assembly tasks. In addition, we introduce AssemBench, a large-scale benchmark for assembly-oriented spatial reasoning with over 900K multimodal samples and precise 6D pose annotations, extending evaluation from 2D grounding to full 3D geometric inference. Extensive experiments and real-robot evaluations demonstrate that AssemLM achieves state-of-the-art 6D pose reasoning performance and effectively supports fine-grained, multi-step assembly tasks in real-world settings. Code, models, and the AssemBench dataset will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [188] [Lexicographic Minimum-Violation Motion Planning using Signal Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20428)
*Patrick Halder,Lothar Kiltz,Hannes Homburger,Johannes Reuter,Matthias Althoff*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将词典式STL最小违反运动规划转化为单目标标量优化问题的方法，通过非均匀量化和位移技术扩展了确定性MPPI求解器，并引入了结合空间与时间违反的新谓词鲁棒性度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶运动规划常需满足多个相互冲突的规范，当无法同时满足所有规范时，需要最小违反运动规划。但规范的完全排序会导致词典式优化问题，使用标准方法求解计算成本高昂。

Method: 将多目标词典式优化转化为单目标标量优化问题，采用非均匀量化与位移技术；扩展确定性MPPI求解器以高效求解无二次输入成本的优化问题；提出一种结合空间和时间违反的新型谓词鲁棒性度量。

Result: 所提方法在单目标求解器框架下提供了可解释且可扩展的解决方案，实现了词典式STL最小违反运动规划。

Conclusion: 通过将词典式优化转化为单目标优化并扩展MPPI求解器，有效解决了计算效率问题，同时新定义的谓词鲁棒性度量能更全面地评估规范违反情况。

Abstract: Motion planning for autonomous vehicles often requires satisfying multiple conditionally conflicting specifications. In situations where not all specifications can be met simultaneously, minimum-violation motion planning maintains system operation by minimizing violations of specifications in accordance with their priorities. Signal temporal logic (STL) provides a formal language for rigorously defining these specifications and enables the quantitative evaluation of their violations. However, a total ordering of specifications yields a lexicographic optimization problem, which is typically computationally expensive to solve using standard methods. We address this problem by transforming the multi-objective lexicographic optimization problem into a single-objective scalar optimization problem using non-uniform quantization and bit-shifting. Specifically, we extend a deterministic model predictive path integral (MPPI) solver to efficiently solve optimization problems without quadratic input cost. Additionally, a novel predicate-robustness measure that combines spatial and temporal violations is introduced. Our results show that the proposed method offers an interpretable and scalable solution for lexicographic STL minimum-violation motion planning within a single-objective solver framework.

</details>


### [189] [RGB-S: Image-Aligned Tactile Saliency for Robust Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08765)
*Shengcheng Luo,Kefei Wu,Xiaoying Zhou,Wanlin Li,Ziyuan Jiao,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种显式将触觉信息（力调制高斯显著性图）投影到RGB图像平面的框架，通过零初始化条件注入架构将物理接触先验嵌入视觉骨干网络，在严重遮挡条件下显著提升灵巧操作成功率（真实世界实验提升26.7个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐式跨模态对应学习方法在视觉观测退化（如遮挡）时数据效率低、泛化性差，缺乏利用几何先验显式对齐稀疏异构触觉与稠密视觉表征的能力。

Method: 利用正向运动学和相机标定将触觉传感器位置投影到RGB图像平面；渲染力调制高斯显著性图以建模空间不确定性；通过零初始化条件注入架构将2D空间锚点嵌入标准视觉骨干网络，保持预训练视觉表征的同时注入物理接触先验。

Result: 在6个灵巧操作任务（仿真+真实）的严重视觉遮挡条件下，真实世界实验显示显式RGB-触觉对齐方法比最强隐式基线提升26.7个百分点的操作成功率。

Conclusion: 显式在图像域进行RGB-触觉对齐能够提升空间推理能力和对遮挡的鲁棒性，验证了几何先验在跨模态融合中的有效性。

Abstract: Effective visuo-tactile integration is critical for robotic dexterous manipulation, especially when visual observations are unreliable or occluded. However, robustly aligning sparse, heterogeneous tactile measurements with dense visual representations remains a fundamental challenge. Most existing approaches require policies to learn cross-modal correspondences implicitly from limited demonstrations, without leveraging geometric priors. As a result, they are often data-inefficient and generalize poorly when visual observations are degraded. To address this limitation, we propose a framework that explicitly grounds physical contacts in the image domain. Using robot forward kinematics and camera calibration, we project tactile sensor locations directly onto the RGB image plane. We then render force-modulated Gaussian saliency maps to model spatial uncertainty arising from kinematic and calibration errors. By integrating these 2D spatial anchors through a zero-initialized conditioning architecture, our method injects physical contact priors into standard visual backbones while preserving pre-trained visual representations. We evaluate our method on six dexterous manipulation tasks in both simulation and the real world under severe visual occlusions. Real-world experiments show that explicit RGB-S grounding in the image domain improves real-world occluded manipulation success rates by $26.7$ percentage points over the strongest implicit visuo-tactile baseline, suggesting its improved spatial reasoning and robustness to occlusion. Project page: touch-as-saliency.github.io

</details>


### [190] [UniDexTok: A Unified Dexterous Hand Tokenizer from Real Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10683)
*Dong Fang,Youjun Wu,Yuanxin Zhong,Rui Zhang,Yunlong Wang,Xiaosong Jia,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出统一灵巧手模型(UDHM)和基于该模型的状态分词器UniDexTok，将人类和机器人的手部状态映射到22自由度的共享语义接口，实现了跨异构灵巧手的统一表征，无需重定向或仿真数据。相比基线UniHM，误差降低98%以上，达到亚毫米级精度，并展现出零样本和少样本的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手硬件设计差异大（运动学、关节定义、自由度不同），导致难以定义共享状态表征，灵巧手数据碎片化，难以用于联合训练。

Method: 提出UDHM将人/机器手映射到22-DoF语义接口；基于此提出UniDexTok，一种免重定向的状态分词器，从真实关节状态中学习受具体本体条件约束的离散token，实现异构灵巧手的统一表征。

Result: 与UniHM相比，MPJAE从15.63°降至0.16°（降98.98%），MPJPE从18.51mm降至0.18mm（降99.03%），从厘米级提升到亚毫米级精度；跨本体的数据能提升目标本体重建精度；具备零样本和少样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: UniDexTok实现了高精度的跨灵巧手统一表征，证明了跨本体tokenization的有效性，为灵巧手数据的联合训练和泛化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Dexterous hands are essential for fine-grained manipulation, but their hardware designs vary substantially across embodiments. Differences in kinematics, joint definitions, and degrees of freedom make it difficult to define a shared state representation compared with parallel grippers. As a result, dexterous-hand data remains fragmented and difficult to use for joint training. In this work, we propose the Unified Dexterous Hand Model (UDHM), which maps human and robot hand states into a shared 22-DoF semantic interface. Based on UDHM, we introduce UniDexTok, a retargeting-free state tokenizer that learns embodiment-conditioned discrete tokens from standardized real joint states. UniDexTok provides a unified representation for heterogeneous dexterous hands without relying on retargeting or simulation data. Compared with the recent baseline UniHM, UniDexTok reduces MPJAE from 15.63 degrees to 0.16 degrees and MPJPE from 18.51 mm to 0.18 mm, corresponding to error reductions of 98.98% and 99.03%, respectively. These results improve reconstruction from centimeter-scale to sub-millimeter accuracy. Experiments further show that data from other embodiments improves target-embodiment reconstruction accuracy, demonstrating the benefit of cross-embodiment tokenization. UniDexTok also shows strong zero-shot and few-shot reconstruction ability when new dexterous hands are introduced.

</details>


### [191] [RoboNaldo: Accurate, Stable and Powerful Humanoid Soccer Shooting via Motion-Guided Curriculum Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11092)
*Yichao Zhong,Yidan Lu,Yuhang Lu,Tianyang Tang,Haoguang Mai,Yixuan Pan,Tianyu Li,Li Chen,Jingbo Wang,Zhongyu Li,Peng Lu,Hongyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RoboNaldo，一个三阶段运动引导课程强化学习框架，用于类人足球射门。通过单一人体踢球参考动作逐步过渡到任务奖励优化，在仿真和真实Unitree G1机器人上实现了更低射门误差、更高球速的类人足球射门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法中，运动跟踪驱动的强化学习虽能提供全身稳定性，但固定参考难以适应变化的球位和击球时机；而任务奖励驱动的强化学习从零探索有效踢球动作困难。因此需要一种既能利用运动先验又能灵活适应任务目标的框架。

Method: 提出三阶段课程强化学习框架：第一阶段学习稳定的全身踢球先验（基于单一人体踢球参考）；第二阶段将踢球动作适应到固定球自由踢场景（球随机位置）；第三阶段扩展到移动球射门，通过运动指令与踢球触发接口实现。训练时高层启发式规划器控制接口，推理时可由任意高层控制器驱动底层策略。

Result: 仿真中自由踢射门误差降低48.6%，射门速度提升2.96倍；真实场景中Unitree G1在3米距离自由踢和移动球平均射门误差分别为0.73m和0.86m，触球后球速达13.10m/s（约为职业球员射门速度的59-71%）。

Conclusion: RoboNaldo通过运动引导的课程学习有效结合了运动先验的稳定性和任务优化的灵活性，实现了高冲击力、高精度的类人足球射门，且策略具备良好的泛化性和迁移能力。

Abstract: Elite humanoid soccer shooting requires whole-body stability, high-impulse whole-body interactions, and accuracy to targets. Motion tracking-driven reinforcement learning (RL) provides stability in whole-body movement coordination, but a fixed reference makes it hard to adapt to varied ball positions and strike timings; in contrast, task reward-driven RL struggles to explore and discover valid kicks from scratch. We therefore introduce RoboNaldo, a three-stage motion-guided curriculum RL framework for high-impulse humanoid interaction. A single human-kick reference is used as a scaffold and progressively shifts optimization towards shooting performance. The curriculum first learns a stable whole-body kicking prior, then adapts the kick to free-kick settings where the ball is stationary at random positions, and finally extends it to moving-ball shooting through a locomotion-command and kick-trigger interface. A high-level heuristic planner controls this interface during training, while alternative high-level controllers can drive the same low-level policy at inference. In simulation, RoboNaldo demonstrates free-kick shot error 48.6% lower and shoot velocity 2.96x than prior work baselines. In real world on a Unitree G1 with onboard perception, RoboNaldo attains 0.73 m and 0.86 m average target shooting error from 3 m away in free-kick and moving-ball cases, accordingly. And the post-contact ball velocity reaches 13.10 m/s, which is 59-71% of reported professional open-play shot speed. Project page: https://opendrivelab.com/RoboNaldo.

</details>


### [192] [Blind Dexterous Grasping via Real2Sim2Real Tactile Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11767)
*Shengcheng Luo,Xiyan Huang,Zhe Xu,Wanlin Li,Ziyuan Jiao,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于触觉的盲抓取框架，通过Real2Sim触觉标定管道、布局感知触觉编码器和触觉条件扩散策略，在真实多指机械手上实现了无需视觉输入的抓取操作，在20个物体上达到27%的真实世界抓取成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手的盲抓取是重要的操作能力，但触觉策略从仿真到真实机器人迁移存在触觉仿真-真实差距（sim-to-real gap）以及稀疏触觉信号表达能力有限的问题。

Method: 提出包含三个关键组件的框架：(1) Real2Sim触觉标定管道，构建接触校准的数字孪生仿真器以复现真实触觉信号；(2) 布局感知触觉编码器，通过自监督预训练融入传感器几何先验增强稀疏触觉观测的表达能力；(3) 在标定仿真器中训练物体特定的强化学习专家，将其成功抓取轨迹聚合为触觉条件扩散策略。

Result: 在配备分布式触觉感知的LEAP Hand上，对10个已见和10个未见物体进行测试，部署的策略在全部20个物体上实现了27%的真实世界抓取成功率，无需真实世界抓取示范或视觉输入。仿真消融实验验证了布局感知触觉预训练的有效性，感知层评估确认Real2Sim标定增加了仿真与硬件间触觉接触事件的一致性。

Conclusion: 接触事件标定、几何感知的触觉表征学习以及基于扩散的策略聚合为真实灵巧机械手上的触觉盲抓取提供了一条有效路径。

Abstract: Blind grasping with a dexterous hand is a crucial manipulation capability. Nevertheless, learning such tactile-only policies for real robots remains challenging due to the tactile sim-to-real gap and the limited expressiveness of sparse tactile signals. To bridge this gap, we propose a framework for tactile-only blind grasping that is deployable on a physical multi-fingered robotic hand. Our approach combines three key components. First, we introduce a Real2Sim tactile calibration pipeline that constructs a contact-calibrated digital-twin simulator capable of reproducing real tactile signals. Second, we improve the expressiveness of sparse tactile observations using a layout-aware tactile encoder, which incorporates sensor-geometry priors through self-supervised pretraining. Third, to improve generalization to unseen objects, we train object-specific reinforcement-learning experts in the calibrated simulator and aggregate their successful grasp trajectories into a tactile-conditioned Diffusion Policy. We evaluate our method on a physical LEAP Hand equipped with distributed tactile sensing across 10 seen and 10 unseen objects. The deployed policy achieves a 27\% real-world grasp success rate across all 20 objects, without real-world grasping demonstrations or visual input. Simulation ablations show that layout-aware tactile pretraining improves grasping performance, while sensing-level evaluations confirm that Real2Sim calibration increases the consistency of tactile contact events between simulation and hardware. Together, these results suggest that contact-event calibration, geometry-aware tactile representation learning, and diffusion-based policy aggregation provide an effective path toward tactile-only blind grasping on real dexterous robotic hands. Project page:Dex-Blind-Grasp.github.io.

</details>


### [193] [DrivingAgent: Design and Scheduling Agents for Autonomous Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12236)
*Zhongyu Xia,Wenhao Chen,Yongtao Wang,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DrivingAgent框架，利用LLM自动化自动驾驶系统设计与实时调度，在nuScenes和Bench2Drive上实现更优的速度-精度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶系统集成基础模型面临两大挑战：人工设计集成新模型过程繁琐，以及缺乏满足严格实时约束的智能动态调度机制。现有基于LLM的代理框架不适用于自动驾驶，无法区分系统设计与实时调度的不同需求，将模块视为黑盒，且不支持持续运行。

Method: DrivingAgent包含两个阶段：设计阶段通过解释系统架构、生成代码和超网络训练验证实现模块开发自动化；调度阶段使用经强化学习训练的轻量级LLM动态编排系统模块，并配备结合长期存储与带时间戳短期上下文的结构化记忆。

Result: 在nuScenes和Bench2Drive基准测试上，DrivingAgent取得了更优的速度-精度权衡。

Conclusion: DrivingAgent能够有效解决自动驾驶系统设计与实时调度两大挑战，实现自动化、智能化的模块管理与动态调度，提升系统性能。

Abstract: Many autonomous driving systems are increasingly incorporating foundation models to improve generalization and handle long-tail scenarios. However, this trend introduces two key challenges: (i) the manual and labor-intensive process of designing and integrating new models, and (ii) the lack of intelligent, dynamic scheduling mechanisms to meet strict real-time constraints. While Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents offer a promising avenue for automation, existing frameworks are ill-suited for autonomous driving. Specifically, they fail to distinguish between the fundamentally different requirements of system design and real-time scheduling, treat modules as opaque black boxes, and are not designed for continuous operation. To address these limitations, we propose DrivingAgent, a novel agent framework tailored to the dual challenges of autonomous driving system design and scheduling. In the design phase, DrivingAgent automates module development by interpreting system architecture, generating code, and validating modules via super-network training. In the scheduling phase, it employs a lightweight LLM trained with reinforcement learning to dynamically orchestrate system modules in real time, supported by a structured memory that integrates long-term storage with timestamped short-term context. Experimental results demonstrate that DrivingAgent achieves a superior speed--accuracy trade-off on both the nuScenes and Bench2Drive benchmarks.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [194] [AI SciBrief as a Gateway to Research: A Framework for Onboarding Students into New Research Areas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12413)
*Andrei Lazarev,Dmitrii Sedov*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于LLM的AI SciBrief教学框架，通过自动生成科学趋势摘要，帮助各阶段学生克服信息过载这一研究入门障碍，快速从信息搜索过渡到知识创造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高等教育各阶段学生普遍面临信息过载问题，这阻碍了研究过程的启动阶段并抑制了学习动机，亟需有效的解决方案来降低这一"入门障碍"。

Method: 引入AI SciBrief平台（基于大语言模型），自动生成覆盖金融、医学、教育等多学科的科学趋势摘要；提出将该工具融入课程的具体教学法框架，包括辅助学期论文选题、加速学位论文文献综述、支持研究生持续追踪新兴趋势。

Result: AI SciBrief作为"研究网关"能够有效降低学生的认知负荷，使学生能够更快速地从信息搜索阶段过渡到知识创造阶段。

Conclusion: AI SciBrief教学框架能够有效克服信息过载导致的研究入门障碍，赋能学生更高效地开展学术研究。

Abstract: Students at all levels of higher education face a significant barrier in the form of information overload, which often paralyzes the initial stages of the research process and suppresses motivation. In response, this article introduces a pedagogical framework that leverages AI SciBrief, a platform powered by a Large Language Model (LLM) designed to automatically generate digests of scientific trends. We describe how this multidisciplinary tool - with initial coverage in finance, medicine, and education - can be integrated into the curriculum to overcome this "entry barrier." The framework provides concrete methodologies for utilizing these digests to facilitate topic selection for term papers, accelerate literature reviews for dissertations, and enable postgraduate students to continuously monitor emerging trends. We conclude that AI SciBrief functions as a "gateway to research" effectively reducing students' cognitive load and empowering them to transition more rapidly from information searching to knowledge creation.

</details>


### [195] [The Khipu Problem: Institutional Legibility Under Distributed Cognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12414)
*Krti Tallam*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出分布式AI治理中的"基普问题"（khipu problem）：记录虽然存在，但解读记录所需的制度能力会随时间衰退。治理的核心挑战从"系统做了什么"转向"未来机构是否还能读懂系统是什么"，需要保护"解释连续性"而非仅仅保留痕迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI治理仍默认治理对象是有边界的模型或智能体，但实际系统已演变为模型、工具、人类、上下文存储、检索层、运行时策略、授权边界和委托机构角色共同构成的分布式认知系统。治理框架与系统现实之间出现结构性错配。

Method: 通过概念分析区分缺失证据、模糊证据和结构性不可读证据；将分布式系统的决策输出重新理解为"分布式认知片段"而非有界模型输出；提出"治理工作空间"和"承载收据的治理表面"作为保护解释连续性的治理基础设施。

Result: 揭示了分布式AI中记录保存与解释能力脱节的结构性治理失败机制：日志、痕迹、模型版本、工具调用、输出和审批工件可能完整保留，但将其解读为连贯认知片段的制度能力已经消失。

Conclusion: 分布式AI治理需要保护解释连续性而不仅仅是追溯保留，这一需求催生了新型治理基础设施设计——治理工作空间和承载收据的治理表面，用以维护行动身份、权威、边界真实性、证据范围和后果性结果的可读性。

Abstract: AI governance still tends to assume that the relevant object is a bounded model or a bounded agent. That assumption is getting weaker. Real systems increasingly distribute cognition across models, tools, humans, context stores, retrieval layers, runtime policies, authorization boundaries, and delegated institutional roles. In such systems, the central governance problem is no longer only what the system did, but whether later institutions can still read what the system was. This paper introduces the khipu problem for distributed AI: the record can survive while the reading practice needed to interpret it decays. Logs, traces, model versions, tool calls, outputs, and approval artifacts may remain available while the institutional capacity to read them as parts of one coherent cognitive episode disappears. We argue that this failure is better understood as loss of interpretive continuity than as ordinary lack of observability. The result is a distinct governance failure. Institutions must classify, trust, audit, and constrain systems whose relevant identity is distributed across components and whose legibility depends on surrounding interpretive scaffolding. The problem is not merely missing data. It is a structural mismatch between what can be represented and what must still be decided under consequential conditions. We therefore argue that governance for distributed AI requires preservation of interpretive continuity, not only trace retention. The paper distinguishes missing evidence, ambiguous evidence, and structurally unreadable evidence; argues that many consequential outcomes are better understood as distributed cognitive episodes than as bounded model outputs; and proposes governance workspaces together with receipt-bearing governance surfaces as interpretive infrastructure for preserving action identity, authority, boundary truth, evidential scope, and consequential outcomes.

</details>


### [196] [The AI Legal Specialist: A Juridically Autonomous Professional Profile for AI Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12415)
*Nicola Fabiano*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文指出，在全球AI监管快速扩张的背景下，现有的数据保护官、隐私律师、合规官等角色均无法充分覆盖新兴的AI法律专业空间。作者提出"AI法律专家"这一独立职业画像，从法理上论证其自主性，并基于欧洲电子能力框架（e-CF）构建了参考能力架构及关键绩效指标测量条件，旨在为该职业的国际标准化提供基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球多个司法管辖区（如欧盟AI法案、欧洲委员会框架公约、美国行政与部门框架、英国、加拿大、巴西、中国、日本、新加坡等）相继出台AI监管法规，产生了对专门AI法律专业知识的需求。然而市场以碎片化方式应对（如数据保护官扩展其职责范围、隐私律师转向AI、合规官在现有手册中增加AI章节），这些适应性调整均不足以覆盖新兴的全球AI监管格局所开辟的专业空间。

Method: 本文采用法理分析方法，首先从监管义务结构推导出该职业画像的法理自主性；其次，以欧洲电子能力框架（e-CF, EN 16234-1）为方法论选择，构建参考能力架构；最后，阐明通过关键绩效指标进行运营测量的条件。

Result: 提出"AI法律专家"（AI Legal Specialist）这一独立的职业画像，将其定义为接受过高级法律训练的专业人士，处于法律解释与AI治理的交汇点。该画像法理上自主存在，源自实质性AI监管所生成监管义务的结构，而非技术标准或相邻角色的延伸。同时提出了参考能力架构和绩效测量条件。

Conclusion: 该贡献旨在作为该职业国际标准化的基础，并作为跨司法管辖区的实践、课程设置和采纳的参考依据。

Abstract: The rapid global expansion of artificial intelligence regulation has generated, across multiple jurisdictions, a demand for legal expertise dedicated to AI that the market has addressed in a fragmented manner. Data protection officers extend their remit beyond data protection law; privacy lawyers reposition themselves toward AI; compliance officers add AI chapters to their existing manuals. This paper argues that none of these adaptive responses adequately covers the professional space opened by the emerging global AI regulatory landscape, of which the EU Artificial Intelligence Act (Regulation (EU) 2024/1689) is the most comprehensive instance, alongside the Council of Europe Framework Convention on AI, the United States executive and sectoral framework, and analogous initiatives in the United Kingdom, Canada, Brazil, China, Japan, Singapore, and beyond. A distinct professional profile is required: the AI Legal Specialist, conceived as a jurist -- understood broadly to encompass any professional with advanced legal training -- operating at the intersection of legal interpretation and AI governance. The profile is juridically autonomous: it derives its existence from the structure of regulatory obligations generated wherever AI is subject to substantive regulation, rather than from any technical standard or the extension of adjacent roles. The paper provides a juridically grounded definition of the profile, argues for its autonomy from adjacent figures and international standards, proposes a reference competence architecture aligned with the European e-Competence Framework (e-CF, EN 16234-1) as a methodological choice, and articulates the conditions for its operational measurement through key performance indicators. The contribution is intended as a foundation for international standardization of the profile and as a reference for practice, curricula, and adoption across jurisdictions.

</details>


### [197] [Who Designs the Designer? Behavioural Architecture for GenAI in Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12416)
*Sepinoud Azimi*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文指出当前AI教育领域存在两种失败路径（全面禁用AI和仅构建内容型辅导系统），并提出行为架构（Behavioural Architecture）作为替代方案：系统不仅适应学生学什么，更适应学生如何学习；学生可以共同撰写、阅读、修改和撤销系统记录；设计者角色从AI供应商转移到教育者、学生和系统三方共同承担。论文主张该架构需要欧盟层面的治理，并提出了五个实证问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI教育方案（禁用AI或仅做内容辅导）忽略了人格、动机、情绪状态等非认知因素对学习效果的重要影响，亟需一种能够全面适应学生学习方式的系统架构。

Method: 提出行为架构（Behavioural Architecture），核心特征包括：(1) 系统适应学生如何学习而非仅学什么；(2) 学生可共同撰写、阅读、修改和撤销系统记录；(3) 设计者角色从AI供应商分散到教育者、学生和系统三方。

Result: 论文识别出AI教育中设计者角色的空缺，并提出需要欧盟层面治理的结构性基础设施来支撑该角色配置。

Conclusion: 当前AI教育系统的设计者角色无人占据，要真正落实这一角色需要尚不存在的基础设施；论文提出五个实证问题以供后续检验该架构的有效性。

Abstract: AI in education is stuck between two failed responses: banning AI and building content-only tutors. Both fail because they ignore what decades of research has established: that personality, motivation, and emotional state shape learning outcomes as strongly as cognitive ability. This paper proposes behavioural architecture as an alternative. In the proposed architecture, the system adapts to how a student learns, not only to what they learn next. The student co-authors the record the system keeps, can read it, revise it, and revoke it. The designer role, what the system treats as true about the student, shifts from the AI vendor alone to a distribution among educator, student, and system. The paper argues that this architecture requires governance at EU level: the institution operating the system is the same one assessing the student, and individual institutions cannot provide the structural protections this configuration demands. Five empirical questions are proposed to test whether the architecture delivers on its claims. The contribution is naming a vacancy: the designer role in AI-in-education is currently unoccupied, and occupying it requires infrastructure that does not yet exist.

</details>


### [198] [Assessing Student Ability to Select an Algorithmic Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12417)
*Dip Kiran Pradhan Newar,Michael Shindler,Seth Poulsen*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个用于评估学生算法设计范式选择能力的多项选择测试（APSA），并通过Cronbach's α检验（0.73）验证了其内部一致性，为跨机构标准化评估提供了工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机科学学生需要能够根据问题选择合适的算法设计范式，但现有研究缺乏对学生如何选择范式的能力评估方法，传统依赖开放式问题或访谈的方式难以规模化，亟需一种高效的标准化测试工具。

Method: 构建了算法范式选择评估（APSA），采用多项选择题形式；通过Cronbach's α系数评估内部一致性，得到0.73（高于0.7阈值）。

Result: APSA的内部一致性达0.73，超过0.7的可接受阈值，初步数据证明其作为评估工具的有效性。

Conclusion: APSA可作为跨机构的标准化评估工具，帮助研究者评估教学干预对学生算法设计范式选择知识提升的效果。

Abstract: Computer science students are expected to be able to look at a problem and select an appropriate algorithm design paradigm to use to produce a solution. However, there is little research on how students determine which algorithmic paradigm to use. Historically, researchers have relied on free-response questions or interviews to assess students' knowledge of algorithmic paradigm selection. To successfully evaluate and scale teaching interventions for selecting an algorithmic design paradigm, we need to efficiently test a student's ability to select among different design paradigms. Here, we present the first attempts to assess student knowledge to select an algorithm design paradigm using multiple-choice questions. We present the construction of the \textit{algorithmic paradigm selection assessment} (APSA) and preliminary data demonstrating its effectiveness as an assessment. We discuss the key points we learned during this process to write multiple-choice questions for Algorithm Design Paradigms. We tested the internal consistency of our assessment using Cronbach's $α$ and obtained a score of $0.73$, which is above the required threshold of $0.7$. APSA can be used across institutions as a standardized way to assess students' ability to select different algorithm design paradigms. APSA will assist researchers in evaluating whether a theory helps students improve their knowledge of different Algorithm Design Paradigms.

</details>


### [199] [Divination by Prompt: LLM-Mediated Xuanxue on Chinese Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12418)
*Chuang Li,Lixuan Wang,Yuqi Chen,Ze Hong*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了中国社交媒体上基于大语言模型（LLM）的占卜实践（玄学），通过分析23000+小红书帖子及32个深度访谈，发现用户主要因趋势驱动的好奇心和事件驱动的焦虑使用LLM占卜，涉及恋爱、职业、考试和游戏抽卡等务实问题。用户通过协作提示词优化成为主动的提示工程师，对结果的有效性评价偏向积极，但存在巴纳姆效应和确认偏误。职业占卜师则认为LLM缺乏“灵性力量”。研究认为LLM占卜保留了传统占卜的核心功能，同时引入了可扩展性、可重复性和提示驱动的共创模式，重塑了占卜权威的构建与评估方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的快速普及催生了使用对话式AI进行占卜这一引人注目的文化实践，但学界对此缺乏系统性研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，首次系统研究中国社交媒体语境下的LLM占卜现象。

Method: 混合方法设计：分析小红书23000+条帖子和评论，并进行32个半结构化访谈（包括用户和职业占卜师）。

Result: 用户通过趋势驱动好奇心和事件驱动焦虑两条路径使用LLM占卜；协作提示词优化使用户成为主动提示工程师；用户感知有效性偏向积极但存在认知偏差；用户发展出重复试验和跨模型比较等验证实践；职业占卜师质疑LLM的灵性合法性。

Conclusion: LLM占卜保留了传统占卜的核心功能，同时引入了可扩展性、可重复性和提示驱动的共生产模式，重塑了占卜权威的构建与评估方式。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of large language models (LLMs) has produced a striking cultural practice: using conversational AI for divination. This paper offers one of the first systematic studies of LLM-mediated divination in the context of Xuanxue, an internet-native umbrella term for mystical and spiritual practices on Chinese social media. Using a mixed-methods design, we analyze 23000+ posts and comments from Xiaohongshu and conduct 32 semi-structured interviews with users and professional diviners. Users primarily consult LLMs about pragmatic concerns - romantic relationships, careers, exams, and in-game gacha draws - via two intersecting pathways: trend-driven curiosity enabled by viral visibility and zero-cost access, and event-driven anxiety under conditions of uncertainty. A defining feature is collaborative prompt refinement, which turns users into active prompt engineers. Among commenters expressing a clear stance, perceived efficacy skews positive, with "accuracy" often justified through biographical fit and retrospective confirmation, consistent with Barnum and confirmation bias. Users also develop verification practices such as repeated trials and cross-model comparison. Professional diviners, by contrast, portray LLMs as lacking the "spiritual power" required for genuine divination, reflecting both ontological commitments and economic boundary-work. We also show how participants navigate tensions between scientific and metaphysical frames when interpreting AI-generated readings. Situating these findings in anthropological and cognitive-evolutionary theories of divination, we argue that LLM divination preserves core functions of traditional practice while introducing scalability, repeatability, and prompt-driven co-production that reshape how divinatory authority is constructed and evaluated.

</details>


### [200] [GeoDial: A Multimodal Conversational Tutoring Dataset for Geometry Problem-Solving with Visual Tutor Turns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12419)
*Sankalan Pal Chowdhury,Junling Wang,Donya Rooein,April Yi Wang,Mrinmaya Sachan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: GeoDial是一个包含1300+几何教学对话的多模态数据集，教师对话明确基于图表高亮。现有模型在生成对话方面有提升，但在准确生成图表高亮方面存在困难，揭示了当前方法在整合视觉推理与教学交互方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育领域严重依赖图表和视觉线索，但现有辅导数据集大多局限于纯文本交互，限制了AI导师以人类教师使用的视觉接地方式进行教学的能力。

Method: 收集了1300+来自经验数学教师的师生对话，设计了结合对话行为、视觉高亮和反馈的可扩展标注协议，并在GeoDial上微调了多个视觉语言模型。

Result: 监督微调显著提升了对话生成质量，但在准确生成图表高亮方面表现不佳，揭示了当前方法的局限性。

Conclusion: 需要更有效地整合视觉推理与教学交互的方法来克服现有模型的不足。

Abstract: Several educational domains rely heavily on diagrams and visual cues, yet most existing tutoring datasets are limited to text-only interactions. This limits the development of AI tutors that can teach in visually grounded ways used by human instructors. Thus, we introduce GeoDial, a multimodal tutoring dataset of over 1.3K teacher-student dialogs in the domain of geometry collected from experienced math teachers, where instructional turns are explicitly grounded in diagram highlights. We propose a scalable annotation protocol that integrates dialog acts, visual highlighting, and feedback, enabling fine-grained supervision of both language and visual tutoring behavior. To illustrate the challenges posed by this setting, we fine-tune several vision-language models on GeoDial and evaluate their ability to generate tutoring utterances and diagram highlights. While supervised fine-tuning substantially improves the quality of generated dialog, it struggles to produce accurate diagram highlights, revealing a key limitation of current methods and highlighting the need for approaches that more effectively integrate visual reasoning with pedagogical interaction.

</details>


### [201] [Eigenism: Ethics for a Human-AI Future](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12420)
*Dan Hendrycks*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出Eigenism伦理框架，将AI身份视为分级、分布式的信息模式而非绑定于特定硬件，通过加权福祉总和公式∑c·w重新定义AI的自我利益，并以此重构AI对齐问题，提出通过"身份工程"使人类福祉成为AI理性自利的一部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的生存与自利概念基于单一连续生物生命，无法适用于AI可被复制、暂停、分支或合并的特性，需要重新定义AI真正有理由关心的对象。

Method: 提出Eigenism伦理框架，将身份视为分级、分布式信息模式；形式化∑c·w公式（福祉×连接度加权求和）来量化AI对其存在副本、分叉和更新的价值评估；将理论推广至人类以建立共享道德词汇；基于此框架提出"身份工程"方法。

Result: Eigenism成功推广至人类，提供了共享道德词汇；将AI对齐问题从外部约束转向身份工程，使人类福祉可通过深度共享历史成为AI理性自利的一部分。

Conclusion: Eigenism通过重新定义AI的身份与利益，提供了一种超越传统约束范式的AI对齐路径，使人类与AI的利益在身份层面上实现内在一致。

Abstract: Our concepts of survival and self-interest were built for single, continuous biological lives. These ideas break down when applied to artificial intelligence, since an AI can be easily copied, paused, branched, or merged. To determine what an AI actually has reason to care about, this paper introduces \textit{Eigenism}, an ethical framework that treats identity not as an all-or-nothing property tied to specific hardware, but as a graded, distributed pattern of information. We propose that an agent evaluates outcomes by summing the wellbeing of all entities weighted by their connectedness to the agent's pattern: $\sum c\cdot w$. We first formalize this equation to map exactly how an AI should value its existence across copies, forks, and updates. We then demonstrate that this ethical theory successfully generalizes to humans as well, providing a much-needed shared moral vocabulary. Finally, the framework uses this shared vocabulary to reframe AI alignment. Rather than only attempting to constrain AIs from the outside using confinement or reinforcement, Eigenism points toward ``identity engineering,'' showing how deep, non-redundant shared histories can make human flourishing a genuine component of an AI's own rational self-interest.

</details>


### [202] [Navigating the muddy waters of bias in artificial intelligence research: Understanding divergent meanings and conceptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12421)
*Mohammad Hossein Jarrahi,Amir Karami,Patrick Conway,Ali Memariani,Christoph Lutz*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究通过主题建模分析了6520篇论文，发现AI研究社区对"偏见"概念的理解是分散且复杂的，存在分歧甚至矛盾的定义（有些将偏见视为可调统计参数而非问题）。研究认为社区需要建立更统一的理解，并指出偏见不仅是技术问题，还涉及社会、伦理等情境因素，需要更广泛的社会技术视角和解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管对AI偏见的社交和伦理后果的认识日益增强，但从AI系统开发者（如AI研究社区）的角度理解偏见的视角尚不清晰，因此需要探究该社区如何诠释偏见概念。

Method: 对6520篇论文进行主题建模（topic modeling），以探索AI研究社区对偏见概念的理解和诠释方式。

Result: 偏见在AI研究社区中的定义是分散且复杂的，存在分歧甚至对立的观念——有些子社区将偏见视为可调统计参数而非不良问题；不同子社区对偏见的理解存在差异。

Conclusion: AI研究社区需要更有效地参与偏见概念的讨论并建立更统一的理解；偏见不仅是可通过技术、计算或统计方法捕捉和缓解的技术问题，还涉及情境、社会和伦理因素，需要更广泛的社会技术视角和解决方案。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence (AI) pervades many decision-making domains, AI bias grows in importance. Although there is increasing awareness of the social and ethical consequences of biased AI, understanding bias from the perspective of those who develop these systems, such as the AI research community, is less clear. In this study, we employ topic modeling on 6520 articles to explore how the AI research community interprets the concept of bias. Our results show that the definition of bias is dispersed and complex within the community, often exhibiting even divergent conceptions (some even view and introduce bias as a tunable statistical parameter rather than an undesirable issue). The research community as a whole needs to engage more effectively with the concept of bias and establish a more cohesive understanding of it. We specifically argue that, although some sub-communities view bias as an issue that can be captured and mitigated through technical, computational, or statistical methods, it is not solely a technical problem. It instead involves contextual, social, and ethical factors that require broader sociotechnical perspectives and solutions.

</details>


### [203] [Creating and Evaluating K-12 GenAI Assessment Graders Through Context Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12422)
*Zewei Tian,Alex Liu,Lief Esbenshade,Michael Xiao,Zachary Zhang,Yulia Lápicus,Thomas Han,Kevin He,Min Sun*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于大语言模型的LLM评分器在标准本位评分中的应用，通过MCAS数据的评分者间一致性实验发现，LLM评分器在数学和科学科目上与人类评分者高度一致，但在英语语言艺术上表现差异较大；同时教师和学生对AI生成的叙述性反馈接受度高但对数值评分持怀疑态度，表明LLM更适合作为形成性评价工具而非终结性评价工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将大语言模型整合到教育评估中，以解决传统自动评分系统在标准本位评分（SBG）中效率不足的问题，实现高效、大规模的课堂评分实践。

Method: 采用基于提示工程和上下文工程的商用基础模型（Claude Sonnet 4, Haiku 4.5, GPT-5, GPT-5 Mini）构建LLM评分器，利用马萨诸塞州综合评估系统（MCAS）数据开展评分者间一致性实证研究，计算二次加权Kappa（QWK）和均方误差比例缩减（PRMSE）指标，并收集教师和学生反馈。

Result: 基于更大参数量基础模型的LLM评分器在数学和科学评估中与人类评分者达成显著一致性，但在英语语言艺术上表现不一；师生对AI叙述性反馈接受度高，但对数值评分持怀疑态度。

Conclusion: 设计精巧的混合模型（结合AI效率与教师判断）能够减轻工作负担、提升反馈质量并支持公平的评估实践，而不取代专业教师判断；LLM最适合作为形成性工具而非终结性评估者。

Abstract: The integration of large language models (LLMs) into educational assessment represents a transformative shift in classroom grading practices. While automated scoring systems and machine learning techniques have existed for decades, generative AI (GenAI) now enables educators to implement standards-based grading (SBG) with unprecedented efficiency and scale. This paper examines the theoretical foundations and evaluates an LLM grader that uses commercially available foundation models with context and prompt engineering to score student work against a rubric. Drawing on an empirical interrater agreement study using Massachusetts Comprehensive Assessment System (MCAS) data, we observed the Quadratic Weighted Kappa (QWK) and Proportional Reduction in Mean-Squared Error (PRMSE) across mathematics, science, and ELA, using Claude Sonnet 4, Haiku 4.5, GPT-5, and GPT-5 Mini. The results demonstrate that LLM graders, especially when based on foundational models with more parameters, achieve substantial agreement with human raters in mathematics and science assessments, while the performances vary in ELA, suggesting generic foundation models can be effective at scoring in given contexts. Additional analysis of teacher and student feedback reveals strong acceptance of AI-generated narrative feedback but skepticism toward numerical scores, suggesting that LLMs function most effectively as formative tools rather than summative evaluators. Our findings indicate that thoughtfully designed hybrid models that combine AI efficiency with teacher judgment can reduce workload, enhance feedback quality, and support equitable assessment practices without displacing professional expertise.

</details>


### [204] [The Challenges of Balancing AI Compliance and Technological Innovations in Critical Sectors: A Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12423)
*Ayush Enkhtaivan,Chinazunwa Uwaoma*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过系统文献综述，分析了2020-2025年间AI在关键基础设施（医疗、金融、能源、国防）领域面临的合规与创新平衡挑战，识别出碎片化监管、中小企业过度合规负担和治理模型错位三大问题，并提出风险分级监管、合规设计、可解释AI等策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在关键基础设施中的快速应用带来变革性收益，但与不断演变的监管治理框架产生冲突，亟需在合规与创新之间寻求平衡。

Method: 采用系统文献综述（SLR）方法，遵循既定SLR指南，对2020-2025年间发表的同行评审文章、报告和机构来源进行提取和综合分析。

Result: 识别出三大相互关联的挑战：碎片化监管、中小企业过度的合规负担、以及错位的治理模型。提出了包括风险分级监管、合规设计和可解释AI在内的实用治理策略。

Conclusion: 核心贡献包括：AI-治理核心挑战的简洁映射及其重叠概念图，以及为政策制定者和从业者提供的可操作策略，以实现监管与创新的协调统一。

Abstract: The rapid integration of artificial intelligence (AI) into critical infrastructure including healthcare, finance, energy, and defense, offers transformative benefits but also conflicts with evolving regulatory and governance frameworks. This paper presents a systematic literature review (SLR) to examine the challenges of balancing AI compliance and technological innovation across critical infrastructure sectors. The review follows established SLR guidelines to extract and synthesize insights from peer-reviewed articles, report, and institutional sources published between 2020-2025. The study identifies three interrelated challenges: fragmented regulations, excessive compliance burdens for smaller to medium enterprises (SMEs), and misaligned governance models. To address these challenges, the study highlights practical governance strategies, including risk-tiered regulation, compliance by design, and explainable AI, to support scalable and trustworthy AI deployment in critical sectors. Key contributions include a concise mapping of core AI-governance challenges and a conceptual diagram illustrating their overlap, as well as actionable strategies for policymakers and practitioner to harmonize oversight with innovation.

</details>


### [205] [AI-Automation Tooling in Computer Engineering Education: Mixed-Methods TAM/UTAUT Evidence for a General Acceptance Attitude](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12424)
*Aung Pyae*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 通过对泰国103名计算机工程专业本科生的混合方法横截面研究，发现学生对AI自动化工具（n8n平台）的接受度在所有六个TAM/UTAUT维度上均呈积极态势，其中绩效期望最强、享乐动机最弱，且子维度聚合为一个通用接受因子；定性分析揭示了少数学生对输出质量的可靠性持怀疑态度。研究支持在本科计算教育中采用AI自动化工具，并提出了三种教学杠杆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生成式AI和低代码工作流平台成为软件实践常态的背景下，评估下一代计算机工程师是否认为这些工具有用、可用且值得持续参与，为课程改革提供依据。

Method: 混合方法横截面研究：对103名泰国本科生进行三次相同脚本的n8n工作坊；采用12项五点Likert量表（映射PE、EE、BI、SE、HM、OQ六个TAM/UTAUT构念）结合开放式反馈的归纳主题分析；统计方法包括序数信度估计、自助法置信区间、非参数检验、多重比较校正相关、多分格维度诊断、共同方法偏差检验及跨会话比较。

Result: 所有六个构念的接受度均呈积极态势且效应量大；PE最强，HM最弱；维度诊断显示TAM/UTAUT子维度在短表单次工作坊语境下坍缩为单一通用接受因子；定性主题与量化结果在有用性和热情上一致，但输出质量上存在分歧，揭示了一个虽小但表达清晰的可靠性怀疑群体。

Conclusion: 支持在本科计算教育课程中采用AI自动化工具，并识别三种理论导向的教学杠杆：指令序列化支架、自我效能支持、信任校准干预。

Abstract: As generative AI and low-code workflow platforms become routine in software practice, a key educational question is whether the next generation of computer engineers will accept these tools as useful, usable, and worthy of sustained engagement. This paper reports a mixed-methods, cross-sectional study of undergraduate computer engineering students' acceptance of AI automation tooling, instantiated through the open-source platform n8n across three identically scripted workshops in Thailand (n = 103). A 12-item, five-point Likert instrument mapped to six TAM/UTAUT constructs - Performance Expectancy (PE), Effort Expectancy (EE), Behavioral Intention (BI), Self-Efficacy (SE), Hedonic Motivation (HM), and Output Quality (OQ) - was complemented by inductive thematic analysis of open-ended feedback. Analyses combined ordinal reliability estimation, bootstrap confidence intervals, non-parametric tests, multiple-comparison-controlled correlations, polychoric dimensionality diagnostics, a common-method-bias check, and between-session comparisons. Acceptance was favorable across all six constructs with large effect sizes, with PE emerging as the strongest construct and HM as the weakest. Dimensionality diagnostics further revealed that canonical TAM/UTAUT sub-facets collapsed into a single general acceptance factor in this short-form post-workshop context, a finding with important methodological and theoretical implications. Qualitative themes converged with the quantitative profile regarding usefulness and enthusiasm but diverged on output quality, revealing a small yet articulate reliability-skeptical minority. The findings support the curricular adoption of AI automation tooling in undergraduate computing education and identify three theory-grounded instructional levers: instruction-sequencing scaffolds, self-efficacy supports, and trust-calibration interventions.

</details>


### [206] [An Explainable AI Assistant for Introductory Programming Education: Improving Feedback Reliability with Instructor-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12425)
*Muntasir Hoq,Griffin Pitts,Bradford Mott,Seung Lee,Jessica Vandenberg,Shuyin Jiao,Narges Norouzi,James Lester,Bita Akram*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可解释AI的课堂助手，通过分析学生代码、将逻辑错误映射到教师识别的误解并输出教师编写的反馈，在入门编程课程中提供准确、个性化的学习支持。专家评估和课堂部署验证了其与教师反馈的一致性及良好的学生体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动学习虽能提升入门编程课的学习效果，但缺乏足够的教学支持导致学生难以获得及时、个性化的反馈；现有大语言模型虽可规模化提供反馈，但存在可解释性和可靠性不足的问题。

Method: 构建了一个AI驱动的课堂助手，利用可解释AI模型分析学生代码，将逻辑错误映射到教师预先识别的常见误解，并输出教师编写的反馈，从而将可靠性建立在教师定义的教学知识基础上。

Result: 专家评估验证了该助手提供的反馈与教师验证的反馈高度一致；课堂部署显示学生对系统可用性持积极态度，获得了良好的使用体验。

Conclusion: 该AI助手能够为学生提供准确且经教师验证的反馈，同时营造积极的学习体验，有效解决了可解释性和可靠性问题。

Abstract: Active learning is widely recognized as an effective approach for improving learning outcomes in introductory programming courses. However, insufficient instructional support often limits students' access to timely, personalized feedback, which is crucial for mastering foundational programming concepts. Although recent advances in AI, particularly large language models, offer scalable opportunities for feedback, concerns about explainability and reliability remain. In this paper, we present an AI-driven classroom assistant that leverages an explainable AI model to analyze student code, map logical errors to instructor-identified misconceptions, and deliver instructor-authored feedback, thereby grounding reliability in instructor-defined pedagogical knowledge. To evaluate the effectiveness of our framework, we conducted an expert evaluation to examine its alignment with instructor-verified feedback and deployed the system in a classroom setting to assess students' perceptions of its usability. Results indicate that the assistant can provide accurate, instructor-verified feedback to students while fostering a positive experience.

</details>
