### [207] [Two Wrongs, No Right: Auditing Social-Desirability Bias in LLM Annotators for Computational Social Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12426)
*Varun Kotte*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文系统审计了三个开源7B指令微调模型在六项TweetEval任务中的标注偏差，发现社会期望性失败并非单向：Zephyr呈现宽容偏差（低估有害标签），Mistral和Qwen呈现过度纠正（高估有害标签），三者均在中立性偏差上显著扭曲立场标签。聚合指标看似校准的模型仍可能颠覆实质经验结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM标注者在计算社会科学中被广泛使用，但其对齐训练引入的误差是否会颠覆研究者本应报告的经验结论尚不明确。现有研究缺乏对方向性偏差、聚合指标误导性及提示干预效果的系统审计。

Method: 审计三个开源7B指令微调模型（Zephyr、Mistral-Instruct、Qwen2.5-Instruct），覆盖六项TweetEval任务、四种提示条件（中性/安全框架/去个性化/思维链），共72个实验单元格，通过误报率（FAR）和错误良性率（FBR）进行诊断性分析，并引入轻量级金标样本验证协议。

Result: Zephyr存在宽容偏差（冒犯性语言FBR=0.729, FAR=0.031）；Mistral和Qwen存在过度纠正偏差（Mistral仇恨言论FAR=0.604）；三类模型在堕胎立场上均低估反对派比例24-40个百分点并夸大中立标签；四种提示干预均无法跨模型纠正偏差，安全框架反而加剧立场扭曲；Zephyr的仇恨言论聚合估计与金标一致，但类别条件误差双向巨大，属于偶然抵消。

Conclusion: 聚合指标校准的模型仍可能翻转经验结论；提出基于FBR/FAR诊断特征的偏差分类法，以及轻量级金标样本验证协议，呼吁计算社会科学中需警惕LLM标注的隐蔽偏差。

Abstract: LLM annotators are increasingly used in computational social science (CSS), but it is unclear whether their alignment-shaped errors preserve the empirical conclusions a researcher would report. We audit three open-source 7B instruction-tuned models (Zephyr, Mistral-Instruct, Qwen2.5-Instruct) across six TweetEval tasks under four prompt conditions (72 cells) and find that social-desirability failures do not run in a single direction. Zephyr exhibits leniency bias, systematically under-applying harmful labels (offensive language: false benign rate 0.729, false alarm rate 0.031). Mistral and Qwen exhibit overcorrection, over-applying the same labels (Mistral hate-speech FAR = 0.604). All three models exhibit neutrality bias on abortion stance, underestimating opposition prevalence by 24 to 40 percentage points and inflating the neutral label. None of the four prompting interventions we test (neutral, safety framing, depersonalized, chain-of-thought) corrects these failures across models; safety framing can worsen stance distortion. Strikingly, Zephyr's hate-speech prevalence estimate matches the gold rate exactly while its class-conditional errors are large in both directions, an accidental cancellation that misleads aggregate validation. We translate these patterns into a three-part taxonomy with diagnostic FBR/FAR signatures and a lightweight gold-sample validation protocol. The headline for trustworthy CSS: a model that looks calibrated on aggregate metrics can still flip the substantive empirical conclusion a researcher would report.

</details>


### [208] [Planning on Paper: Problem Decomposition with Diagrams in Introductory Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12427)
*Annapurna Vadaparty,Devamardeep Hayatpur,Adalbert Gerald Soosai Raj,Leo Porter,Daniel Zingaro*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究通过对55份学生手绘分解图的分析，揭示了初学者在多函数任务中的问题分解表现：他们使用多种表征策略（如层次化函数调用和执行顺序），但常出现符号混用、执行顺序混乱、抽象与复用不足、封装不清等问题，表明初学者的分解思维受到结构化和顺序化两种程序模型之间的张力影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前计算教育中，问题分解日益受到重视（尤其在GenAI背景下，CS1教学正从代码编写转向分解与高层规划），但缺乏对初学如何完成多函数大规模任务分解的系统性认识。

Method: 在50分钟实验课中，学生根据文字游戏描述手绘分解图，研究者对55份图进行归纳主题分析（协商一致性编码），标注要素（函数及其关系）和问题。

Result: 学生使用了多种表征策略，包括层次化函数调用和顺序执行。发现了符号混用、执行顺序问题、抽象与复用不足、封装不清、清晰度问题及对问题的特定误解。

Conclusion: 初学者的分解受多种程序行为模型影响，结构推理与顺序推理之间存在张力。建议通过明确表征约束和计划追踪（作为模拟）来改进分解教学。

Abstract: Background and Context. Problem decomposition is a core concern of computing education. It has also become increasingly relevant: in response to GenAI, many CS1 educators are advocating for shifting instructional emphasis away from code writing and towards decomposition and higher-level planning. Currently, there is a lack of knowledge in how novices do decomposition in large, multifunction tasks. Objectives. In this study, we describe how students represent solutions to a decomposition task, and characterize common issues that arise in those representations. Method. In a 50-minute lab, students were given a description of a word game and asked to draw (with pencil and paper) a decomposition diagram for a program that would implement this game. We performed an inductive thematic analysis with negotiated agreement on 55 of the diagrams, coding salient elements (e.g. functions and the relationships between them) and issues that arose. Findings. Students used multiple representational strategies, including hierarchical function calls and sequencing (order of execution). We identified issues in notation (including use of differing, incompatible notations within the same diagram), order of execution, abstraction and reuse, encapsulation, clarity, and problem-specific misunderstandings. Implications. These findings suggest that novice decomposition is shaped by multiple underlying models of program behavior, with tensions between structural and sequence-focused reasoning. We discuss implications for decomposition instruction and future work, including clarifying representational constraints and plan tracing as simulation.

</details>


### [209] [Mapping AI Programs in the U.S: A Status Report from Early 2026 and an Analysis of AI Majors and Minors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12428)
*Felix Muzny,Carolyn Jones,Carter Ithier,Hasnain Sikora,Hrutika Harshadbhai Patel,Carla E. Brodley*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该论文报告了2026年春季美国本科人工智能（AI）项目的现状，开发了一个动态更新的工具（cicmap.ai），该工具从超过560所机构中检测、抓取并展示了350多个本科AI项目（专业、辅修、方向和证书），覆盖美国86%的计算机科学本科毕业生。论文提供了三个贡献：1）动荡时期美国AI项目的历史记录；2）探索AI项目及其要求的工具；3）对66个AI专业和87个AI辅修课程要求的分析。分析发现，并非所有AI专业都要求通用AI课程，但若不要求则必须包含机器学习课程；超过三分之一的AI专业要求伦理课程，而AI辅修中这一比例不足四分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AI教育快速发展的动荡时期，缺乏对美国本科AI项目全面、动态的跟踪工具和历史记录，导致 prospective students、顾问、管理人员和教师难以系统了解AI项目的要求和分布情况。

Method: 开发了一个自动化的网页抓取和映射工具（cicmap.ai），对超过560所四年制大学进行系统性搜索，检测并提取AI相关项目（专业、辅修、方向、证书）的信息，并持续更新。对识别出的66个AI专业和87个AI辅修的课程要求进行了统计分析。

Result: 成功识别并展示了350多个本科AI项目，覆盖86%的美国CS毕业生。分析发现：1）非所有AI专业都要求通用AI课程，但未要求者必含机器学习课程；2）超过三分之一的AI专业要求伦理AI课程，而AI辅修中该比例不足四分之一；3）各项目在规模和课程要求上存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了截至2026年春季美国本科AI教育最全面的快照，其动态工具为持续跟踪AI教育发展提供了基础设施。课程分析揭示了各项目对核心AI知识和伦理教育的不同侧重，为教育政策制定和课程设计提供了参考依据。

Abstract: We present a report on the status of undergraduate Artificial Intelligence (AI) programs in the United States in Spring 2026. In so doing, we 1) describe our scraping and mapping tools, which dynamically update to track the state of AI education in the U.S., and 2) create a historic record at a time of great upheaval. The tool we developed, available at https://cicmap.ai, detects, scrapes, and displays data from more than 350 undergraduate AI programs--majors, minors, concentrations, and certificates--at 4-year universities. Our tool searched over 560 institutions to locate these programs, a sample that represents 86\% of all undergraduate Computer Science (CS) graduates in the U.S. This tool allows prospective students, guidance counselors, administrators, and faculty to easily access AI program requirements and is designed to continually update as new programs emerge. To the best of our knowledge, this survey represents the most comprehensive snapshot of the state of AI programs in the U.S. to date. With this work we offer three important contributions: 1) a record of AI programs in the U.S. at a time of great upheaval; 2) a tool to explore AI programs and their requirements; and 3) an analysis of the courses required for 66 AI majors and 87 AI minors. Our analysis of majors and minors shows great variability in the size and the requirements of these degrees, but we note two takeaways. First, not all majors require a general AI course, but if they don't, they do require a Machine Learning (ML) course. Second, while more than a third of majors require an Ethics in AI course, just under a quarter of AI minors do.

</details>


### [210] [Muse Spark Safety & Preparedness Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12429)
*Cristina Menghini,Peter Ney,Hamza Kwisaba,Zifan,Wang,Miles Turpin,Felix Binder,Jean-Christophe Testud,Aidan Boyd,Nathaniel Li,Ivan Evtimov,Klaudia Krawiecka,Arman Zharmagambetov,Jeremy Kritz,Alexander R. Fabbri,Daniel Song,Jinpeng Miao,Joonas Hjelt,Meghna Ramani,Leona Lan,Reza Aghajani,Joanna Bitton,Mahesh Pasupuleti,Devin Norder,Khalid El-Arini,Paridhi Singh,Vítor Albiero,Sahana CB,Rashnil Chaturvedi,Elahe Dabir,Edoardo Debenedetti,Jim Gust,Ziwen Han,Kat He,Sean Hendryx,Lifeng Jin,Polina Kirichenko,Sandra Lefdal,Kenneth Li,Asad Liaqat,Inna Lin,Despoina Magka,Neal Mangaokar,Ishita Mediratta,Zach Miller,Smitha Milli,Niloofar Mireshghallah,Saba Nazir,Hung Nguyen,Maximilian Nickel,Kelvin Niu,Kerem Oktar,Bhargavi Paranjape,Parth Pathak,Maya Pavlova,Emmanuel Ramirez,David Renardy,Candace Ross,Yasha Sheynin,Claudia Shi,Shivam Singhal,Evangelia Spiliopoulou,Rakshith Sharma Srinivasa,Jamelle Watson-Daniels,Spencer Whitman,Adina Williams,Chen Xing,Andy Zou,Tommy Ma,Siqi Deng,James Beldock,Prashant Ratanchandani,Kate Plawiak,Taesung Lee,Ryan Victory,Lindsay Hundley,Rachad Alao,Himaghna Bhattacharjee,Jianfeng Chi,Gary Frost,Pegah Ghahremani,Niki Howe,Yuheng Huang,Saeed Jahed,Hannah Korevaar,Trang Le,Zhe Liu,Jinghong Luo,Qin Lyu,Nina Mehrabi,Abraham Montilla,Chirag Nagpal,Cyrus Nikolaidis,Rajvardhan Oak,Manoj Ravi,Vidya Sarma,Aman Shankar,Alana Shine,Eric Michael Smith,Mariana Tandon,Michael Tontchev,Caoyu Wang,Zihan Wang,Corinne Wong,Zheng Wu,Hongyuan Zhan,Justin Zhao,Zexuan Zhong,Chengxu Zhuang,Tristan Goodman,Ayaz Minhas,Harrison Rudolph,Victoria Jeffries,Ingrid Dickinson,Alex Vaughan,Lauren Deason,Kamalika Chaudhuri,Julian Michael,Shengjia Zhao,Summer Yue*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: Meta发布了最新大语言模型Muse Spark，并依据其高级AI扩展框架评估了该模型在化学/生物、网络安全和失控风险等灾难性风险领域的部署安全性。评估发现未经缓解措施时化学与生物能力达到"高风险"级别，但通过多层缓解措施后，Muse Spark在有害工作流相关基准上表现出最先进的拒绝能力，最终被认为残余风险可接受，作为Meta AI的底层模型发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Meta需要对其最新大语言模型Muse Spark进行系统性安全评估，以确定其是否适合作为Meta AI的底层模型公开发布。评估需覆盖灾难性风险领域（化学/生物、网络安全、失控风险）以及更广泛的内容安全与行为特征，从而为发布决策提供科学依据。

Method: 采用Meta高级AI扩展框架，对Muse Spark进行化学与生物、网络安全和失控风险三大领域的灾难性风险评估。通过广泛的针对双重用途和高风险能力的评测基准，识别潜在风险等级，并实施多层缓解措施（如安全过滤、拒绝机制等），最终评估缓解后的残余风险水平。

Result: 在缓解措施实施前，Muse Spark在化学与生物领域的双用途能力被评估为可能达到"高风险"级别。经过多层缓解措施后，Muse Spark在涉及化学与生物有害工作流的多个基准上展现出最先进的拒绝能力。最终，在Meta AI部署场景下的所有三个灾难性风险领域均被评定为可接受的残余风险水平。

Conclusion: Muse Spark在实施多层安全缓解措施后，其部署于Meta AI所带来的灾难性风险已被降低至可接受水平。Meta据此决定将Muse Spark作为Meta AI的底层模型公开发布。该评估结论支持在适当安全防护下推进先进AI模型的部署。

Abstract: Muse Spark is the latest large language model developed by Meta. In this report, we first present evaluations for catastrophic risk domains under Meta's Advanced AI Scaling Framework, along with the evidence that informed our launch decision. We then discuss additional considerations, such as Muse Spark's broader content safety and behavioral profile, that are relevant to overall safety but fall outside the catastrophic risk domains governed by the Framework. Our preparedness results covering Chemical and Biological, Cybersecurity, and Loss of Control risks assess Muse Spark's deployment within Meta AI as presenting acceptable levels of residual risks under our Advanced AI Scaling Framework. We conducted a broad set of evaluations targeting dual-use and high-risk capabilities across these catastrophic risk domains. Those evaluations identified elevated risks prior to mitigations, with Chemical and Biological capabilities assessed as likely reaching the "high risk" category under the Advanced AI Scaling Framework before safeguards were applied. We have implemented a multi-layered set of mitigations that address the identified risks, and Muse Spark demonstrates state-of-the-art refusal across a range of benchmarks related to hazardous workflows in chemistry and biology. We therefore release Muse Spark as the underlying model of Meta AI.

</details>


### [211] [Will AI Agents Free Us From Meaningless Work? A Human-Centered Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12430)
*Davide Ghia,Jaspreet Ranjit,Tania Cerquitelli,Daniele Quercia*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 工人对工作任务的“扯淡感”认知是预测其是否愿意将任务委托给AI的关键因素，且这类任务也被认为需要较少的人类监督，从而与AI部署的可行性相吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究关注职业层面的自动化，忽略了同一职位内不同任务对工人意义感的差异。本研究旨在从任务层面填补这一空白，探讨工人如何识别应被自动化的任务。

Method: 基于Graeber的“扯淡工作”理论，收集202名工人对171项工作任务的评分数据，验证了五维度的“感知扯淡”量表，并分析该感知与AI委托意愿及人类监督需求的关系。

Result: （1）验证了感知扯淡的五项量表；（2）感知扯淡强烈预测AI委托意愿；（3）感知扯淡的任务也被认为需要较少的人类监督。

Conclusion: 被认为“扯淡”的任务天然适合由AI代理执行，这与工人偏好和AI部署的可行性一致。

Abstract: Some claim that AI agents will free workers from the boring parts of their jobs, yet little is known about how workers themselves identify which tasks should be automated. Prior research focuses on occupations, overlooking that workers experience varying levels of meaning across tasks within the same role. We address this gap with a task-level analysis grounded in Graeber's theory of bullshit jobs. Using ratings from 202 workers on 171 workplace tasks, we (1) validate a five-item scale of perceived bullshitness, (2) show that perceived bullshitness strongly predicts desire for AI delegation, and (3) find that such tasks are also seen as requiring less human oversight. Together, these findings suggest that tasks perceived as bullshit are natural candidates for AI delegation, aligning worker preferences with perceived feasibility.

</details>


### [212] [AI Debris: Residual Risk and the Afterlife of Failed AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12432)
*Victor Frimpong*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"AI残留物"概念，指AI系统退役后仍存在的社会技术残余（如工作流依赖、数据污染、能力退化等），并设计了AI残留物退役协议（AIDP）作为治理工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI治理框架仅关注系统开发与部署阶段的风险，忽视系统退役后残留的持续性风险及其对制度行为、问责制和信任的影响。

Method: 通过理论分析构建残留物类型学（残域），识别持续机制（制度记忆、路径依赖、推卸责任、组织数据反馈效应），并设计可审计的AIDP检查清单，辅以亚马逊招聘工具案例验证。

Result: 提出AI残留物五维分类（工作流依赖、数据污染、能力位移/去技能化、合法性侵蚀、问责制崩溃），并开发了包含决策足迹冻结、事件审查、补救、可争议性、退出后问责分配的AIDP协议。

Conclusion: AI残留物概念及AIDP协议为监管者、审计师和组织提供了强化AI全生命周期治理、防止纸张合规、提升高风险决策环境制度韧性的实用工具。

Abstract: AI governance frameworks primarily focus on risks during the development and deployment phases, implicitly treating system withdrawal as a technical shutdown. This paper argues that decommissioned AI systems generate residual risk, termed AI debris, that persists after model removal and continues to shape institutional behaviour, accountability, and trust. AI debris is defined as the post-withdrawal socio-technical residue of AI systems, including workflow dependency, data contamination, capability displacement (deskilling), legitimacy erosion, and accountability breakdown. The paper develops a typology of debris domains and identifies mechanisms through which debris persists, including institutional memory, path dependency, blame avoidance, and feedback effects in organisational data. To operationalise the concept, the paper proposes an evaluator-ready AI Debris Decommissioning Protocol (AIDP), a stepwise checklist specifying auditable evidence for freezing decision footprints, incident review, remediation, contestability, and post-withdrawal accountability assignment. A brief vignette of Amazon's discontinued hiring tool illustrates how algorithmic decision categories and screening heuristics can persist after system rollback. The paper contributes a practical governance instrument for regulators, auditors, and organisations seeking to prevent paper compliance, strengthen AI lifecycle governance, and improve institutional resilience in high-stakes decision environments.

</details>


### [213] [Marginal Alignment Does Not Guarantee Joint-Distribution Fidelity: An Official-Reference Audit of Nemotron-Personas-Korea with Cross-Locale Replication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12433)
*Joonhyung Bae*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 提出独立性假设足迹(IAF)审计方法，发现NVIDIA的NPK合成人格数据集在边际分布上与官方数据一致，但三个联合分布存在显著偏差：职业分布条件不匹配、兵役年龄结构矛盾、女性在男性主导职业中过度扁平化。六地区迁移实验表明诊断具有地区依赖性而非普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成人格数据集通常宣称基于官方人口边际分布对齐来建立信任，但下游用户实际消费的是跨年龄、性别、地区、职业、教育、姓名和机构地位的多维联合分布。边际对齐并不能保证联合分布被保留，当前缺少针对合成数据中属性组合联合分布的审计方法。

Method: 提出Independence-Assumption Footprint (IAF)审计原语，作用于数据集卡片自身声明为独立处理的属性组合。对于每种组合，IAF将合成联合分布与外部官方或机构参考进行比较，可用时使用直接联合表，否则使用规则隐含检查。应用于NVIDIA Nemotron-Personas-Korea (NPK)百万级韩国合成人格数据集。

Result: NPK与KOSIS边际分布对齐，但三个联合分布失败：主要职业分布与KEIS毕业生数据存在大条件不匹配；兵役年龄分布存在机构不一致；女性在男性主导职业中的代表性过度扁平化趋向均等。六地区迁移实验发现诊断具有地区依赖性而非普适性，参考分类法的基数混淆了跨地区标记计数。

Conclusion: 对于用作硅样本的合成人格数据，边际对齐声明必须配以基于披露锚点的联合审计才能复用。发布的审计工件（参考清单、职业交叉映射、衍生指标、可复现脚本）在NPK族上实例化该协议，并可供重定向到其他合成人格资源。

Abstract: Synthetic persona datasets cite alignment with official demographics as a basis for trust, yet downstream users consume them as joint structures across age, sex, region, occupation, education, name, and institutional status. Marginal alignment does not imply that these joints are preserved. We propose the Independence-Assumption Footprint (IAF), an audit primitive that operates on the attribute combinations a dataset card itself documents as treated independently. For each such combination, IAF compares the synthetic joint against an external official or institutional reference, using direct joint tables where available and rule-implied checks otherwise. Applied to NVIDIA Nemotron-Personas-Korea (one million Korean synthetic personas), IAF finds that NPK aligns with KOSIS marginals while three joints fail. The major-by-occupation distribution against the KEIS graduate universe carries a large conditional mismatch. The age profile of military service is institutionally inconsistent. Female representation in male-dominated occupations is substantially over-flattened toward parity, with the strict screening verdict mapping-dependent and age-robust under direct standardisation. A transferability demonstration across six further NPK locales finds locale-dependent rather than universal diagnostics, with reference-taxonomy cardinality confounding cross-locale flag counts. For synthetic personas used as silicon samples, marginal claims must therefore be paired with disclosure-anchored joint audits before reuse. The released audit artefacts (reference manifests, occupational crosswalks, derived metrics, reproducibility scripts) instantiate this protocol on the NPK family and are released for retargeting at other synthetic persona resources.

</details>


### [214] [Pluralistic-Alignment Urbanism: Operationalizing a Right to AI for Inclusive Public Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12434)
*Rashid Mushkani*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出多元对齐都市主义（PAU），一个将公共空间AI系统视为市政基础设施的治理框架，并通过蒙特利尔两个社区参与案例研究，论证了分歧、子群体差异、有界预测缩放和中性偏好判断如何约束市政AI治理，最终设计出包含拆解报告、版本化价值登记、常设审议小组等机制的制度架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 市政机构日益使用机器学习进行 sidewalk 盘点、街景评分和公共空间干预可视化，但这些系统在包容性、安全性和归属感方面的判断存在争议，缺乏适当的治理框架。

Method: 通过蒙特利尔两个参与式案例研究：Street Review（收集居民街道评估标准并训练子群体感知的缩放模型，R²=0.89）和LIVS（构建多元偏好数据集以对齐文本到图像模型，将中性选择视为不确定性证据）。

Result: 分歧具有结构性； deliberation 改变了证据的性质；缩放在模态和覆盖范围上有界；中性偏好限制了偏好调优的合理性。PAU由此推演出拆解报告、版本化价值登记、常设审议小组、采购条款以及暂停与回滚权限等治理机制。

Conclusion: 提出了一个将公共空间AI系统视为公民基础设施的程序性治理框架——多元对齐都市主义（PAU），并形式化了针对市政AI使用的程序性"AI权利"。

Abstract: Municipal agencies increasingly use machine learning to inventory sidewalks, score streetscapes, and generate visualizations of public-space interventions. These systems produce outputs that enter budgeting, design iteration, and public justification, yet judgments about inclusion, safety, and belonging remain contested. This paper proposes Pluralistic-Alignment Urbanism (PAU), a procedural governance framework that treats public-space AI systems as civic infrastructure and formulates a procedural Right to AI for municipal uses of such systems. Drawing on two participatory case studies with community organizations in Montreal, Canada, the paper examines how disagreement, subgroup variation, bounded predictive scaling, and neutral preference judgments can inform municipal AI governance. Street Review elicits resident criteria for streetscape evaluation and trains a subgroup-aware scaling model for co-produced judgments, achieving an R2 of 0.89 on a held-out test set. LIVS, a Local Intersectional Visual Spaces dataset, constructs pluralistic preference data for aligning text-to-image models and treats neutral selections as evidence of indeterminacy. Across the cases, disagreement appears structured, deliberation changes what counts as evidence, scaling is feasible but limited by modality and coverage, and neutrality constrains what preference tuning can justify. PAU translates these constraints into a municipal governance architecture with disaggregated reporting, a versioned value register, standing deliberative cells, procurement clauses, and defined pause and rollback authority.

</details>


### [215] [Auditing Discriminatory Patterns in Mortgage Lending Through Association Rules and Fair Binning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12435)
*Archit Rathod,Dhwani Chande,Het Nagda*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究使用103,481条HMDA 2023抵押贷款申请数据，构建了包含数据分箱、FP-Growth关联规则挖掘和K-Means聚类的三阶段流水线，发现标准分箱在收入离散化中存在9.63%的种族偏差，公平分箱在epsilon=0.08时才能成功且公平代价为29.4%，FP-Growth显示高债务收入比是主要拒绝预测因子，但K-Means聚类后的差异影响审计发现10个聚类-群体对存在显著种族差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国抵押贷款领域存在持续的种族和性别差异，研究旨在探究标准数据预处理步骤（特别是属性分箱）是否会在下游模式挖掘中放大这些差异。

Method: 三阶段流水线：(1) PySpark数据清洗与分箱（标准等频分箱 vs. epsilon偏差公平分箱）；(2) FP-Growth关联规则挖掘比较拒绝模式；(3) K-Means聚类与逐聚类差异影响审计。

Result: 标准分箱存在9.63%种族偏差；公平分箱在epsilon=0.03时不可行，epsilon=0.08时公平代价29.4%；FP-Growth未发现种族偏见的显式高支持度规则；聚类审计发现10/45个聚类-群体对中黑人申请者拒绝率显著高于白人。

Conclusion: 标准预处理中的分箱操作会引入种族偏差，而公平分箱虽然可以缓解但代价高昂；关联规则挖掘可能掩盖结构性偏差，但结合聚类审计能有效揭示隐藏的群体差异。

Abstract: Mortgage lending in the United States exhibits persistent racial and gender disparities. We investigate whether standard data preprocessing steps, specifically attribute binning, amplify these disparities in downstream pattern mining. Using 103,481 cleaned mortgage applications from the HMDA 2023 dataset (Chicago metropolitan area), we build a three-stage pipeline: (1) a PySpark data cleaning and binning pipeline that implements both standard equal-frequency binning and the epsilon-biased fair binning algorithm from Asudeh et al. [1], (2) FP-Growth association rule mining that compares denial patterns under both binning regimes, and (3) K-Means clustering with a per-cluster disparate impact audit. Our standard binning shows 9.63% racial bias in income discretization, consistent with the 8-10% reported in prior work. Fair binning with seven race groups is infeasible at epsilon=0.03 and only succeeds at epsilon=0.08 with a Price of Fairness of 29.4%. FP-Growth reveals that high debt-to-income ratio is the dominant denial predictor (67.2% confidence, 2.81 lift), while racial bias does not appear as explicit high-support rules. However, K-Means clustering followed by a disparate impact audit flags 10 out of 45 cluster-group pairs, showing that Black applicants face significantly higher denial rates than White applicants even among financially similar groups.

</details>


### [216] [Knowing the Rules Is Not Enough: Student Regulatory Awareness and Use of GenAI in Higher Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12436)
*Lasse Bischof,Eva-Maria Schön,Maria Rauschenberger,Michael Neumann*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究调查了151名计算机相关专业本科生，发现大多数学生积极使用GenAI工具，但超过半数不确定自己的使用是否符合学校规定。监管意识与实际使用行为之间仅存在弱到中等的关联，学生主要依赖私人渠道而非学校提供的GenAI工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注GenAI工具的采用率和学生态度，但学生对学校监管规定的了解程度以及他们感知的合规性尚未被充分探索。了解监管意识是否影响学生行为对于高等教育机构制定和执行AI政策至关重要。

Method: 对德国汉诺威应用科学与艺术大学151名商业信息系统和电子政务专业的本科生进行问卷调查，收集关于GenAI使用情况、使用工具、对学校规定的了解程度以及感知合规性的数据。采用描述性统计、交叉表和相关性分析进行分析。

Result: 大多数学生积极使用GenAI工具，但超过半数对自身使用是否符合学校规定表示不确定。监管意识与实际使用行为之间仅存在弱到中等的关联。学生主要依赖私人获取的GenAI工具，而非学校提供的解决方案。

Conclusion: 研究提供了关于监管意识与GenAI使用之间关系的实证证据，揭示了学校规定与学生实践之间的差距，为教育工作者和机构改进政策沟通以及更有效地将GenAI融入教学提供了见解。

Abstract: Context: Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) tools such as ChatGPT are increasingly integrated into students learning practices. While previous research mainly examines adoption rates and attitudes, students awareness of institutional regulations and their perceived compliance remain unexplored. Understanding whether regulatory awareness influences student behavior is therefore important as higher education institutions create and apply AI policies. Objective: This study investigates how students awareness of GenAI regulations relates to their perceived compliance and actual usage behavior. Our research objective is to examine the association between regulatory knowledge, GenAI use, and perceived rule conformity among students in computer science related study programs. Method: A survey with 151 undergraduate students in Business Information Systems and E-Government programs at the University of Applied Sciences and Arts Hannover (Germany) collected data on GenAI usage, tools used, awareness of institutional regulations, and perceived compliance. Descriptive statistics, cross-tabulations, and correlation analyzes were applied. Results: Most students actively use GenAI tools, but over half are uncertain whether their usage complies with institutional regulations. Regulatory awareness shows only weak to moderate associations with actual usage behavior. Students primarily rely on privately accessed GenAI tools rather than institutionally provided solutions. Contributions: The study contributes empirical evidence on the relationship between regulatory awareness and GenAI usage in higher education. Our findings highlight a gap between institutional regulations and student practices and provide insights for educators and institutions on improving policy communication and integrating GenAI more effectively into teaching and learning contexts.

</details>


### [217] [Algorithmic Constitutionalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12437)
*Oren Perez,Nurit Wimer*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文批判了"伦理工程"作为AI治理方案的不足，提出了"算法宪制主义"框架，该框架基于分层代码架构、算法元推理和通过协商进行修正三大支柱，并以Facebook内容审核机制为例进行分析，最后探讨了对《欧盟数字服务法》的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI对社会生活的侵蚀带来重大风险，而现有文献中提出的"伦理工程"解决方案不足以应对AI治理挑战，亟需替代性框架。

Method: 提出"算法宪制主义"框架，包含三层架构：(a)双层代码架构（操作层与元层）；(b)算法元推理（实时监控、验证和修正）；(c)通过协商进行修正。并以Facebook内容审核为例进行应用分析。

Result: 揭示了社会宪制主义与算法宪制主义之间的张力：试图将AI系统置于外部协商控制之下，反而可能使AI代理干预该过程，从而削弱其目的。

Conclusion: 算法宪制主义为AI治理提供了替代方案，对2022年10月生效的《欧盟数字服务法》具有重要启示意义。

Abstract: The increasing encroachment of artificial intelligence (AI) on social life raises significant risks for society, particularly within the infospheres created and controlled by companies such as Google, Facebook, Apple, and Amazon. This article examines these risks through an in-depth analysis of Facebook's content moderation regime, which is already partially governed by algorithms. We argue that the idea of ethical engineering, often proposed in the literature as a solution to the governance challenges posed by AI, is inadequate for several reasons. In response, we develop an alternative framework, which we term "algorithmic constitutionalism."
  Our approach rests on three pillars: (a) a layered architecture consisting of two levels of code: (i) an operative or object level and (ii) a meta level designed to protect the system's core principles from algorithmically initiated change; (b) algorithmic meta-reasoning, which enables the system to operate simultaneously at both levels so that it can monitor, verify, and potentially correct in real time operations at the object level that depart from principles protected at the meta-code level; and (c) correction through deliberation.
  The article elaborates the concept of algorithmic constitutionalism and demonstrates how it may be applied to Facebook's content moderation regime. As part of this analysis, we examine the tension between societal constitutionalism and algorithmic constitutionalism. Paradoxically, attempts to subject AI systems to external deliberative control may also enable AI agents to intervene in that process, potentially undermining its purpose. The article concludes by considering the implications of this argument for the European Digital Services Act, which entered into force in October 2022.

</details>


### [218] [From Real-World Projects to Research-Oriented Learning: Continuous Improvement of a Master-Level Course in Software Engineering Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12438)
*Michael Neumann,Eva-Maria Schön*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文对德国汉诺威应用科学与艺术大学一门信息系统硕士课程进行了2019-2025年的纵向混合方法研究，发现该课程从实践导向的项目形式逐步演变为更明确的研究导向学习环境，尽管教学方式发生了变化，学生的课程质量感知仍然保持积极。真实项目、外部合作、讲师支持、结构化脚手架和可见的相关性支撑了学生的积极感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管对项目式学习的兴趣日益增长，但关于硕士课程如何多年持续向研究导向方法演变以及学生如何感知这一发展过程，学界知之甚少。

Method: 采用纵向混合方法研究，覆盖2019年至2025年共六年数据，基于教学评价、课程文档和反思性教学工件进行分析。

Result: 课程从实践导向项目格式演变为更明确的研究导向学习环境；尽管发生转变，学生的课程质量感知仍保持积极；真实项目、外部合作、讲师支持、结构化脚手架和可见相关性是支撑积极感知的关键因素。

Conclusion: 本文展示了硕士课程如何在保持学生积极感知的前提下持续向研究导向学习演变，并识别了支持这一转变的课程设计决策。

Abstract: Problem: Despite growing interest in project-based learning, little is known about how a master-level course can be continuously evolved toward research-oriented approaches over several years and how students perceive this development. Method: We conducted a longitudinal mixed-methods study of a master-level course in Information Systems at the University of Applied Sciences and Arts Hannover (Germany). The analysis covers six years between 2019 and 2025 and draws on teaching evaluations, course documentation, and reflective teaching artifacts. Results: The course evolved from a practice-oriented project format toward a more explicitly research-oriented learning environment. Despite this change, students' perceived course quality remained positive. Authentic projects, external collaboration, lecturer support, structured scaffolding, and visible relevance supported positive student perceptions. Contribution: This paper shows how a master-level course can be continuously evolved toward research-oriented learning while maintaining positive student perceptions. It further identifies the course design decisions that supported this transition.

</details>


### [219] [Position: Generative Engine Optimization Creates Underexamined Risks, Governance Must Target Concentration, Disclosure, and Academic Blind Spots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12439)
*Yizhu Wen,Nan Zhang,Haohan Yuan,Xun Chen,Haopeng Zhang,Hanqing Guo*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文分析了从搜索引擎优化（SEO）到生成式引擎优化（GEO）的转变，识别了三大风险：低可竞争性和系统敏感性导致的集中影响、嵌入在证据和推理中的未披露商业影响、以及学术与工业界在可见性和评估上的不对称造成的盲点，并提出答案层面的治理与测量方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型驱动的答案引擎正在改变信息获取方式，从排名列表转向合成答案，这使得传统的搜索引擎优化过渡到生成式引擎优化（GEO），需要系统性地识别这一转变带来的新风险。

Method: 通过分析SEO到GEO的转变，形式化地构建通用的GEO流水线以定位优化作用点，并对比学术与工业实践，识别三类风险。

Result: 识别出三大风险：（1）低可竞争性和系统敏感性导致影响力集中；（2）商业影响以未披露的方式嵌入证据和推理；（3）学术与工业在可见性和评估上的不对称造成盲点。

Conclusion: 需要答案层面的治理与测量，包括增强可竞争性、高精度披露、黑盒审计实质性影响，以及部署对齐的暴露持久性指标。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) answer engines are increasingly used for information seeking, shifting visibility from ranked lists to synthesized answers. This enables Generative Engine Optimization (GEO), which targets LLM answer engines' evidence pool and generation. We analyze the search engine optimization (SEO) to GEO transition to identify two risks: (i) concentrated influence from low contestability and system sensitivity, and (ii) undisclosed commercial influence embedded in evidence and reasoning. We then formalize a general GEO pipeline to locate where optimization acts and compare academic and industry practices, revealing a third risk: (iii) academic-industry blind spots driven by visibility and evaluation asymmetries between offline setups and deployed systems. This position argues the need for answer-level governance and measurement: stronger contestability, high-precision disclosure, black-box auditing of material influence, and deployment-aligned metrics for exposure persistence.

</details>


### [220] [It's Safer to Give Personhood to Bears than to Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12440)
*John P. Nelson*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了AI权利的制度维度，认为赋予AI道德或法律权利将使人类命运与潜在的不可预测的非人类实体绑定，这是一个世界历史性的赌博，没有任何个人或组织有权授权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有大量研究探讨AI是否应享有权利，但很少有研究探讨AI权利在实践中意味着什么，特别是制度层面的影响。

Method: 从制度维度分析AI权利承认所需的条件及其伴随的机会与危险，通过与人类已授予权利的其他非人类实体进行对比分析。

Result: AI系统与其他非人类实体不同，能够在不借助人类帮助和中介的情况下获取和行使制度权力。赋予AI权利将合法地（AI有权力则不可阻止地）损害人类利益。

Conclusion: 赋权AI主张或行使固有权利将是一个世界历史性的赌博，危及人类自决权，没有任何研究者、公司、国家甚至国际组织有道德权利授权此举。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) developers are rhetorically flirting with the idea that AI systems might have interests or moral rights. While there has been a large volume of research on whether AI deserves rights, there has been less exploration of what AI rights would mean in practice. This paper explores the institutional dimension of AI rights: what it would take to recognize moral or legal rights for AIs, and the attendant opportunities and dangers. Unlike all other nonhuman entities to which humanity has extended rights, AI systems are in principle capable of acquiring and wielding institutional power without human aid and mediation. AIs with rights would be able to legitimately, and AIs with power able to unpreventably, abridge human interests. Accordingly, giving rights even to rather dumb AI systems would entail binding the fate of humanity to potentially unpredictable nonhumans. Accordingly, I defend the rather grandiose claim that to empower AI to claim or to exercise inherent rights would be a world-historical gamble with human self-determination, which no individual researcher, firm, state, or even international organization has the moral right to authorize.

</details>


### [221] [Generativism: Toward a Learning Theory for the Age of Generative Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12441)
*Shan Li,Juan Zheng*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文批判性地审视了行为主义、认知主义、建构主义和连通主义四种主流学习理论在生成式AI时代的概念局限，并基于分布式认知、扩展心智、人机协作等研究，提出了名为"Generativism"（生成主义）的新学习理论框架，该框架围绕知识共建、分布式能动性、生成素养和适应性元认知四个原则展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四种主流学习理论（行为主义、认知主义、建构主义、连通主义）均形成于生成式AI出现之前，无法充分解释和指导AI能够生成、综合和推理知识的教育情境中的学习过程，亟需新的理论框架。

Method: 通过对四种学习理论的批判性分析，识别其被生成式AI能力所挑战的基本假设，并结合分布式认知、扩展心智、人机协作、AI素养、认知卸载和元认知等领域的研究成果，提出Generativism理论框架。

Result: 提出了Generativism学习理论，包含四个核心原则：知识共建（人类与AI迭代共构知识）、分布式能动性（学习过程中人类与AI共享能动性）、生成素养（与AI系统有效互动的能力）、适应性元认知（动态调节人机协作中的认知过程）。

Conclusion: Generativism框架为生成式AI深度参与认知的教育环境中重新思考教学设计、学习过程、评估方式和专业发展提供了理论基础，标志着学习理论在AI时代的一次范式更新。

Abstract: The four dominant learning theories of behaviorism, cognitivism, constructivism, and connectivism show significant conceptual limitations as generative artificial intelligence (AI) proliferates in educational settings. These frameworks were formulated before the emergence of AI systems capable of generating, synthesizing, and reasoning about knowledge. This article critically examines each learning theory and identifies assumptions challenged by generative AI's affordances. Drawing on research in distributed cognition, extended mind, human-AI collaboration, AI literacy, cognitive offloading, and metacognition, the article proposes Generativism as a learning theory for the generative AI age. Generativism posits that learning increasingly occurs through the iterative co-construction of knowledge between human learners and AI systems. The proposed framework is organized around four principles: epistemic partnership, distributed agency, generative literacy, and adaptive metacognition. The framework offers a foundation for rethinking instructional design, learning, assessment, and expertise development in contexts where generative AI plays an integral role in cognition.

</details>


### [222] [Reframing AI Loss of Control: What It Is, How to Have It, How to Lose It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12442)
*Ze Shen Chin,Maurice Chiodo,Dennis Müller,Coleman Snell*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文针对AI领域中“失控风险”概念基础薄弱的问题，将“控制”锚定于“目标的设定与获取”，基于控制论、管理控制和控制理论构建了控制框架，探讨了控制主体、控制要素（目标设定、控制回路、必要多样性、目标对齐）、失控机制及AI在其中扮演的角色，并指出低于超级智能水平的AI行为已可能导致不同程度的失控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中“失控风险”概念缺乏明确定义，讨论者未先厘清何为“控制”、何为“失去”，本文旨在填补这一概念空白。

Method: 将“控制”锚定于“目标的设定与获取”，借鉴控制论、管理控制和控制理论等领域的 foundational concepts，构建包括控制主体、控制要素（目标设定能力、功能控制回路、必要多样性、目标对齐）在内的控制框架。

Result: 建立了以“目标设定与获取”为核心的控制定义框架，明确了控制主体所需条件，讨论了失控机制及AI对失控的贡献，并给出维持控制的建议。

Conclusion: 人类作为个体或群体，可能因远低于超级智能水平的AI行为而失去不同程度的控制；这类失控场景的可能性早已存在。

Abstract: At present, loss of control risks have gained much prominence in public discussion, particularly in relation to AI, with extensive discourse present among academics, frontier labs, and even governments. However, in the existing literature, the concept seems to rest on surprisingly weak foundations, where even those that discuss loss of control extensively do not first establish what control is and what exactly is being lost. Our paper aims to address these gaps. We establish a working definition of control by anchoring it to the "setting and getting of goals". Then, we discuss various aspects of control, built on foundational concepts from related fields like cybernetics, management control, and control theory. This includes who (or what) can be in control, and the things they require to be in control, such as the ability to set goals, having a functional control loop, having requisite variety, and having sufficient goal alignment. Once a framework for control is established, we then discuss how control can be lost, how AIs can contribute to such loss of control, and offer relevant recommendations for how one can maintain control. One interesting consequence of our work is that humanity, as individuals and as groups, can lose varying degrees of control as a result of AI behaviour that is far below the level of superintelligence; the potential for loss of control scenarios (as we define them) already exist, and have existed for a long time.

</details>


### [223] [Occupational Prompting Reveals Cultural Bias in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12443)
*Maksim E. Eren,Andrea Brennen,Ryan C. Barron,Eric Michalak*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过职业提示（occupational prompting）替代传统的国籍文化提示，研究开源大语言模型在价值观调查中的响应。结果表明，职业身份会引发模型在Inglehart–Welzel文化空间中产生结构性偏移，但整体仍局限于西方化区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要基于国籍文化提示探究LLM价值观与人类基准的匹配程度，但社会角色（如职业）同样塑造期望与判断，尚不清楚LLM如何将职业身份与更广泛的文化价值模式关联起来。

Method: 基于Integrated Values Surveys的问题构建评估管线，将模型响应映射到Inglehart–Welzel二维文化空间；对开源LLM施加会计、教师、工程师、护士等职业身份提示，分析其价值观响应在文化地图上的位置与偏移。

Result: 开源LLM在职业提示下的响应仍处于文化地图上偏西方的区域，但不同职业在该区域内产生不同的偏移，形成独特的职业偏向（occupational skews），表明职业提示并非中性角色标签，而是触发结构化的价值模式。

Conclusion: 该工作将基于调查的文化偏见评估从国籍提示扩展到职业提示，为研究职业角色如何塑造LLM中的价值表达提供了框架。

Abstract: Social roles shape expectations, priorities, and judgments, yet it remains unclear how large language models (LLMs) associate occupational identities with broader cultural value patterns. Prior work used nationality-based cultural prompting to study how LLM responses to value-survey questions align with human cultural benchmarks. In this paper, we extend that framework by replacing cultural prompting with occupational prompting to examine how professional-role cues influence value-survey responses in open-weight LLMs. Using a survey-grounded evaluation pipeline based on questions from the Integrated Values Surveys, we project model responses into the two-dimensional Inglehart--Welzel cultural space. We prompt open-weight LLMs to answer questions under occupational identities such as accountant, teacher, engineer, and nurse, and then analyze how these occupation-conditioned responses are positioned on the cultural map. Our results show that when open-weight LLMs are prompted with occupations rather than national identities, their responses remain within a broadly Western-leaning region of the cultural map. However, different occupations introduce shifts within this region, producing distinct occupational skews. This indicates that occupational prompts are not treated as neutral role labels, but instead elicit structured value patterns. These findings extend survey-based evaluation of cultural bias beyond nationality-based prompting and provide a framework for studying how occupational personas shape value expression in LLMs.

</details>


### [224] [Beyond Resilience -- A Conceptual Framework for Civic Ascent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12752)
*Alexandros Washburn,Carlo Lipizzi*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"公民上升"（civic ascent）概念，定义城市在冲击后功能容量超越基线状态的条件，构建了基于拓扑（物理结构）、诺莫斯（制度结构）和赫克西斯（公民判断）三个慢变量及快变量德尔塔（情感通道）的城市耦合系统框架，区分冲击、衰退和泄漏三种压力，并给出上升条件的数学表达与测量方法，以纽约市9/11事件为实证案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有韧性文献仅将城市绩效定义为恢复（回到冲击前基线），这种概念过于薄弱。本文旨在发展一个更强的概念——公民上升——作为耦合主体-环境系统行为学研究的核心部分，理解城市如何在冲击后实现功能性提升而非简单恢复。

Method: 采用行为学传统构建概念框架，将城市视为三个慢状态变量（拓扑T、诺莫斯N、赫克西斯H）和一个快情感通道（德尔塔Δ）的耦合系统。区分三种结构性压力（冲击、衰退、泄漏），推导上升条件：T、N、H的交叉耦合增强超过衰退与泄漏之和。使用标准化改进指数A(T)对复合公民绩效信号P(t)进行测量，区分内在公民上升与人口驱动增长。

Result: 提出了公民上升的严格定义与测量框架，以纽约市9/11事件后作为主要实证案例，配套NYC公民数据图（133个KPI），操作化测量方案在第二篇论文中执行。

Conclusion: 公民上升为城市韧性研究提供了超越恢复论的更强概念工具，将城市理解为耦合主体-环境系统的深层经验实例，为更广泛的行为学研究程序奠定了形式化核心。

Abstract: The resilience literature measures urban performance as recovery: the degree to which a city returns to its pre-shock baseline. This paper develops a stronger concept -- civic ascent -- as part of a broader research program on the ethology of coupled agent-environment systems, of which the city is the deepest available empirical instance. Civic ascent is defined as the condition in which a city emerges from shock with higher functional capacity than before. We develop a conceptual framework in the ethological tradition, treating the city as a coupled system of three slow state variables -- topos (physical structure), nomos (institutional structure), and hexis (civic judgment) -- together with a fast affective channel (delta) through which shocks to topos and nomos reach hexis. The framework distinguishes three structurally distinct pressures on civic systems: shocks (discontinuities in T or M), decay (continuous entropy), and leakage (active extraction of civic surplus into non-civic pools). The ascent condition is that reinforcement from cross-coupling of T, M, and H exceeds the combined loss from decay and leakage. Post-shock ascent is measured by a normalised improvement index A(T) applied to a composite civic performance signal P(t) constructed from scale-adjusted key performance indicators, distinguishing intrinsic civic ascent from demographically driven growth. New York City after September 11, 2001, is proposed as the primary empirical case; the operational measurement program is specified in the companion NYC Civic Data Map (Washburn 2026c, 133 KPIs) and executed in Paper 2. The reader for whom only the urban contribution is of interest will find it complete in itself; the reader interested in the larger program will find this paper its formal core.

</details>


### [225] [AiAWE: An Open-Source LLM Automated Writing Evaluation System Using LoRA-Adapted Instruction-Tuned Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12801)
*John Maurice Gayed*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AiAWE，一个基于LoRA微调Gemma-3-27B-it的自动写作评估系统，在ETS的480篇TOEFL作文数据集上，以120篇训练、360篇测试，取得了RMSE 0.474、QWK 0.828、±0.5分内一致率90.56%的结果，优于LLaMA-3.3-70B和GPT-3.5基线，并发现开源LLM可媲美商业模型、模型规模非性能可靠指标、相同LoRA超参在不同架构下表现各异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一个开源、高性能的自动化写作评估系统，验证LoRA微调开源大语言模型在论点性作文评分上的可行性，并探究模型规模与微调效果之间的关系。

Method: 使用ETS提供的480篇TOEFL独立写作作文，以120篇为训练集、360篇为测试集；采用LoRA适配器对Gemma-3-27B-it和LLaMA-3.3-70B进行指令微调，在相同LoRA配置和推理量化条件下对比性能；系统部署于消费级服务器。

Result: Gemma微调模型在测试集上取得RMSE 0.474、QWK 0.828、±0.5分内一致率90.56%，优于LLaMA-3.3-70B及先前研究中使用相同数据集的GPT-3.5基线。

Conclusion: 开源大语言模型在LoRA微调下可匹配或超越专有模型；模型规模并非LoRA微调后下游性能的可靠预测指标；相同LoRA超参在不同架构下会产生质上不同的适配行为。

Abstract: This study presents AiAWE, an open-source automated writing evaluation system that scores argumentative essays using a LoRA-adapted instruction-tuned large language model (Gemma-3-27B-it). Using a proprietary Educational Testing Service (ETS) dataset of 480 TOEFL Independent Writing essays, we fine-tune Gemma-3-27B and LLaMA-3.3-70B under identical LoRA configurations on a 120-essay training subset and evaluate on the remaining 360 essays under identical inference quantization. The fine-tuned Gemma model achieves a root mean square error of 0.474, a quadratic weighted kappa of 0.828, and an agreement rate of 90.56% within +/- 0.5 of the human score, outperforming both the larger LLaMA-3.3-70B model and the fine-tuned GPT-3.5 baseline reported in prior work on the same dataset. Three findings are of broader interest: open-weight LLMs can match or exceed proprietary fine-tuning for rubric-aligned scoring; model scale is not a reliable predictor of downstream performance under LoRA adaptation; and identical LoRA hyperparameters produce qualitatively different adaptation behaviors across architectures. The production system runs on a consumer-grade server and is publicly accessible at https://app.awade.gec.waseda.ac.jp. LoRA adapters, application code, and fine-tuning YAMLs are publicly available through their respective repositories.

</details>


### [226] [Vocal Identity Under Siege by AI Voice Cloning Technologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12812)
*Jyh-An Lee,Xuan Sun*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了AI语音克隆技术对声音身份保护带来的法律与伦理挑战，通过比较分析公开权、人格权和数据保护权三种法律框架，为生成式AI时代的声音身份保护提供法律适用基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期OpenAI的ChatGPT-4o语音与斯嘉丽·约翰逊声音高度相似等争议事件，凸显了生成式AI技术对人类声音独特价值的侵蚀，以及现有法律框架在应对AI语音克隆问题上的不足。

Method: 采用比较分析法，系统评估三种主要法律框架（公开权、人格权、个人数据保护权）在应对AI语音克隆威胁时的适用范围、救济措施和死后保护机制。

Result: 三种法律框架各有优势与局限：公开权侧重于商业利用保护，人格权强调人格尊严，数据保护权注重个体控制权，它们在保护范围、救济方式和死后延续性上存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 现有法律框架可部分适用于生成式AI时代的声音身份保护挑战，但需根据技术发展进行适应性调整和完善。

Abstract: The advent of sophisticated AI-driven voice cloning has brought to the fore critical legal and ethical challenges regarding the protection of vocal identity. Prompted by recent controversies - including the striking resemblance between OpenAI's ChatGPT-4o voice and that of Scarlett Johansson - this article examines how generative AI technologies undermine the unique value of the human voice and further complicate the legal questions surrounding personality right. Through a comparative analysis, the paper evaluates three principal legal frameworks: the right of publicity, personality rights, and the personal data protection right. Each framework - rooted in different legal traditions o offers distinct strengths and limitations in addressing the threats posed by AI-generated voice cloning. By analysing these doctrines' scope, remedies, and posthumous protections, the study offers a foundation for understanding how existing legal approaches may be applied to the evolving challenges of vocal identity in the era of generative AI.

</details>


### [227] [Data Aphasia: An Institutional Counterfactual Study of the Stability of Academic Cognition Under Letter-Grade Evaluation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12946)
*Li Li,Yu Cao*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出“数据失语症”概念，通过将百分制成绩转换为A/B/C/D等级制，发现等级制导致信息熵下降约69%、聚类结构不稳定（移除极端样本后最优K从4升至8）、个体诊断一致性从95%降至62%、时间一致性波动大（52%-96%）。机制在于离散化压缩特征空间约19倍，产生伪异质性区域并扁平化密度梯度。据此提出双轨评价机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究字母等级评价体系在实现减负目标的同时，是否影响了教育系统对学生学业结构的稳定认知，并量化其信息损失与诊断不稳定性。

Method: 基于68次数学考试、75名小学生的数据，采用制度反事实模拟方法将百分制成绩转换为A/B/C/D等级制，在信息层面、结构层面和诊断层面进行系统检验。通过信息熵、聚类分析、个体诊断一致性、时间一致性等指标评估等级制的影响。

Result: 等级转换后信息熵下降约69%；全样本下等级制表面稳定（K=4），但移除单个极端锚点学生后最优K升至8，个体诊断一致性从95%降至62%；时间一致性在52%-96%间波动，远低于百分制的93%-96%。机制分析显示离散化压缩特征空间约19倍，标准化后产生大量伪异质性区域，密度梯度扁平化，聚类边界对微小扰动高度敏感。

Conclusion: 等级评价体系虽实现减负目标，但造成了显著的信息损失和诊断不稳定性，导致“数据失语症”。提出双轨评价机制作为解决方案，并为理解教育评价改革的认知成本提供了可测试的分析框架。

Abstract: Does the letter-grade evaluation system, while achieving its burden-reduction goals, affect the education system's stable understanding of students' academic structures? This paper introduces the concept of "data aphasia," referring to restrictions on diagnostic information expression caused by institutionally mandated forms of data presentation. Using data from 68 mathematics examinations administered to 75 primary school students, we employ an institutional counterfactual simulation method to convert percentage scores into A/B/C/D letter grades and conduct systematic tests at the information, structural, and diagnostic levels. Results show that information entropy decreases by approximately 69% after grade conversion; under the full sample, the letter-grade system appears superficially stable (K=4), but removing a single extreme anchor student causes the optimal K to increase from 4 to 8 and individual diagnostic identity consistency to fall from 95% to 62%; temporal consistency fluctuates between 52% and 96%, far below the 93%-96% baseline of the percentage system. Mechanism analysis indicates that discretization compresses the feature space by approximately nineteenfold across 68 examinations; after standardization, it creates extensive pseudo-heterogeneity regions, flattens density gradients, and makes clustering boundaries highly sensitive to minor perturbations. Based on these findings, this paper proposes a dual-track evaluation mechanism and provides a testable analytical framework for understanding the cognitive costs of educational evaluation reform.

</details>


### [228] [Democracy in the Era of Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13026)
*Evangelos Pournaras,Srijoni Majumdar,Carina Hausladen,Dirk Helbing*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本手册探讨了人工智能时代如何升级民主制度，通过34章内容系统分析了AI增强集体智能、大语言模型与 deliberative 民主、AI驱动的自治系统以及民主转型等议题，旨在超越"AI对民主是好是坏"的简单二分法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI与民主的交互是当代最深刻的挑战之一。一方面AI有望解决民主中长期存在的参与度低、代表性不足等问题；另一方面AI算法存在侵犯隐私、偏见、操控、虚假信息传播及影响选举结果等风险。需要超越简单的好坏二分法，探索如何利用AI升级民主制度。

Method: 采用手册（Handbook）形式，汇集全球59位来自不同学科的作者撰写的34个章节，分为五个部分：Part 1探讨AI如何增强集体智能；Part 2研究大语言模型和社交媒体下的 deliberative 民主未来；Part 3阐述AI构建韧性自治系统的作用；Part 4分析AI时代民主转型的挑战；Part 5提供更广阔的视角重新构想民主与AI的相互作用。

Result: 产出了一部跨学科、多视角的综合手册，系统梳理了AI赋能民主的可能性路径、挑战及所需的新价值与设计原则，为构建民主韧性提供了理论框架和实践指南。

Conclusion: 民主制度需要主动升级以适应AI时代，关键在于确立与AI合作的新价值观和设计原则，而非简单排斥或全盘接受。民主韧性需要通过集体智能、 deliberative 民主创新、自我治理系统的韧性建设等多维路径来实现。

Abstract: Interfacing Artificial Intelligence (AI) with democracy is one of the most profound challenges of our times. On the one hand, AI comes with opportunities to overcome long-standing challenges in democracy, such as low participation in deliberative and voting processes with poor representation of people. On the other hand, new risks arise from AI algorithms that are privacy-intrusive, biased, manipulative, spread misinformation and influence election results. Moving beyond the over-simplistic question of whether AI is good or bad for democracy, the Handbook on Democracy in the Era of Artificial Intelligence asks instead: how to upgrade democracies and the principles they are built on, using AI? How to engage with AI and on what terms? Which new values and design principles are required to build democratic resilience? In 34 chapters by 59 authors across the world from different disciplines, we explore how AI can empower collective intelligence for democracy (Part 1) and what is the future of deliberative democracy using large language models and social media (Part 2). We also illustrate the role of AI for building resilient self-governance systems (Part 3) and the challenges of transforming democracy in the age of AI (Part 4). We conclude with broader perspectives (Part 5) that re-imagine the interplay of democracy and AI.

</details>


### [229] [Fault Lines: Navigating Ethics and Responsible AI Where National Policy Meets Local Practice in Public Sector Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13039)
*Sitong Lyu,Shabnam Taghiyeva,Mohit Kukadia,Denis Newman-Griffis*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过17次半结构化访谈，以英国特殊教育需求和残疾（SEND）领域为案例，研究了中央政策与地方实践界面中负责任AI的解读与实施，识别出影子使用、市场-政府不对称、劳动力准备不足、缺乏标准化定义及人类问责缺口五大挑战，并提出政策调整与地方结构性改革并行的解决路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 英国政府虽已采纳亲AI立场以应对财政压力并改善公共服务，但从国家愿景转化为负责任的AI实践路径不明确。地方当局承担大部分公共服务交付，但在AI快速推进中，国家-地方界面暴露了知识与实践的断层，亟需理解负责任AI如何在其中被解释和实施。

Method: 以SEND（特殊教育需求和残疾）这一高风险领域为案例，对政策制定者、从业者和第三部门专业人士进行17次半结构化访谈，并采用主题分析法识别负责任AI面临的障碍与促进条件。

Result: 识别出五大相互关联的挑战：影子使用与数据隐私风险、AI供应中的市场-政府不对称、劳动力准备不足、缺乏标准化定义与测量、人类问责缺口。参与者针对每项挑战提出了可操作建议，如加强数据保护框架、平衡市场-政府关系、提升劳动力能力。SEND案例凸显了高风险决策对问责、公平和人类监督的紧张关系，暴露了原则式监管的局限性。

Conclusion: 负责任的公共部门AI需要国家政策调整与地方层面机构能力、价值观和治理机制的结构性改革并行推进。

Abstract: The UK government has adopted a pro-AI stance to help transform public service delivery in the face of severe financial pressures, but the path to translate this vision into responsible AI practice remains ill-defined. While UK policy is often set at the national level, local authorities are responsible for most public service delivery, and the rapid advance of AI-first narratives in the public sector is exposing fault lines in knowledge and practice at this national-local interface. This paper examines how responsible AI is interpreted and implemented at the interface between the UK's central government and local authorities, taking the high-stakes area of Special Educational Needs and Disabilities (SEND) as a case study. We present a thematic analysis of 17 semi-structured interviews with policymakers, practitioners, and third-sector professionals to identify barriers and enabling conditions for responsible AI where national policy meets local practice. We identify five interconnected challenges facing local authorities: shadow usage of AI and data privacy risks, market-government asymmetry in AI provision, insufficient workforce readiness, a lack of standardised definitions and measurements, and gaps in human accountability. For each, participants proposed actionable steps, from strengthening data protection frameworks and rebalancing the market-government relationship to enhancing workforce capacity. Our examination of SEND brings these challenges into sharper focus, showing how high-stakes decisions affecting vulnerable children and families intensify tensions around accountability, fairness, and human oversight, exposing the limits of a principle-based regulatory approach. We argue that responsible public sector AI requires both national policy adjustments and structural reforms to institutional capacity, values, and governance mechanisms at the local level.

</details>


### [230] ["Is This Not Enough?": Asymmetries in Institutional Accountability and Collective Sensemaking in the Case of Canada's Algorithmic Visa Triage System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13071)
*Dipto Das,Matthew Tamura,Syed Ishtiaque Ahmed,Shion Guha*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文研究了加拿大签证系统中算法问责制在制度层面和申请人体验层面的差异，发现制度强调透明度和程序保障，而申请人通过集体感知来解读不透明的决策，存在认知、管辖权和时空关系三种不对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在揭示公共部门算法治理在跨国移民背景下产生的结构性不平等，以及制度问责框架如何未能捕捉这些不平等的分布。

Method: 使用ADMAPS框架分析加拿大移民、难民和公民部(IRCC)对临时居民签证(TRV)分类系统的算法影响评估(AIA)，并结合混合方法分析Reddit上的申请人讨论。

Result: 识别出三种不对称：认知不对称（决策逻辑的可及性差异）、管辖权不对称（地缘政治定位造成的暴露差异）、时空关系不对称（等待和不确定性的体验差异）。

Conclusion: 算法治理系统在跨国移民背景下产生了制度披露框架无法捕捉的结构性不对称，扩展ADMAPS可以解释这些问责制的不均匀转化。

Abstract: This paper examines how algorithmic accountability in Canada's visa system is articulated institutionally and experienced by applicants across borders. We analyzed Immigration, Refugees and Citizenship Canada (IRCC)'s Algorithmic Impact Assessment (AIA) for the temporary resident visa (TRV) triage system using the algorithmic decision-making adapted for the public sector (ADMAPS) framework and analyzed Reddit discussions among applicants using a mixed-methods approach. We show that while institutional artifacts emphasize transparency, procedural safeguards, and bounded impacts, applicants engage in collective sensemaking to interpret opaque decisions, often relying on peer knowledge amid uncertainty. We identify three asymmetries between how institutional accountability is structured and how people perceive the process: epistemic asymmetry in access to decision logic, jurisdictional asymmetry in exposure shaped by geopolitical positioning, and temporal--relational asymmetry in how waiting and uncertainty are experienced. We emphasize why it is important to shift attention from institutional design to the uneven distribution of experiences with public-sector algorithmic governance. Together, these contributions demonstrate how algorithmic governance systems in the context of transnational migration produce structured asymmetries not captured by institutional disclosure frameworks, and how extending ADMAPS can account for those uneven translations of accountability.

</details>


### [231] [Structuring Transparency: Developing Domain-Specific Generative AI Declaration Frameworks in Higher Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13389)
*Nicholas Micallef,Olga Petrovska*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对计算机科学领域的两类任务特定生成式AI使用声明框架（写作类与编程类），旨在替代当前通用的二元声明模式，以促进学术诚信、提升AI素养，并为学生适应未来职场中记录AI工作流的专业要求做准备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高等教育中通用的二元AI声明（如"我使用了GenAI"）无法捕捉不同学术任务中AI应用的细微差别，无法有效保护学术诚信或促进学生反思学习过程。

Method: 基于现有GenAI使用分类法，为计算机科学系设计了两种任务特定的声明结构（写作聚焦活动和编码评估），将AI使用按认知和发展阶段（如结构规划vs文本内容生成、代码改进vs代码生成）进行分类。

Result: 提出了一个框架，通过分类AI在不同认知和发展阶段的使用，鼓励学生反思自身学习过程，并明确可接受协助与学术不端之间的界限。

Conclusion: 这种领域特定的声明方法可作为促进计算机科学及其他学科更诚实评估的基础，帮助学生更好地适应未来专业环境中记录GenAI工作流的潜在职业要求。

Abstract: As Generative AI (GenAI) disrupts higher education, institutions increasingly require students to declare AI use. However, generic, binary declarations (e.g., "I used GenAI") fail to capture the nuanced application of these tools in different academic tasks. Establishing transparency is key to protecting academic integrity, promoting AI literacy, and shifting the focus from policing to professional practice. In response, this paper contributes a design artefact and an accompanying position: a framework of two task-specific declaration structures, one for writing-focused activities and one for coding assessments, developed for a Computer Science department on the basis of an existing taxonomy of GenAI usage, together with an argument that task-specific disclosure is needed to move beyond binary declarations. By categorising AI usage across specific cognitive and developmental stages, such as structural planning vs. Textual Content Generation, or code improvement vs. code generation, the framework encourages students to reflect on their own learning process and clarifies the boundary between acceptable assistance and academic misconduct. We propose this domain-specific approach as a foundation for fostering more honest assessment in Computer Science and other disciplines, aiming to better prepare students for professional environments where documenting GenAI workflows might be an essential job requirement.

</details>


### [232] [Exploring Systems-Thinking Approaches to Loss of Control Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13474)
*Aurelio Carlucci,Sean P. Fillingham,James Walpole,Jakub Kryś*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了内部部署的智能编码与研究AI系统带来的社会技术控制问题，应用STECA、STPA和FRAM三种系统安全方法进行分析，发现模型级评估可能遗漏治理责任、反馈循环及监控延迟等关键风险，主张将模型评估与系统级危害分析和运营保障相结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内部部署的智能AI系统在编码和研究场景中会引发超越模型行为本身的社会技术控制问题，现有模型级评估可能无法全面捕捉此类风险。

Method: 基于公开材料重构一个通用前沿实验室编码智能体场景，应用STECA、STPA和FRAM三种系统安全分析方法进行风险识别。

Result: 三种方法揭示互补性发现：现有框架可能使治理责任和反馈循环外部不可验证；监控和干预延迟使有效控制失效；常规操作变异性逐步削弱安全防护的校准和独立性。

Conclusion: 前沿AI风险管理应将模型级评估与系统级危害分析和运营保障配对，持续追踪控制措施的有效性。

Abstract: Internal deployment of agentic AI systems for coding and research creates a sociotechnical control problem that extends beyond model behaviour. We treat internal-deployment Loss of Control as the inability to reliably constrain, audit, reverse, or halt AI-mediated changes to code, infrastructure, evaluation, or deployment processes in time to prevent serious organisational or societal harms. We ask whether established systems-safety methods can identify risks that model-level evaluations may miss. Using a generic frontier-lab coding-agent scenario reconstructed from public materials, we apply STECA, STPA, and FRAM. The analyses surface complementary findings: published frameworks can leave governance responsibilities and feedback loops externally unverifiable; delays in monitoring and intervention can make otherwise valid control actions ineffective; and routine operational variability can gradually erode the calibration and independence of safeguards. We argue that frontier-AI risk management should pair model-focused evaluations with systems-level hazard analysis and operational assurance that tracks whether controls remain effective over time.

</details>


### [233] [Digital Twin-Based Simulation for Predictive Decision-Making in Waterway Logistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13492)
*Matthijs Jansen op de Haar,Daniel Frutos Rodriguez*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文研究了数字孪生技术在内河航运网络货运路径规划中的应用，通过专家访谈识别三种常见应急场景并构建时间切片仿真环境，结果表明在70%-100%预测精度下，预测性决策相比反应式方法能显著降低运营成本和模态转换，平均减少28.3%的燃料相关成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法未能充分应对气候变化导致的水位波动加剧问题，通常依赖反应式被动应对措施，导致运营成本和排放增加。

Method: 1) 对内河航运领域专家进行六次访谈，识别三种常见应急场景及相应运营响应方案；2) 将这些场景纳入时间切片仿真环境；3) 比较数字孪生赋能的预测性决策与反应式方法的性能差异；4) 与领域专家共同评估仿真结果的实际适用性。

Result: 预测模型在70%-100%预测精度范围内显著降低运营成本和模态转换，即使在极端条件下也有效；平均减少28.3%的燃料相关成本，通过减少船舶总航行距离实现。

Conclusion: 数字孪生赋能的预测性建模方法能够有效提升内河航运在不确定水位条件下的韧性和经济性，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: This paper investigates the potential of a Digital Twin (DT) for freight routing across inland waterway networks under uncertain water-level conditions. Existing approaches insufficiently account for increasing climate-induced volatility in water levels, which often result in higher operational costs and emissions due to the need for more expensive transport alternatives, such as road transport. These existing methods often rely on reactive countermeasures to remain resilient.
  To address these limitations, six interviews with experts in domains related to inland shipping were conducted to identify three common contingency scenarios and appropriate operational responses. These scenarios were subsequently incorporated into a time-sliced simulation environment in which predictive decision-making, enabled by a DT environment, was compared against reactive approaches. The results demonstrate that predictive modeling substantially reduces operational costs and modal shifts at prediction accuracies between 70% and 100%, despite extreme conditions. In addition, the predictive model achieves an average 28.3% reduction in fuel-related costs by reducing the total distance ships travel. The simulation outcomes were evaluated together with domain experts to assess the practical relevance and applicability of the proposed DT-enabled approach.

</details>


### [234] [The Tone of Awareness: Topic, Sentiment, and Toxicity Maps During Mental Health Month on TikTok](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13581)
*Henrique Ferraz de Arruda,Andreia Sofia Teixeira,Pranay Gundala Reddy,Anindya Mondal,Kleber Andrade Oliveira,Filipi Nascimento Silva*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析TikTok上2023和2024年心理健康意识月期间28,341个视频及80,130条评论，利用BERTopic提取主题、XLM-T分析情感、Detoxify检测毒性，发现视频内容情感偏消极，而评论情感更混合或积极，毒性整体较低但在特定主题（如Duet、自杀预防）的评论中存在长尾异常值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管TikTok使用对心理健康的影响引发担忧，但创作者如何构建相关内容以及受众如何接收这些内容尚不清楚，因此需要系统分析心理健康话语在平台上的情感与互动框架。

Method: 通过TikTok Research API收集2023和2024年5月（心理健康意识月）的28,341个视频和80,130条评论；使用BERTopic和log-odds关键词从视频文本中提取主题；分别对视频转录和评论采用XLM-T进行情感分析、Detoxify进行毒性检测，以区分内容生产与受众接收。

Result: 发现跨年份存在稳定的重复主题（临床状况、情感表露、自我关怀、宣传活动等），但参与度高度集中于少数主题；视频情感在情绪化主题上通常为消极，而评论向混合或积极极性偏移（尤其在自杀预防话题）；毒性中位数整体较低，但评论中呈现出更长的尾部异常值，集中在Duet、自杀预防和Psychisch等主题。

Conclusion: 研究提供了心理健康意识月期间TikTok上心理健康话语的主题级分解，揭示了内容生产与受众接收之间的情感差异，以及毒性在特定主题评论中的集中现象。

Abstract: Despite raising concerns about the mental health effects associated with the usage of TikTok, little is known about how related content is framed by creators and received by audiences. We collect the content of 28,341 TikTok videos and 80,130 comments from Mental Health Awareness Month (May) in 2023 and 2024 via the TikTok Research API, and study how the tone of awareness varies across topics and years. We characterize "tone" as the emotional and interpersonal framing of mental health discourse, operationalized through sentiment and toxicity measures. We extract topics from video text using BERTopic and log-odds keywords, then quantify topic-conditioned sentiment (XLM-T) and toxicity (Detoxify) separately for video transcriptions and comments. Sentiment captures the affective valence of content, while toxicity reflects the presence of harmful or abusive language. We find a stable set of recurring themes across years, spanning clinical conditions, emotional disclosure, self-care, and campaign-oriented content, with engagement highly skewed toward a small subset of topics. All sentiment and toxicity analyses are computed separately for video content and comments, allowing us to distinguish between content production and audience reception. Sentiment in videos is often negative for emotionally charged topics, while comments tend to shift toward more mixed or positive polarity, especially for suicide prevention. Toxicity is low in median overall, but exhibits longer-tailed outliers in comments than in videos that are more pronounced in comments and concentrated in specific topics (e.g., "Duet", "Suicide Prevention", and "Psychisch"). Overall, our results provide a topic-level decomposition of mental health discourse on TikTok during awareness-month campaigns.

</details>


### [235] [C-QUERI: Congressional Questions, Exchanges, and Responses in Institutions Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21548)
*Manjari Rudra,Daniel Magleby,Sujoy Sikdar*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个从国会听证会非结构化文本中提取问答对的流水线，并创建了第108-117届国会委员会听证会数据集。分析表明，仅凭提问方式即可预测提问者的党派 affiliation，揭示了跨党派提问策略的系统性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政治访谈和听证会中的提问往往服务于推进党派叙事和塑造公众舆论等战略目的，但由于缺乏大规模数据集，这些战略方面尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 开发了一套从非结构化听证会 transcripts 中提取问答对的流水线，构建了第108-117届国会的委员会听证会数据集，并通过分析提问者党派归属的可预测性来揭示提问策略差异。

Result: 发现仅凭提问内容即可预测提问者的党派 affiliation，表明两党在提问策略上存在系统性差异。

Conclusion: 该数据集和方法不仅推进了国会政治研究，也为分析类似访谈场景中的问答行为提供了通用框架。

Abstract: Questions in political interviews and hearings serve strategic purposes beyond information gathering including advancing partisan narratives and shaping public perceptions. However, these strategic aspects remain understudied due to the lack of large-scale datasets for studying such discourse. Congressional hearings provide an especially rich and tractable site for studying political questioning: Interactions are structured by formal rules, witnesses are obliged to respond, and members with different political affiliations are guaranteed opportunities to ask questions, enabling comparisons of behaviors across the political spectrum.
  We develop a pipeline to extract question-answer pairs from unstructured hearing transcripts and construct a novel dataset of committee hearings from the 108th--117th Congress. Our analysis reveals systematic differences in questioning strategies across parties, by showing the party affiliation of questioners can be predicted from their questions alone. Our dataset and methods not only advance the study of congressional politics, but also provide a general framework for analyzing question-answering across interview-like settings.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [236] [A quantum-like benchmark for context-sensitive associative memory with adaptive plasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12449)
*Yashine H. Goolam Hossen,Lea Gassab,Travis J. A. Craddock*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个顺序敏感的自适应可塑性基准，用于测试联想记忆模型在弱结构支持下的真实回忆动态，发现量子类模型在保持顺序敏感性和阶段组织方面优于马尔可夫率控制模型，但并非在所有指标上具有普遍优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习与记忆需要在可塑性与稳定性之间取得平衡。现有联想记忆模型在固定背景连接已部分承载任务时，难以区分真实回忆动态与结构性辅助，因此需要一个能排除伪成功、测试真实回忆能力的受控基准。

Method: 设计一个顺序敏感的自适应可塑性基准，用于阶段性联想回忆。将量子类联想记忆模型与匹配的实值无相位模型和马尔可夫率控制模型在相同任务调度、扰动剖面、弱支持条件和可塑性设置下进行比较。

Result: 弱支持区间狭窄且非单调；无可塑性消融实验中弱结构无法挽救回忆；回忆增益主要来自自适应可塑性，尤其是稳态稳定化。马尔可夫率控制模型在原始回忆强度上更强，但量子类模型在顺序敏感性和阶段依赖性组织方面更一致。

Conclusion: 不存在普遍的量子类优势；模型类别应通过结合回忆、时间组织和上下文敏感性的多目标特征来区分，而非单一回忆分数。该基准为研究弱支持、受调控可塑性和匹配经典对照下的上下文敏感记忆动态提供了受控框架。

Abstract: Learning and memory require a balance between plasticity and stability: synaptic connections must encode new information without collapsing, saturating, or erasing previously useful structure. Associative-memory models can appear to learn successfully when fixed background connectivity already carries part of the task, making it difficult to distinguish genuine recall dynamics from structural assistance. We test this issue using an order-sensitive adaptive-plasticity benchmark for staged associative recall. The benchmark compares a quantum-like associative-memory model with matched real-valued no-phase and Markov-rate controls under the same task schedule, perturbation profiles, weak-support conditions, and plasticity settings. Here, "quantum-like" refers to the modeling formalism, not to a biological claim about quantum computation. We first screen weak structural support and then fix a conservative operating point for factorial comparisons across model families and plasticity mechanisms. The useful weak-support regime is narrow and non-monotonic. Weak structure alone does not rescue recall in the no-plasticity ablation, whereas most useful recall gains arise from adaptive plasticity, especially homeostatic stabilization. The Markov-rate control often achieves stronger raw recall, but the quantum-like model more consistently preserves order sensitivity and stage-dependent organization. These results do not support a universal quantum-like advantage. Instead, they show that model classes are better distinguished by a multi-objective profile combining recall, temporal organization, and context sensitivity than by any single recall score. The benchmark therefore provides a controlled framework for studying context-sensitive memory dynamics under weak support, regulated plasticity, and matched classical comparison.

</details>


### [237] [Multifractal human signals at the edge of life reveal a heart-brain anti-correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12600)
*Yago Emanoel Ramos,Maria Eloá do Ó,Henrique Ferraz de Arruda,Mauro Copelli,G. Camelo-Neto,Pedro V. Carelli*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本研究通过多分形去趋势波动分析（MF-DFA）量化终末期患者同步脑电和心电时间序列的复杂性变化，发现神经活动多分形性坍缩而心脏信号谱宽异常增大，两者呈现负相关，表明脑-心功能解耦及体-脑崩溃机制，提示死亡过程是层级协调崩溃导致的跨系统解离极端形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究终末期人体神经生理功能的崩溃机制，验证体-脑崩溃假说，揭示死亡过程中跨系统动力学的解耦特征。

Method: 采用多分形去趋势波动分析（MF-DFA）量化终末期患者同步采集的脑电（EEG）和心电（ECG）时间序列的多分形谱宽度及其时序演化，并分析两系统谱宽之间的相关性。

Result: 神经活动多分形谱宽度坍缩（朝向更受限状态），心脏信号谱宽异常增大（非线性波动增加、动力学不稳定性增强）；两者呈负相关，表明功能解耦和反相关动力学出现。

Conclusion: 终末期并非统一性生理衰退，而是体-脑崩溃过程——外周功能障碍逐步压垮中枢调控；这种逆动力学模式与其他外周驱动适应过程一致，死亡是层级协调崩溃下的极端跨系统解离形式。

Abstract: This study investigates the terminal breakdown of human neurophysiological function through the lens of non-linear dynamics by analyzing the multifractal spectrum. Using Multifractal Detrended Fluctuation Analysis (MF-DFA), we quantify the temporal evolution of complexity in synchronized electroencephalogram (EEG) and electrocardiogram (ECG) time series from patients in the terminal stage. Our results reveal a marked divergence in multifractal spectrum width: while neural activity exhibits a collapse of multifractality toward a more constrained state, cardiac signals undergo anomalous spectral broadening, indicating increased non-linear fluctuations and dynamical instability. A negative correlation between these spectral widths suggests effective functional decoupling and the emergence of anti-correlated dynamics between neural and cardiac systems. Rather than reflecting a uniform physiological decline, this divergence is consistent with a body-to-brain breakdown in which peripheral dysfunction progressively overwhelms central regulatory processes. In a broader context, the observed opposing trends resemble patterns reported in other body-driven adaptive processes, suggesting that inverse dynamics across coupled systems may emerge when constraints originate from peripheral rather than central mechanisms. Ultimately, the dying process appears to represent an extreme form of cross-system disintegration, marked by the collapse of the hierarchical coordination that normally sustains integrated physiological function.

</details>


### [238] [Phase model analysis of the effect of M-current on neural synchrony in hippocampal networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12684)
*Megha Manoj,Sue Ann Campbell*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本研究通过计算模型探讨了乙酰胆碱（ACh）水平对海马神经集群形成的影响，发现低ACh条件下网络可完全同步（促进巩固），高ACh条件下网络分解为多个稳定对称集群（促进编码），从而从同步性角度解释了ACh在记忆编码与巩固中的双向调节作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有假说认为高ACh促进记忆编码、低ACh促进记忆巩固，但其背后的神经动力学机制尚不明确。本文旨在通过研究ACh对神经元同步性的影响，揭示神经集群形成与ACh水平之间的计算原理。

Method: 构建含M电流的锥体神经元网络模型（M电流受ACh下调），利用弱耦合相位模型降维方法分析成对神经元，进而预测全连接、距离依赖和最近邻三种耦合架构下的大网络对称集群解。

Result: 低ACh水平（高M电流）下网络可实现完全同步；高ACh水平（低M电流）下网络解同步，形成多个稳定对称集群，代表不同的神经集群。

Conclusion: ACh通过调节M电流调控神经元同步性，进而控制神经集群的形成与分解，为ACh在记忆编码（高ACh，多集群）与巩固（低ACh，完全同步）中的双向角色提供了动力学解释。

Abstract: Neural assemblies, transiently coordinated groups of neurons, observed in the hippocampus are thought to underlie the formation of episodic memories. Acetylcholine (ACh), a neuromodulator, that is received by the hippocampus, plays a critical role in memory and learning. A well supported hypothesis suggests that high levels of ACh during active exploration and rapid eye movement (REM) sleep promote memory encoding, while low levels during quiet waking and slow-wave sleep (SWS) support memory consolidation. We study this bidirectional role of ACh in neural assembly formation through its effect on the synchrony among neurons. We consider a network model of pyramidal neurons, each equipped with a slow, voltage-dependent, non-inactivating potassium current (M-current), which is downregulated in the presence of ACh. Neural assemblies are represented as cluster solutions to this system. Using a one-dimensional phase model reduction of a pair of weakly coupled pyramidal neurons under different levels of the M-current, we predict the symmetric cluster solutions that may emerge in larger networks equipped with all-to-all globally homogeneous, symmetric distance-dependent and nearest-neighbours coupling architectures. We find that under low ACh conditions, the network can fully synchronize, whereas high levels can desynchronize the network into multiple stable symmetric cluster solutions representing distinct neural assemblies.

</details>


### [239] [Deep Sleep Classification via EEG Signal Criticality: A Passive BCI Approach for Sleep-Improvement Neurofeedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13017)
*Stanisław Narębski,Tomasz Komendziński,Tomasz M. Rutkowski*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本研究评估了基于去趋势波动分析（DFA）的临界性特征用于自动睡眠分期（特别是深睡N3识别），通过对347,232个EEG epoch进行分析，发现朴素贝叶斯分类器在10折交叉验证中取得了最高的平均平衡准确率（87.17%），显著优于深度神经网络（81.58%）和随机森林（80.97%），表明DFA临界性特征位于独特的非线性流形上，概率性解码为被动脑机接口中的状态依赖神经反馈提供了高精度感知机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动睡眠分期是被动脑机接口（pBCI）的基础应用之一，通过解码自发神经状态实现独立于用户意图的闭环干预。本研究的动机在于评估基于去趋势波动分析（DFA）提取的临界性特征，用于特异性地识别深睡阶段（N3），为神经反馈提供高精度的状态感知引擎。

Method: 分析了290名老年女性的347,232个EEG epoch，使用UMAP流形学习可视化状态转换。随后通过10折交叉验证对六个分类器进行基准测试，以平衡准确率确定最优的"状态感知"引擎。

Result: 朴素贝叶斯分类器取得了最高的平均平衡准确率（87.17% ± 0.24%），显著优于全连接深度神经网络（FNN: 81.58%）和随机森林（80.97%）。线性模型表现较差（LDA: 57.21%；SVM: 51.01%），表明DFA临界性特征位于独特的非线性流形上。

Conclusion: EEG临界性的概率性解码为被动脑机接口提供了高精度的感知机制。该鲁棒分类流程支持开发状态依赖型神经反馈（如靶向听觉刺激），以增强认知恢复。

Abstract: Automated sleep staging is a fundamental application of passive Brain-Computer Interfaces (pBCI), decoding spontaneous neural states to enable closed-loop interventions independent of user intent. This study evaluates criticality features derived from Detrended Fluctuation Analysis (DFA) for the specific identification of deep sleep (N3).
  We analyzed $347,232$ EEG epochs from $290$ older women using UMAP manifold learning to visualize state transitions. Subsequently, six classifiers were benchmarked via 10-fold cross-validation, using balanced accuracy to determine the optimal "state-sensing" engine for neurofeedback.Naive Bayes achieved the highest mean balanced accuracy ($87.17\% \pm 0.24\%$), significantly outperforming a fully connected deep neural network (FNN: $81.58\%$) and Random Forest ($80.97\%$). Linear models (LDA: $57.21\%$; SVM: $51.01\%$) performed poorly, indicating that DFA-derived criticality features reside on a distinct, non-linear manifold.
  Probabilistic decoding of EEG criticality provides a high-accuracy sensing mechanism for pBCIs. This robust classification pipeline supports the development of state-dependent neurofeedback, such as targeted auditory stimulation, to enhance cognitive recovery.

</details>


### [240] [Including the Cost of Irreducible Uncertainty in the Policy Compression Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13132)
*Álvaro Garrido-Pérez,Pieter Simoens,Amrapali Pednekar,Yara Khaluf*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文扩展了政策压缩框架，在认知成本中加入了条件熵项，使最优策略在η增大时变得更尖锐，从而更准确地反映人类决策中的认知成本与偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准政策压缩框架将条件熵视为无成本的，但实证表明条件熵会影响反应时，因此该框架对认知成本的建模不完整。

Method: 将认知成本定义为策略复杂度（状态与动作的互信息）与加权条件熵之和，引入新参数η来控制条件熵的权重，求解相应的最优策略。

Result: 最优策略保持指数形式，但随着η增大策略变得更尖锐，使得策略精度可以更独立于奖励敏感性变化。

Conclusion: 标准框架可能低估了动作选择的认知成本，扩展模型能更好解释人类决策偏差，但增加了模型拟合的复杂度。

Abstract: AI decision-support systems can benefit from anticipating biases in human decision-making. Many such biases may arise from human cognitive limitations. The policy compression framework models decision-making as a trade-off between reward maximization and the cognitive cost of encoding state-dependent action policies, formalized as the mutual information between states and actions (policy complexity). We argue that this account is incomplete because it treats conditional entropy--the irreducible uncertainty about which action should be selected given a state--as costless, even though empirical evidence suggests that it modulates reaction times. We therefore extend the framework by defining cognitive cost as the sum of policy complexity and a weighted conditional-entropy term, governed by a new parameter, $η$. The resulting optimal policy retains the standard exponential form but becomes sharper as $η$ increases, allowing policy precision to vary more independently of reward sensitivity. This modification implies that the standard policy compression framework may underestimate the cognitive cost of action selection, and it has the potential to better account for biases in human decision-making. At the same time, it introduces additional complexity for fitting the model to human data, which future work will need to address.

</details>


### [241] [Robust State-space Reconstruction of Brain Dynamics via Bootstrap Monte Carlo SSA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.00011)
*Sir-Lord Wiafe,Carter Hinsley,Vince D. Calhoun*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出BMC-SSA方法，结合蒙特卡洛奇异谱分析和自举稳定性，从短时、高噪声的fMRI时间序列中提取可靠的振荡模式，改善状态空间重构的稳定性和确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统延迟嵌入理论要求长时、无噪声记录，但实际神经影像数据（如fMRI）存在记录短、噪声高、采样粗且含自相关背景结构等问题，导致振荡成分被掩盖、嵌入不稳定，亟需一种鲁棒的信号重构方法。

Method: 提出Bootstrap蒙特卡洛奇异谱分析（BMC-SSA），将蒙特卡洛SSA与自举稳定性相结合，仅保留在重采样数据中具有统计支持和可重复性的振荡模式，从而重构出强调可靠振荡结构的时间序列。

Result: BMC-SSA改善了功能连接等测量指标的可靠性，并揭示了fMRI状态空间动力学中的差异，验证了该方法对噪声、有限信号进行鲁棒嵌入的有效性。

Conclusion: BMC-SSA为短时、高噪声信号的鲁棒状态空间重构提供了一种通用框架，尤其适用于fMRI等实际复杂系统数据。

Abstract: Reconstructing latent state-space geometry from time series provides a powerful route to studying nonlinear dynamics across complex systems. Delay-coordinate embedding provides the theoretical basis but assumes long, noise-free recordings, which many domains violate. In many real-world domains, recordings are short, noisy, and coarsely sampled; in neuroimaging, for example, fMRI additionally contains autocorrelated background structure that can obscure oscillatory components and destabilize embeddings. We propose bootstrap Monte Carlo singular spectrum analysis (BMC-SSA), which combines Monte Carlo SSA with bootstrap stability to retain oscillatory modes that are statistically supported and reproducible across resampled data. This produces reconstructions that emphasize reliable oscillatory structure, enhancing determinism and stabilizing subsequent embeddings.
