While straightforward in discrete spaces, efficiently sampling this residual in continuous space is non-trivial. Consequently, existing diffusion adaptations either use computationally inefficient sampling techniques or rely on an alternative scheme. In this work, we introduce a novel scheme that efficiently implements the original speculative sampling mechanism for diffusion models. Our approach offers a critical advantage over current methods: it enables us to adapt block verification from LLMs to diffusions -- which provably improves the acceptance rate of drafts. Furthermore, we formalize and analyze the Free Drafter, a heuristic self-speculative drafter for diffusions that requires no training. By enabling block verification, our Free Drafter yields up to a 6.3% speedup over existing speculative methods with no additional training and negligible overhead beyond the existing parallel verification pass.

</details>


### [354] [How Much Memory Do We Need? Adaptive Memory Gate for Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13443)
*Jihyeon Hur,Yongseok Kwon,Min-Gi Jo,Jeongwhan Choi,Noseong Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AMGFNO，通过可学习门控动态调节记忆权重，解决固定记忆权重在不同观测条件下适应性不足的问题，在低分辨率下实现55-79%的nRMSE降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强型神经算子使用固定记忆权重，无法适应不同分辨率或物理参数的变化，导致性能受限。初步实验表明最优记忆权重随分辨率和粘度变化，需要动态调整机制。

Method: 提出AMGFNO，通过可学习门控机制动态调节记忆权重，使模型能根据观测条件自适应地调整历史状态信息的利用程度。

Result: 在Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程和Burgers方程上，低分辨率下nRMSE降低55-79%；门控值从约0.7自动降至接近零（随分辨率增加）。

Conclusion: 动态记忆门控机制有效提升了神经算子在多变观测条件下的适应性和性能，验证了记忆权重应根据观测条件自适应调节。

Abstract: Neural operators have emerged as a powerful data-driven approach for solving time-dependent PDEs. Among recent advances, memory-augmented neural operators explicitly incorporate past states and have achieved remarkable performance under low-resolution observation settings. However, existing approaches apply a fixed memory weight regardless of observation conditions, such as resolution or physical parameters, limiting their adaptability. Our preliminary experiments reveal that optimal memory weight varies with resolution and viscosity, implying that a fixed memory weight cannot simultaneously optimize performance across diverse settings. We propose AMGFNO, which dynamically modulates memory weight through a learnable gate. On the Kuramoto-Sivashinsky and Burgers' equations, AMGFNO achieves 55-79% nRMSE reduction over at low resolution, with the learned gate value automatically decreasing from $\bar{g} \approx 0.7$ to near-zero as resolution increases.

</details>


### [355] [Clustering Node Attributed Networks with Graph Neural Networks and Self Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13444)
*Rodrigo de Sapienza Luna,Daniel Ratton Figueiredo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图神经网络（GNN）的无监督图聚类框架，通过多轮自学习迭代优化节点表示和聚类结果。每一轮中，GNN生成节点表示用于聚类，聚类结果影响下一轮的图结构，同时利用上下文图增强表示学习。实验表明，该方法在合成数据上优于仅依赖网络或属性的方法，在真实数据上对平衡聚类具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图聚类方法往往仅利用网络拓扑结构，而真实世界中节点通常带有属性信息。现有方法未能有效联合利用网络边信息和节点属性信息，因此需要一种能够同时融合这两类信息的无监督聚类框架。

Method: 提出一种多轮自学习框架：每轮中，GNN生成节点表示并进行聚类；聚类结果用于构建下一轮的新图结构；同时构建上下文图以辅助节点表示生成。整个过程在完全无监督环境下进行。

Result: 合成数据上，该方法优于仅依赖网络或属性的算法，尤其在两者信息都不强时表现突出。多轮学习性能始终优于单轮训练。在真实数据集上，当簇大小平衡时，与最先进方法竞争。

Conclusion: 该框架有效融合了图结构和节点属性信息，通过多轮自学习机制增强了聚类性能，为无监督图聚类提供了新思路。

Abstract: Graph clustering - partitioning the node set of a graph into disjoint subsets that reflect some latent information - is a fundamental problem as it finds applications in a myriad of different scenarios. While this classic problem has been tackled for decades by different communities, a recent variation of the problem driven by real data considers the scenario where nodes have attributes that are also informative. This has triggered novel methods that simultaneously leverage network information (edges) and node information (attributed) in the design of novel clustering algorithms. This work proposes a novel framework that builds on prior works that have applied graph neural networks (GNN) to graph clustering. The proposed framework operates in rounds of self learning in a fully unsupervised setting. In each round, a GNN generates representations for nodes that are used to cluster the nodes. This clustering influences the graph used to generate the node representation in the next round. Moreover, a context graph built in each round using the original graph is used to generate the node representations. Empirical results show that the proposed methodology extracts information from both network edges and node attributes in synthetic data, outperforming algorithms focused solely on the network or attributes when neither are very informative. Multiple rounds of learning also improve the performance and always outperforms a long single round of training (i.e., classic GNN graph clustering). When considering real datasets, empirical results indicate that the proposed methodology is competitive to state-of-the-art methods when cluster sizes are balanced.

</details>


### [356] [Uncertainty Estimation for Molecular Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13451)
*Paul Seij,Christian A. Naesseth,Stephan Mandt,Metod Jazbec*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于拉普拉斯近似的后处理方法，用于估计预训练分子扩散模型生成样本的不确定性，该分数与样本质量负相关，可用于过滤低质量样本以提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型被广泛用于3D分子生成，但缺乏评估生成分子质量高低的原则性信号，因此需要一种能够估计每个样本不确定性的方法。

Method: 基于去噪网络的拉普拉斯近似，测量生成轨迹中噪声预测的变异性，从而得到每个样本的不确定性分数。

Result: 不确定性分数与样本质量呈负相关；通过过滤低质量样本，可以在测试时提升模型性能（test-time scaling）。

Conclusion: 该后处理方法能够有效识别低质量分子生成样本，为预训练扩散模型提供了无需重新训练的质量控制手段。

Abstract: Diffusion models have seen wide adoption for 3D molecular generation, yet they offer no principled signal of when a generated molecule is likely to be of low quality. We propose a post-hoc method for estimating per-sample uncertainty in pretrained molecular diffusion models. Building on a Laplace approximation of the denoising network, we measure the variability of the noise prediction across the generation trajectory. Empirically, we show that the resulting uncertainty score is informative of sample quality, exhibiting a negative correlation with established sample-level quality metrics. We further study how the proposed uncertainty score can be used to filter generated samples, improving model performance via test-time scaling.

</details>


### [357] [MaxProof: Scaling Mathematical Proof with Generative-Verifier RL and Population-Level Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13473)
*Jiacheng Chen,Xinyu Zhang,Shunkai Zhang,Yanmohan Wang,Lin Li,Tiancheng Qin,Qin Wang,Zhengmao Zhu,Tianle Li,Jingyang Li,Zehan Li,Binyang Jiang,Jin Zhu,Han Ding,Fei Yu,Chenyu Du,Zijian Song,Jiayuan Song,Zhi Zhang,Yunan Huang,Weiyu Cheng,Pengyu Zhao,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MaxProof是一种面向竞赛级数学证明的群体级测试时扩展框架，通过结合证明生成、验证和基于批评的修复能力，在IMO 2025和USAMO 2026上超越人类金牌阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在竞赛级数学证明任务中，现有方法在证明的可靠性和正确性上难以达到人类金牌水平，需要一种能够在测试时通过扩展计算量来提升证明质量的框架。

Method: M3模型先训练三种面向证明的能力（证明生成、证明验证、基于批评的证明修复），使用低假阳性率的深度防御生成式验证器；测试时MaxProof将模型同时用作生成器、验证器、精炼器和排序器，通过群体搜索和锦标赛选择返回最终证明。

Result: 在IMO 2025上达到35/42，在USAMO 2026上达到36/42，两项均超过人类金牌阈值。

Conclusion: MaxProof通过测试时扩展和群体级搜索，显著提升了竞赛级数学证明的性能，超越了人类金牌水平。

Abstract: We present MaxProof, a population-level test-time scaling framework for competition-level mathematical proof in the MiniMax-M3 series. M3 first trains three proof-oriented capabilities -- proof generation, proof verification, and critique-conditioned proof repair -- using a defense-in-depth generative verifier engineered for low false-positive rate. These capabilities are merged into a single released M3 model. At test time, MaxProof treats the model as a generator, verifier, refiner, and ranker, searches over a population of candidate proofs, and returns one final proof through tournament selection. With MaxProof test-time scaling, the M3 model reaches 35/42 on IMO 2025 and 36/42 on USAMO 2026, exceeding the human gold-medal threshold on both.

</details>


### [358] [SupraBench: A Benchmark for Supramolecular Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13477)
*Tianyi Ma,Yijun Ma,Zehong Wang,Weixiang Sun,Ziming Li,Connor R. Schmidt,Chuxu Zhang,Matthew J. Webber,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个超分子化学基准测试SupraBench，包含四个推理任务和一个视觉辅助任务，并发布了16M token的领域语料库SupraPMC。通过评测多种开源和商业LLM，发现模型在所有任务上仍有显著提升空间，领域适应性预训练对分布内回归有效但会牺牲严格格式输出，不同任务类型的难度分布差异揭示了当前超分子化学推理的具体缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计主客体系统耗时耗力，每个候选对需要数天的干实验验证。虽然LLM在分子结合任务上表现优异，但缺乏系统评估LLM在超分子化学推理（如结合亲和力预测）能力的基准。

Method: 与领域专家合作设计SupraBench，包含4个基础任务（结合亲和力预测、最优结合物选择、溶剂识别、主客体描述）和1个基于视觉的分子识别辅助任务；同时从Europe PMC筛选构建16M token的SupraPMC语料库，支持领域适配。

Result: 广泛评测表明LLM在所有任务上仍有较大提升空间；领域适应性预训练在分布内回归任务上表现良好，但与严格格式输出之间存在权衡；不同任务类型的难度分布差异显著，揭示了当前模型在超分子化学推理中的不同失败模式。

Conclusion: 现有LLM在超分子化学推理方面能力不足，本研究提供了基准、语料库和开源代码，为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Supramolecular chemistry, which includes the study of non-covalent host-guest assemblies, has advanced various applications. However, designing host-guest systems remains time-consuming, requiring days of dry-lab verification per candidate pair. Although LLMs have emerged as a fast alternative with strong performance on molecular binding tasks, no benchmark currently systematically evaluates LLMs for host-guest reasoning across fundamental supramolecular chemistry tasks, e.g., binding affinity prediction. To this end, we collaborate with domain experts to release the first Supramolecular Benchmark, called SupraBench, to evaluate LLMs in chemistry reasoning. Specifically, we design four fundamental tasks, i.e., binding affinity prediction, top-binder selection, solvent identification, and host-guest description, plus an auxiliary vision-based task for molecular identification. We also release SupraPMC, a curated 16M-token corpus of Supramolecular chemistry articles distilled from Europe PMC, to support the adaptation to the supramolecular domain. We benchmark a broad range of open and proprietary LLMs and find that LLMs leave substantial headroom across all tasks. Domain adaptation pretraining over SupraPMC transfers cleanly to in-distribution regression but trades off against strict letter-format output. Moreover, the difficulty profile differs sharply across task families, revealing distinct failure modes that indicate specific gaps in current supramolecular chemistry reasoning. Our source codes and benchmark datasets are available at https://github.com/Tianyi-Billy-Ma/SupraBench.

</details>


### [359] [CRAFTIIF: Cross-Resolution Analytic Four-Type Interpretable Isolation Forest for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13486)
*William Smits*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRAFTIIF是一个无监督多元时间序列异常检测框架，通过生成500个随机小波特征（四种类型分别对应点异常、分布异常、时间异常和集体异常）并送入五个结构化隔离森林，结合自适应Otsu/MAD阈值校准，在mTSBench基准的19个数据集上达到平均F1=0.228，VUS-PR比先前最优方法提升40.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督方法大多只针对四种结构不同的异常类型（点异常、分布异常、时间异常、集体异常）中的一两种，且可解释性有限，缺乏能同时覆盖所有类型且无需数据集特定调参的统一框架。

Method: 生成K=500个跨四种小波族（Morlet、DOG、Haar、Coiflet）的随机解析小波特征，每种专攻一种异常类型；构建五个结构化隔离森林（四种类型各一个加一个元IF处理复合异常）；采用自适应Otsu/MAD阈值自动校准检测率。

Result: 在mTSBench全部19个数据集上，平均F1=0.228（可检测的13个数据集上F1=0.322），VUS-PR达0.463，比先前最优0.329提升40.7%；消融实验证实自适应阈值（+38%）、四分支结构（+20%）、元IF（+23%）均不可或缺。

Conclusion: CRAFTIIF通过类型特定特征与结构化隔离森林的设计，首次实现了对全部四种异常类型的无监督检测，同时提供内建的类型归因可解释性，并提出了诊断框架识别出6个数据集对任何无监督方法均不可检测。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in multivariate time series is challenged by four structurally distinct anomaly types -- point (isolated spikes), distributional (level shifts), temporal (rhythm changes), and collective (inter-sensor correlation breakdowns) -- each requiring different feature representations. Most unsupervised methods target only one or two types and provide limited interpretability. We present CRAFTIIF (Cross-Resolution Analytic Four-Type Interpretable Isolation Forest), a fully unsupervised framework targeting all four types without dataset-specific tuning. CRAFTIIF generates K=500 random analytic wavelet feature draws across four families (Morlet, DOG, Haar, Coiflet), each targeting a specific anomaly type, feeding five structured Isolation Forests -- one per type plus a meta-IF for compound anomalies. An adaptive Otsu/MAD threshold calibrates detection automatically across anomaly rates from 0.1% to 69.2%. Because each IF is trained exclusively on type-specific features, branch firing provides direct anomaly-type attribution by construction, without post-hoc explanation. Evaluated on all 19 datasets of the mTSBench benchmark (Zhou et al., TMLR 2026), CRAFTIIF achieves mean F1=0.228 (all 19 datasets) and F1=0.322 (13 detectable datasets), ranking first among all 25 evaluated methods on VUS-PR (0.463 vs. previous best 0.329, +40.7%). A diagnostic framework -- oracle F1, detectability limits, and branch separation ratios -- identifies 6 of 19 datasets as fundamentally undetectable by any unsupervised method. Ablation over 11 conditions confirms adaptive thresholding (+38% F1), four-branch structure (+20%), and meta-IF (+23%) are each essential. Code: https://github.com/smitswil/craftiif

</details>


### [360] [A2D2: Fine-Tuning Any-Length Discrete Diffusion for Adaptive Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13565)
*Sophia Tang,Yuchen Zhu,Molei Tao,Pranam Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了A2D2框架，通过联合优化插入和去掩码策略以及基于质量的推理调度，实现了对任意长度离散扩散模型的奖励引导微调，并推导了Radon-Nikodym导数以确保收敛到奖励倾斜的序列分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任意长度的离散扩散模型在序列生成中具有潜力，但基于奖励引导的微调方法尚未得到充分探索，现有方法局限于固定长度或仅依赖推理时引导。

Method: A2D2框架：（1）联合优化插入（insertion）和去掩码（unmasking）策略；（2）推导联合插入-去掩码路径测度的Radon-Nikodym导数，保证理论收敛；（3）提出自适应联合解码（AJD）损失，最小化解码误差并生成最优路径测度。

Result: A2D2在奖励优化、生成灵活性和准确性上优于固定长度微调和推理时引导方法。

Conclusion: A2D2为任意长度离散扩散模型的奖励引导微调提供了统一的理论框架和有效的实践方法。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models offer a simple and stable likelihood-based framework for sequence generation, recently extended to any-length settings via token insertion. Principled reward-guided fine-tuning for any-length discrete diffusion, however, remains largely unexplored. We introduce Fine-Tuning Any-Length Discrete Diffusion for Adaptive Decoding (A2D2), a unified framework for reward-guided fine-tuning of any-length discrete diffusion models via joint optimization of the insertion and unmasking policies together with a quality-based inference schedule. We derive the Radon-Nikodym derivative for the joint insertion-unmasking path measures, enabling theoretically guaranteed convergence to the intractable reward-tilted sequence distribution without requiring target samples. Building on this, we establish unmasking and insertion quality as tractable approaches for minimizing decoding error and introduce the Adaptive Joint Decoding (AJD) loss, which provably yields the optimal path measure that generates the reward-tilted distribution. Empirically, A2D2 improves reward optimization while enhancing generation flexibility and accuracy over prior fixed-length fine-tuning and inference-time guidance methods.

</details>


### [361] [Adjusted Cup-Product Neural Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13568)
*Snigdha Chandan Khilar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于调整杯积（adjusted cup product）的神经层，该层通过硬编码杯积与高阶规范理论的调整项，实现了设计上的规范不变性。理论结果表明，在闭合循环上输出完全依赖于调整系数；若该系数为零，则输出完全消失，因此调整是规范不变信号的唯一来源。该可观测量是一个非零二次型，且严格在一阶和二阶规范变换下保持不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理学和几何学中的许多重要可观测量是上同调的杯积，但现有方法缺乏内置的规范不变性。作者希望设计一种神经原语，通过硬连线杯积并引入高阶规范理论的调整项，使得读出结果天然具有规范不变性。

Method: 提出调整杯积神经层（adjusted cup product neural layer），将杯积与来自高阶规范理论的调整项结合，作为神经网络的原始构件。该层在结构上强制实现规范不变性，无需通过学习来近似。

Result: 理论分析表明，在闭合循环上输出完全由调整系数决定；调整系数为零时输出消失，证明调整是唯一贡献源。该可观测量被证明是一个非零二次型，且在一阶和二阶规范变换下精确不变。

Conclusion: 调整杯积神经层提供了一种内置规范不变性的神经原语，可用于提取物理和几何中具有规范不变性的可观测量，为将对称性先验融入神经网络架构提供了新思路。

Abstract: Many important observables in physics and geometry are cup products of cochains. The adjusted cup product neural layer has been introduced in this paper. It is a neural primitive that hard wires the cup product with an adjustment term from higher gauge theory. This creates a readout that is gauge invariant by design. Their main theoretical result shows that on a closed cycle the output relies entirely on the adjustment coefficient. Setting this coefficient to zero removes the output completely regardless of other parameters. Thus the adjustment is the only source of gauge invariant signal. They prove this observable is a nonzero quadratic form and is exactly invariant under one and two gauge transformations.

</details>


### [362] [Existence Precedes Value: Joint Modeling of Observational Existence and Evolving States in Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13571)
*Yifan Hu,Hongzhou Chen,Peiyuan Liu,Yiding Liu,Zewei Dong,Jiang-Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Timeflies是一个统一框架，将时间序列预测重新定义为未来观测可能性推理与数值估计的联合问题，通过双流架构（观测流和数值流）及三个专用模块显式建模观测动态与状态演化的交互，在包含自然缺失数据和工业数据的Shadow基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如Neural ODEs和连续时间图网络）在推理时需要预先知道未来有效观测的时间戳（隐式Oracle假设），但在实际系统中，未来是否会有有效观测本身就是一个不确定问题。因此需要同时预测未来观测的可能性及其数值。

Method: 提出Timeflies框架：(1) 双流架构——观测流（observation stream）和数值流（value stream）；(2) 三个耦合模块：可靠性感知嵌入（reliability-aware embedding）、观测引导的依赖建模（observation-guided dependency modeling）、联合预测（joint prediction）；(3) 构建Shadow基准（结合公开数据集和工业数据）；(4) 提出观测-数值联合熵（OVJE）指标综合评估耦合可预测性。

Result: 大量实验表明Timeflies在各项指标上持续优于现有方法，验证了显式建模未来观测可能性的重要性。

Conclusion: 在非完整和不规则时间序列预测中，显式建模未来观测的可能性是必要的，Timeflies通过联合预测框架有效解决了这一问题。

Abstract: Real-world time series are often highly incomplete and irregular due to sensor dormancy, transmission delays, and event-driven sampling, making reliable forecasting fundamentally challenging. Existing methods have evolved from impute-then-forecast pipelines to continuous-time models such as Neural ODEs and continuous-time graph networks. While these approaches improve the modeling of historical irregularity, they still rely on an implicit oracle assumption at inference time: the timestamps of future valid observations are presumed to be known in advance. This assumption limits practical relevance, since in many real systems the more fundamental question is not only what the future value will be, but also whether a valid observation will occur at all. In this paper, we propose Timeflies, a unified framework that reformulates forecasting as a joint problem of future observability inference and value estimation. To explicitly model the interaction between observation dynamics and state evolution, Timeflies adopts an observation stream and a value stream, coupled through three dedicated modules for reliability-aware embedding, observation-guided dependency modeling, and joint prediction. We further construct Shadow, a benchmark that combines natural missingness from public datasets with real-world industrial data, and introduce the Observation-Value Joint Entropy (OVJE) metric to comprehensively evaluate this coupled predictability. Extensive experiments show that Timeflies consistently outperforms existing methods, highlighting the importance of explicitly modeling future observability in time series forecasting with missing values. Code and dataset are available in https://github.com/ant-intl/Timeflies.

</details>


### [363] [Learning with Simulators: No Regret in a Computationally Bounded World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13576)
*Sasha Voitovych,Abhishek Shetty,Noah Golowich,Alexander Rakhlin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"可模拟过程"框架，证明在可访问近似数据生成过程的模拟器时，即使数据高度依赖，也能恢复与独立数据相同的经典学习保证（基于VC维的误差界），并展示了条件采样的统计与计算优势，最终提出一个能同时学习所有有界多项式时间可采样过程的单一算法，其遗憾由过程的时间有界Kolmogorov复杂度控制，显著拓展了经典PAC模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典学习理论中大多数泛化结果依赖数据独立性假设，而强依赖数据下的结果十分有限。为填补这一空白，作者引入可模拟过程框架，探索在数据生成过程任意复杂且依赖的情况下，能否恢复与独立数据相同的学习保证。

Method: 提出可模拟过程框架，假设学习器可访问一个近似数据生成分布的模拟器；利用该模拟器证明可恢复基于VC维的误差界；进一步研究条件采样的能力，并设计单一算法同时学习所有有界多项式时间可采样过程，遗憾由时间有界Kolmogorov复杂度控制。

Result: 在可模拟过程框架下，即使数据高度依赖，也能恢复与经典独立数据设定下相同的学习保证（依赖于VC维的误差界）；条件采样在统计和计算上具有严格优势；所提单一算法能同时学习所有有界多项式时间可采样过程。

Conclusion: 该工作显著拓宽了经典PAC学习模型，表明在仅有模拟器访问而非独立同分布假设的条件下，仍能实现强泛化保证，为强依赖数据场景下的学习理论提供了新基础。

Abstract: Understanding the minimal assumptions necessary for generalization is the fundamental question in learning theory. Unfortunately, most results rely heavily on independence (or some proxy thereof) of the data-generating process, while results for strongly dependent data are far more limited. Towards addressing this gap, we introduce the framework of simulatable processes, where the learner has access to a simulator that approximates the distribution generating the data (which may be an arbitrarily complex and dependent process). Surprisingly, given access to such a simulator, we show that we can recover the same learning guarantees as in the classical setting with independent data, namely, error bounds that depend on the VC dimension. Further, we use this framework to study the power of conditional sampling and show strict statistical and computational advantages in this setting. As a highlight of our framework, we exhibit a single algorithm that simultaneously learns any given VC class under all processes samplable in bounded polynomial time, with regret controlled by the time-bounded Kolmogorov complexity of the process. This provides a significant conceptual broadening of the classical PAC model.

</details>


### [364] [Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging: Transitioning from Uniform Priors to Sparse Posteriors in Ensemble Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13589)
*Meher Sai Preetam,Meher Bhaskar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为SCSB的数学框架，用于对bootstrap集成模型进行后训练压缩和概率校准，通过引入凹二次惩罚项解决L1-单纯形悖论，实现高达96%的集成压缩和更好的校准效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准bagging集成（如随机森林、Bagged SVM等）对所有基估计器赋予均匀投票权重，忽视了基估计器在不同局部区域的能力差异，导致模型过度自信。因此需要一种既能压缩集成规模又能校准概率输出的方法。

Method: 将集成剪枝和校准建模为概率单纯形上的联合优化问题，最小化袋外（OOB）损失；为解决L1范数在单纯形上为常数而无法实现稀疏化的理论困境（L1-单纯形悖论），引入凹二次惩罚项诱导稀疏性。

Result: 模型无关的方法，可实现高达96%的集成压缩，推理速度线性提升，预期校准误差（ECE）显著降低，同时保持或提升泛化精度。

Conclusion: SCSB通过数学严谨的方式同时解决了集成模型的压缩和校准问题，在保持精度的前提下大幅提升推理效率并改善概率输出质量。

Abstract: We present Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging (SCSB), a mathematically rigorous framework for post-training compression and probability calibration of bootstrap-based bagging ensembles. Standard bagging ensembles (such as Random Forests, Bagged SVMs, and Bagged Neural Networks) assign uniform voting power to all constituent estimators. However, this naive uniform prior ignores the varying local competence of base estimators and contributes to model overconfidence. We formulate ensemble pruning and calibration as a joint optimization problem over the probability simplex by minimizing the Out-Of-Bag (OOB) loss. To induce sparsity, we address the theoretical "L1-simplex paradox" -- the mathematical reality that the L1 norm is constant on the simplex and fails to prune -- by introducing a concave quadratic penalty. SCSB is model-agnostic and achieves up to 96% ensemble compression, yielding linear inference speedups and superior probability calibration (lowered Expected Calibration Error) while preserving or enhancing generalization accuracy.

</details>


### [365] [Beyond the Commitment Boundary: Probing Epiphenomenal Chain-of-Thought in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13603)
*Daniel Scalena,Sara Candussio,Luca Bortolussi,Elisabetta Fersini,Malvina Nissim,Gabriele Sarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现链式思维推理中存在一个"承诺边界"——模型从临时猜测到稳定答案的尖锐转变点，该点往往在推理块结束前出现，后续步骤对最终答案无实质影响。基于此，作者提出早停机制，可平均减少55%的推理步数且性能损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CoT推理中，各步骤对最终答案的因果影响尚不明确，缺乏对推理过程中答案形成机制的理解。

Method: 1) 通过早退（early exit）估计每个推理步骤的因果重要性；2) 利用注意力探针（attention probes）从中间推理步骤线性解码答案形成阶段；3) 在承诺边界处实施早停（early-exit）来压缩推理长度。

Result: 1) 推理过程中存在清晰的承诺边界，该边界通常在单个步骤内完成从临时猜测到稳定高置信答案的转变；2) 承诺边界后的CoT步骤是"副现象"（epiphenomenal）的，不影响最终答案概率；3) 基于该信号早停可减少高达55%的推理长度，且性能影响可忽略。

Conclusion: CoT推理中存在可预测的承诺边界，后续推理步骤多为冗余，通过检测该边界可实现高效推理压缩。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning is the dominant paradigm for inference-time scaling in language models, yet the causal influence of individual steps on the final answer poorly understood. We estimate each step's causal importance via early exit and use this measure to study how answers form across the reasoning traces of several model families. Across diverse tasks, we find that reasoning typically crosses a \emph{commitment boundary} -- a sharp transition from transient intermediate guesses to a stable, high-confidence answer. This transition often happens in a single step, well before the model's reasoning block ends, and is followed by \emph{epiphenomenal} CoT steps that leave the final answer probability unaltered. Using attention probes, we show that answer-formation stages can be linearly decoded from intermediate reasoning steps with high accuracy and generalize robustly to unseen reasoning tasks. We exploit this signal to early-exit reasoning blocks at the commitment boundary, reducing the length of CoTs up to 55\% on average with negligible impact on model performance.

</details>


### [366] [The Stable Recovery Manifold: Geometric Principles Governing Recoverability in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13637)
*Ayushman Trivedi,Bhavika Melwani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过引入恢复子空间维度（k_t）等几何度量，研究了持续学习中的可恢复性结构。实验发现，尽管表征漂移显著，但恢复维度保持稳定（平均k_t=8.0），主角度漂移能强预测可恢复性（r=-0.862），几何模型解释82.2%方差。结果支持稳定恢复流形假说，表明灾难性遗忘本质上是可访问性和流形对齐问题，而非信息破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于可访问性坍缩框架，探究持续学习中遗忘知识的几何恢复结构，验证遗忘是信息破坏还是可访问性损失。

Method: 在Split CIFAR-100上用ResNet-18进行十任务序列训练，引入恢复子空间维度（k_t）度量最小奇异方向数，分析可恢复性、表征漂移和恢复复杂度，检验可恢复性扩散假说与稳定恢复流形假说。

Result: 恢复维度稳定（平均k_t=8.0），主角度漂移强预测可恢复性（r=-0.862），几何模型解释82.2%方差，拒绝可恢复性扩散假说，支持稳定恢复流形假说。

Conclusion: 灾难性遗忘主要是可访问性和流形对齐问题，而非先前知识的破坏；遗忘知识仍以紧凑可解码形式存在于表征空间中。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting is often viewed as the destruction of previously learned knowledge during sequential learning. Building on the Accessibility Collapse framework, we investigate the geometric structure of recoverability in continual learning. Using Split CIFAR-100 and a sequentially trained ResNet-18, we analyze recoverability, representational drift, and recovery complexity across ten tasks. We introduce Recovery Subspace Dimensionality (k_t), a measure of the minimum number of singular directions required to preserve 90 percent of full probe performance. Contrary to our Recoverability Diffusion hypothesis, recovery dimensionality remains stable throughout training (mean k_t = 8.0) despite substantial representational drift. Principal-angle drift strongly predicts recoverability (r = -0.862), and a simple geometric model explains 82.2 percent of recoverability variance. These findings support the Stable Recovery Manifold hypothesis, suggesting that forgotten knowledge remains compactly decodable despite representational reorganization. The results indicate that catastrophic forgetting is primarily an accessibility and manifold-alignment problem rather than information destruction.

</details>


### [367] [Dense Supervision, Sparse Updates: On the Sparsity and Geometry of On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13657)
*Guo Yu,Wenlin Liu,Yulan Hu,Hao-Xuan Ma,Jun-Peng Jiang,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了在线策略蒸馏（OPD）的参数更新特性，发现其更新具有稀疏性（集中在FFN层）和几何上的低秩集中性（远离源权重主奇异子空间），并论证了密度教师监督并未将OPD转变为普通的密集参数重写。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD结合了在线策略学生轨迹和密集教师监督两种理想成分，但其对模型参数的具体影响机制尚不明确，本文旨在揭示OPD如何改变模型参数。

Method: 在多个语言和视觉-语言模型对及用例上，从稀疏性和几何两个维度分析OPD更新的特性，包括更新分布、坐标稀疏性、秩结构、谱集中性等，并进行优化器消融实验。

Result: 1) OPD更新稀疏且坐标稀疏，分布在各层且以FFN层为主；仅训练发现的子网络可恢复接近完整OPD的性能；但稀疏诱导的SGD优化器不如AdamW。2) 更新数值上满秩但谱集中，主要位于源权重主奇异子空间之外，且落在源权重接近零的坐标上。

Conclusion: 密集教师监督并未使OPD变成普通的密集参数重写；OPD保留了在线策略后训练的重要几何特征。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (\textsc{OPD}) has recently become a prominent post-training recipe as it combines two desirable ingredients: on-policy student trajectories and dense teacher supervision, yet how this hybrid changes a model's parameters remains unclear. Across several language and vision-language model pairs and use cases, our analysis yields two main findings. On sparsity, \textsc{OPD}-style updates are small and coordinate-sparse. They are distributed across layers and are usually FFN-heavy. This sparse structure is operationally useful: training only the discovered subnetwork recovers nearly the same performance as full \textsc{OPD}. However, the sparsity-inducing SGD optimizer underperforms AdamW in our optimizer ablation, likely because dense teacher supervision preserves heterogeneous coordinate-wise gradient scales where AdamW's adaptive scaling remains useful. On geometry, the updates are numerically full-rank but spectrally concentrated; they lie mostly away from the principal singular subspaces of the source weights and fall disproportionately on coordinates where the source weights are close to zero. These findings suggest that dense teacher supervision does not turn \textsc{OPD} into ordinary dense parameter rewriting; instead, \textsc{OPD} retains important geometric signatures of on-policy post-training.

</details>


### [368] [Understanding Truncated Positional Encodings for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13671)
*James Flora,Mitchell Black,Weng-Keen Wong,Amir Nayyeri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了图神经网络中位置编码（PEs）的截断版本。虽然完整版谱PE和基于随机游走的PE表达力等价（介于1-WL和3-WL之间），但截断后这些PE的表达力出现根本性差异，截断谱PE不再强于1-WL测试。实验表明混合使用不同截断PE效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践中常用截断版位置编码（如前k个特征空间或邻接矩阵幂次），但其理论性质未知，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 理论分析截断PE的表达力差异，引入k-调和距离（k-harmonic distances）作为一类谱PE进行研究，并在真实数据集上进行实验验证。

Result: 截断条件下多种PE表达力出现根本性差异；截断谱PE不再强于1-WL测试；不同截断PE之间表达力存在显著差异；混合截断PE效果优于单一家族。

Conclusion: 截断PE的理论性质与完整版截然不同，实践中混合使用多种截断PE比使用单一家族更优。

Abstract: Positional encodings (PEs) enhance the power of graph neural networks (GNNs), both theoretically and empirically. Two of the most popular families of PEs - spectral (e.g., Laplacian eigenspaces, effective resistance) and walk-based (polynomials of the adjacency matrix) - are theoretically equivalent in expressive power, with expressivity between the 1-WL and 3-WL tests. However, this equivalence assumes the GNN uses the "complete" version of these PEs, which requires $O(n^3)$ time and space complexity. Instead, practitioners commonly use truncated variants of these encodings, such as the first $k$ eigenspaces or powers of the adjacency matrix. However, the theoretical properties of these truncated PEs are unknown. In this work, we initiate the study of these truncated PEs. Theoretically, we show that, under truncation, several families of PEs are fundamentally different in expressive power. As a corollary, we show that truncated spectral PEs are no longer stronger than the 1-WL test. We also study a family of spectral PEs, the $k$-harmonic distances, to highlight the differences in expressive power of even closely related truncated PEs. Finally, we experimentally show that a mix of truncated PEs is preferable to any single family on real-world datasets.

</details>


### [369] [On Pitfalls of $\textit{RemOve-And-Retrain}$: Data Processing Inequality Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2304.13836)
*Junhwa Song,Keumgang Cha,Junghoon Seo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从信息论角度揭示ROAR基准测试存在根本性缺陷：通过无法增加决策函数信息量的后处理（如模糊化）反而能提升ROAR分数，说明该指标与归因图的信息含量无关，而是偏向空间模糊的掩码。实验验证了模糊性与ROAR表现的正相关性，并在ROAD变体中也观察到类似模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ROAR基准被广泛用于评估特征归因方法，但其有效性从未从信息论角度被严格审视。作者旨在揭示ROAR分数的提升是否真正反映归因图携带了更多关于模型决策函数的信息。

Method: 采用信息论中的数据预处理不等式（DPI）作为理论工具，证明模型和数据无关的后处理无法增加关于决策函数的信息量。在CIFAR-10、SVHN、CUB-200三个数据集上进行实验，系统分析归因图模糊程度与ROAR/ROAD评分之间的关联。

Result: 模型和数据无关的后处理（如模糊化）能够提升ROAR分数；模糊性与ROAR性能之间存在一致的正相关关系；该模式在ROAD变体中同样存在。

Conclusion: ROAR基准存在偏向模糊掩码的系统性偏差，其改进的排名并不能证明归因图包含更多关于模型的信息。作者提出更谨慎的基于移除的基准测试指南，对验证神经网络内部机制的 mechanistic understanding 具有重要意义。

Abstract: The RemOve-And-Retrain (ROAR) benchmark is widely used to evaluate feature attribution methods, yet its validity remains underexplored from an information-theoretic perspective. We show that model- and data-agnostic post-processing of attribution maps (transformations that, by the data processing inequality, \emph{cannot} add information about the decision function) can often improve ROAR scores. This means that an improved ROAR ranking is not, by itself, evidence that an attribution map carries more information about the model. We trace this failure mode to a bias toward spatially blurry masks. Experiments on CIFAR-10, SVHN, and CUB-200 show a consistent association between blurriness and ROAR performance, a pattern that also appears in the ROAD variant. We provide guidelines for more cautious removal-based benchmarking, with implications for validating mechanistic understanding of neural network internals.

</details>


### [370] [On Approximating the Dynamic Response of Synchronous Generators via Operator Learning: A Step Towards Building Deep Operator-based Power Grid Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2301.12538)
*Christian Moya,Amirhossein Mollaali,Guang Lin,Meng Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于算子学习（DeepONet）的框架，用于逼近同步发电机的动态响应。该框架可用于构建神经网络发电机模型以与电网模拟器交互，或实时跟踪真实发电机的暂态响应。作者设计了递归数值方案、残差DeepONet方案（含累积误差估计）以及DAgger数据聚合策略，证明了该框架能有效逼近同步发电机的暂态模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同步发电机的动态响应模拟对电力系统稳定性分析至关重要，但传统基于物理模型的方法计算成本高，且难以与数据驱动方法灵活结合。因此，需要一种既能高效逼近发电机无限维解算子，又能与电网模拟器交互或实时跟踪真实响应的数据驱动框架。

Method: 1) 使用数据驱动的DeepONet逼近发电机的无限维解算子；2) 设计递归数值方案，利用训练好的DeepONet在多维输入下模拟发电机响应；3) 提出残差DeepONet方案，可融入现有数学模型并给出预测累积误差估计；4) 构建DAgger数据聚合策略，利用模拟交互中产生的数据对DeepONet进行微调。

Result: 作为概念验证，所提框架能够有效逼近同步发电机的暂态模型，验证了DeepONet在电力系统动态响应模拟中的可行性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的算子学习框架为同步发电机动态响应模拟提供了一种灵活、高效的数据驱动替代方案，其递归数值方案、残差方案及DAgger策略增强了模型的准确性和实用性，未来可推广至更复杂的电力系统组件。

Abstract: This paper develops an Operator Learning framework for approximating the dynamic response of synchronous generators. The framework can be used to (i) build a neural network-based generator model that interacts with a power grid simulator or (ii) shadow the true generator's transient response. First, we develop a data-driven Deep Operator Network (DeepONet) to approximate the infinite-dimensional solution operator of the generators. Then, we design a numerical scheme based on DeepONet that simulates the generator's response over a given time horizon. The proposed scheme recursively employs the trained DeepONet to simulate the response for a given multi-dimensional input that describes the interaction between the generator and the power grid. In addition, we design a residual DeepONet numerical scheme that can incorporate information from existing mathematical models. We accompany this residual DeepONet scheme with an estimate for the prediction's cumulative error. Finally, we build a data aggregation (DAgger) strategy that allows fine-tuning of DeepONets using aggregated training data that the DeepONets will likely encounter during interactive simulations with other grid components. As a proof of concept, we demonstrate that the proposed frameworks can effectively approximate the transient model of a synchronous generator.

</details>


### [371] [Geometry of Lightning Self-Attention: Identifiability and Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.17221)
*Nathan W. Henry,Giovanni Luca Marchetti,Kathlén Kohn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用代数几何工具，从理论上分析了无归一化自注意力网络定义函数空间的几何性质，包括深度注意力的可辨识性、函数空间维度、单层模型的奇异点和边界点，并提出了对归一化自注意力网络的猜想性扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自注意力网络在现代深度学习中被广泛使用，但其函数空间的几何结构（如可辨识性、维度等）缺乏严格的理论分析。作者希望从代数几何角度填补这一空白。

Method: 将无归一化的自注意力网络视为多项式映射，利用代数几何工具（如参数化的通用纤维描述）分析其几何性质；针对单层模型刻画奇异点和边界点；对归一化网络提出猜想并给出单层证明和深层数值验证。

Result: 给出了任意层数下深度注意力的参数化通用纤维描述，进而计算了函数空间维度；刻画了单层模型的奇异点和边界点；对归一化自注意力网络提出了猜想性扩展，并在单层情形证明、深层情形数值验证。

Conclusion: 代数几何为理解无归一化自注意力网络的函数空间提供了有效工具；归一化自注意力网络的类似性质可能成立，但需要进一步的理论证明。

Abstract: We consider function spaces defined by self-attention networks without normalization, and theoretically analyze their geometry. Since these networks are polynomial, we rely on tools from algebraic geometry. In particular, we study the identifiability of deep attention by providing a description of the generic fibers of the parametrization for an arbitrary number of layers and, as a consequence, compute the dimension of the function space. Additionally, for a single-layer model, we characterize the singular and boundary points. Finally, we formulate a conjectural extension of our results to normalized self-attention networks, prove it for a single layer, and numerically verify it in the deep case.

</details>


### [372] [Is Stochastic Gradient Descent Effective? A PDE Perspective on Machine Learning processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.08425)
*Davide Barbieri,Matteo Bonforte,Peio Ibarrondo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过Fokker-Planck型抛物型偏微分方程分析随机梯度下降（SGD）的行为，识别出漂移阶段和扩散阶段两个不同机制，在非凸势函数和退化扩散矩阵条件下，对SGD的集中现象、平均逃逸时间以及渐近收敛性给出了定量估计和理论证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管SGD在监督学习中广泛用于优化神经网络权重，但非凸损失函数和退化扩散矩阵情形下的SGD动力学行为几乎未被研究。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，从PDE角度深入理解SGD的底层机制。

Method: 采用Fokker-Planck型抛物PDE分析SGD；识别漂移阶段和扩散阶段两个不同机制；利用对偶方法和熵方法处理非凸成本函数和退化扩散矩阵下的渐近收敛性问题；通过定量估计给出MET的上下界。

Result: 识别出SGD的两个阶段：漂移阶段（权重向最近局部最小值集中）和扩散阶段（随机波动帮助逃离次优局部最小值）；提供了漂移阶段浓度现象的定量估计；证明了平均逃逸时间（MET）的上下界；在非凸+退化情形下建立了渐近收敛性结果。

Conclusion: 本文建立了随机优化与PDE理论之间的深层联系，为机器学习中的基本问题（SGD逃离坏最小值的时间、参数收敛性、训练初期演化）提供了理论回答和见解。

Abstract: In this paper we analyze the behaviour of the stochastic gradient descent (SGD), a widely used method in supervised learning for optimizing neural network weights via a minimization of non-convex loss functions. Since the pioneering work of E, Li and Tai (2017), the underlying structure of such processes can be understood via parabolic PDEs of Fokker-Planck type, which are at the core of our analysis. Even if Fokker-Planck equations have a long history and a extensive literature, almost nothing is known when the potential is non-convex or when the diffusion matrix is degenerate, and this is the main difficulty that we face in our analysis.
  We identify two different regimes: in the initial phase of SGD, the loss function drives the weights to concentrate around the nearest local minimum. We refer to this phase as the drift regime and we provide quantitative estimates on this concentration phenomenon. Next, we introduce the diffusion regime, where stochastic fluctuations help the learning process to escape suboptimal local minima. We analyze the Mean Exit Time (MET) and prove upper and lower bounds of the MET. Finally, we address the asymptotic convergence of SGD, for a non-convex cost function and a degenerate diffusion matrix, that do not allow to use the standard approaches, and require new techniques. For this purpose, we exploit two different methods: duality and entropy methods.
  We provide new results about the dynamics and effectiveness of SGD, offering a deep connection between stochastic optimization and PDE theory, and some answers and insights to basic questions in the Machine Learning processes: How long does SGD take to escape from a bad minimum? Do neural network parameters converge using SGD? How do parameters evolve in the first stage of training with SGD?

</details>


### [373] [ASTER: Latent Pseudo-Anomaly Generation for Unsupervised Time-Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13924)
*Romain Hermary,Samet Hicsonmez,Dan Pineau,Abd El Rahman Shabayek,Djamila Aouada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ASTER是一个在潜在空间中生成伪异常来训练Transformer异常检测器的框架，利用预训练LLM增强时序和上下文表示，实现了时序异常检测的最新性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序异常检测面临异常稀少、异质性高以及标注数据稀缺的挑战，现有无监督方法（基于重构、预测或嵌入）存在处理复杂数据能力有限或需要领域专家知识合成异常等不足。

Method: ASTER在潜在空间中直接生成伪异常，避免手工设计异常注入；使用潜在空间解码器产生定制的伪异常来训练基于Transformer的异常分类器；同时利用预训练LLM增强该空间的时序和上下文表示。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上，ASTER取得了最先进的性能，为基于LLM的时序异常检测设立了新标准。

Conclusion: ASTER通过潜在空间伪异常生成和LLM增强表示，有效解决了现有无监督时序异常检测方法的局限，验证了LLM在该任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Time-series anomaly detection (TSAD) is critical in domains such as industrial monitoring, healthcare, and cybersecurity, but it remains challenging due to rare and heterogeneous anomalies and the scarcity of labelled data. This scarcity makes unsupervised approaches predominant, yet existing methods often rely on reconstruction or forecasting, which struggle with complex data, or on embedding-based approaches that require domain-specific anomaly synthesis and fixed distance metrics. We propose ASTER, a framework that generates pseudo-anomalies directly in the latent space, avoiding handcrafted anomaly injections and the need for domain expertise. A latent-space decoder produces tailored pseudo-anomalies to train a Transformer-based anomaly classifier, while a pre-trained LLM enriches the temporal and contextual representations of this space. Experiments on three benchmark datasets show that ASTER achieves state-of-the-art performance and sets a new standard for LLM-based TSAD.

</details>


### [374] [Fourier Multi-Component and Multi-Layer Neural Networks: Unlocking High-Frequency Potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.18959)
*Shijun Zhang,Hongkai Zhao,Yimin Zhong,Haomin Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出傅里叶多分量多层神经网络（FMMNN），结合正弦型激活函数与多分量多层结构，证明其在低秩架构下仍具有指数级函数逼近能力，并展现出比标准全连接网络更优的优化景观，同时提出缩放随机初始化方法加速训练。实验表明FMMNN在振荡函数逼近任务上实现了高精度和良好的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络的架构与激活函数的选择对性能至关重要，而两者的匹配更是实现有效表示和学习的关键。现有方法在这方面的对齐不够充分，尤其是在处理高频目标时标准全连接网络的优化景观较差。

Method: FMMNN将每个分量表示为可训练的固定随机正弦型基函数的线性组合，通过多层组合生成更复杂且自适应的高频特征。同时提出第一层权重的缩放随机初始化方法。

Result: FMMNN在低秩架构下保持指数级函数逼近能力；优化景观显著优于标准全连接网络（尤其对高频目标）；缩放随机初始化在样本充足时加速训练并提升最终性能；在振荡函数逼近基准上取得高精度和良好收敛行为。

Conclusion: FMMNN通过正弦型激活函数与多分量多层结构的结合，有效解决了高频目标逼近的挑战，理论分析和实验验证了其优越性。

Abstract: The architecture of a neural network and the choice of its activation function are both fundamental to its performance. Equally important is ensuring that these two elements are well matched, as their alignment is key to effective representation and learning. In this paper, we introduce the Fourier Multi-Component and Multi-Layer Neural Network (FMMNN), a model that combines sine-type activations with the multi-component and multi-layer structure of MMNNs. In an FMMNN, each component is represented as a trainable linear combination of fixed random sine-type basis functions, while multi-layer composition generates more complex and adaptive high-frequency features. We establish that FMMNNs retain exponential expressive power for function approximation even under a low-rank architectural structure. We also analyze the optimization landscape of FMMNNs and find it to be substantially more favorable than that of standard fully connected neural networks, especially for high-frequency targets. In addition, we propose a scaled random initialization method for the first-layer weights in FMMNNs, which accelerates training and improves final performance when sufficient samples are available. Extensive numerical experiments support our theoretical insights, showing that FMMNNs achieve strong accuracy and favorable convergence behavior on oscillatory function-approximation benchmarks.

</details>


### [375] [BrainDINO: A Brain MRI Foundation Model for Generalizable Clinical Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27277)
*Yizhou Wu,Shansong Wang,Yuheng Li,Mojtaba Safari,Mingzhe Hu,Chih-Wei Chang,Harini Veeraraghavan,Xiaofeng Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BrainDINO是一个基于自蒸馏的脑MRI基础模型，在大规模无标签轴向切片上训练（约660万张，来自20个数据集），通过冻结编码器+轻量任务头的方式，在肿瘤分割、神经退行性疾病分类、脑龄估计、卒中后时间预测、分子状态预测、MRI序列分类和生存建模等多种任务上达到或超过现有基线，尤其在小样本场景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的脑MRI方法多为任务特定且需要大量标注数据，缺乏能够在多种脑MRI任务间泛化的统一表示模型，因此作者希望构建一个无需监督、可跨任务迁移的脑MRI基础模型。

Method: 采用自蒸馏（self-distillation）自监督学习框架，在约660万张未标注的轴向切片上训练BrainDINO模型。推理时冻结编码器参数，仅训练轻量任务头（task heads），避免了全网络微调或体积预训练。

Result: 在肿瘤分割、神经退行性与神经发育疾病分类、脑龄估计、卒中后时间预测、分子状态预测、MRI序列分类、生存建模等多种任务上，BrainDINO持平或超越了基于自然图像和MRI特定的自监督基线方法，在标签稀缺条件下优势尤为显著。表征分析显示其特征结构具有解剖组织性和病理敏感性。

Conclusion: 大规模基于切片的自监督学习可以产生统一的脑MRI表征，支持多样化的神经影像任务，为鲁棒且数据高效的脑影像分析建立了可扩展的基础。

Abstract: Brain MRI underpins a wide range of neuroscientific and clinical applications, yet most learning-based methods remain task-specific and require substantial labeled data. Here we show that a single self-supervised representation can generalize across heterogeneous brain MRI endpoints. We trained BrainDINO, a self-distilled foundation model, on approximately 6.6 million unlabeled axial slices from 20 datasets encompassing broad variation in population, disease, and acquisition setting. Using a frozen encoder with lightweight task heads, BrainDINO supported transfer across tumor segmentation, neurodegenerative and neurodevelopmental conditions classification, brain age estimation, post-stroke temporal prediction, molecular status prediction, MRI sequence classification, and survival modeling. Across tasks and supervision regimes, BrainDINO consistently equaled or exceeded natural-image and MRI-specific self-supervised baselines, with particularly strong advantages under label scarcity. Representation analyses further showed anatomically organized and pathology-sensitive feature structure in the absence of task-specific supervision. Our findings indicate that large-scale slice-wise self-supervised learning can yield a unified brain MRI representation that supports diverse neuroimaging tasks without volumetric pretraining or full-network fine-tuning, establishing a scalable foundation for robust and data-efficient brain imaging analysis. Code is available at https://github.com/mclwu22/BrainDINO

</details>


### [376] [Learning on a Razor's Edge: Identifiability and Singularity of Polynomial Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.11846)
*Vahid Shahverdi,Giovanni Luca Marchetti,Kathlén Kohn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用代数几何工具研究由神经网络参数化的函数空间（神经流形），证明了MLP的参数化存在有限多个参数对应同一函数，而CNN的参数化几乎是一一对应的；同时刻画了神经流形的奇异点，发现MLP的奇异性与均方误差损失的临界点相关，从而从几何角度解释了MLP的稀疏性偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解神经网络参数化函数空间的几何结构，特别是参数可识别性（不同参数是否对应相同函数）以及奇点性质，为神经网络的理论分析提供几何基础。

Method: 采用代数几何作为核心分析工具，研究以充分一般的多项式为激活函数的深度MLP和CNN所构成的神经流形。通过对参数化映射的代数性质进行分析，研究函数的可识别性和流形的奇点结构。

Result: 1) MLP神经流形中几乎处处只有有限个参数选择对应同一函数；CNN的参数化几乎是一一对应的。2) 计算了神经流形的维数。3) 完全刻画了CNN的奇点，部分刻画了MLP的奇点，两者均源于稀疏子网络。4) MLP的奇点往往对应均方误差损失的临界点，而CNN不成立。

Conclusion: 从几何角度揭示了MLP具有稀疏性偏好的原因：其奇异点结构与损失函数的临界点相关联，这一机制在CNN中并不存在。

Abstract: We study function spaces parametrized by neural networks, referred to as neuromanifolds. Specifically, we focus on deep Multi-Layer Perceptrons (MLPs) and Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) with an activation function that is a sufficiently generic polynomial. First, we address the identifiability problem, showing that, for almost all functions in the neuromanifold of an MLP, there exist only finitely many parameter choices yielding that function. For CNNs, the parametrization is generically one-to-one. As a consequence, we compute the dimension of the neuromanifold. Second, we describe singular points of neuromanifolds. We characterize singularities completely for CNNs, and partially for MLPs. In both cases, they arise from sparse subnetworks. For MLPs, we prove that these singularities often correspond to critical points of the mean-squared error loss, which does not hold for CNNs. This provides a geometric explanation of the sparsity bias of MLPs. All of our results leverage tools from algebraic geometry.

</details>


### [377] [Possibilistic Predictive Uncertainty for Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00600)
*Yao Ni,Jeremie Houssineau,Yew-Soon Ong,Piotr Koniusz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Dirichlet-近似可能性后验预测（DAPPr），一种基于可能性理论的不确定性量化框架。它通过将参数的后验可能性投影到预测空间并用可学习的Dirichlet可能性函数近似，实现了原则性推导与计算效率的统一，在多个基准上达到或超越现有二阶预测器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性建模方法面临根本困境：贝叶斯方法提供原则性估计但计算成本高昂，而高效二阶预测器缺乏其特定目标与认知不确定性量化之间的严谨联系。为了解决这一困境，需要一种既具有原则性推导又保持计算效率的不确定性量化方法。

Method: DAPPr基于可能性理论构建框架：首先定义参数上的后验可能性分布，通过上确界算子将其投影到预测空间，然后使用可学习的Dirichlet可能性函数近似投影后的后验。这种投影-近似策略产生了一个具有闭式解的简单训练目标。

Result: 在多种基准测试上的大量实验表明，DAPPr在不确定性量化性能上与现有最先进的二阶预测器相比具有竞争力或更优表现，同时保持了原则性推导和计算效率。

Conclusion: DAPPr成功解决了贝叶斯方法计算昂贵与二阶预测器缺乏理论严谨性之间的矛盾，为深度神经网络的不确定性建模提供了一个既原则又高效的框架。

Abstract: Deep neural networks achieve impressive results across diverse applications, yet their overconfidence on unseen inputs necessitates reliable epistemic uncertainty modeling. Existing methods for uncertainty modeling face a fundamental dilemma: Bayesian approaches provide principled estimates but remain computationally prohibitive, while efficient second-order predictors lack rigorous connections between their specific objectives and epistemic uncertainty quantification. To resolve this dilemma, we introduce Dirichlet-approximated possibilistic posterior predictions (DAPPr), a principled framework grounded in possibility theory. We define a possibilistic posterior over parameters, project it to the prediction space via supremum operators, and approximate the projected posterior using learnable Dirichlet possibility functions. This projection-and-approximation strategy yields a simple training objective with closed-form solutions. Despite its simplicity, extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks show that DAPPr achieves competitive or superior uncertainty quantification performance over state-of-the-art second-order predictors while maintaining both principled derivation and computational efficiency. Code is available at https://github.com/MaxwellYaoNi/DAPPr.

</details>


### [378] [Lightweight and Interpretable Transformer via Mixed Graph Algorithm Unrolling for Traffic Forecast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.13102)
*Ji Qi,Tam Thuc Do,Mingxiao Liu,Zhuoshi Pan,Yuzhe Li,Gene Cheung,H. Vicky Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级、可解释的类Transformer神经网络，通过展开基于混合图优化的算法来进行时空交通预测，在保持竞争性能的同时大幅减少参数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的"黑箱"Transformer模型参数量大且可解释性差，作者希望构建一种轻量级且可解释的神经网络来实现时空交通预测。

Method: 构建两个图：无向图G^u捕捉空间相关性，有向图G^d捕捉时间序列关系；设计新的一范数和二范数变分项来量化并促进信号在图上平滑（低频重构）；基于ADMM设计迭代算法并展开为前馈网络进行数据驱动参数学习；周期性插入图学习模块充当自注意力机制。

Result: 展开网络在交通预测任务上达到了与最先进方法相当的竞争性能，同时参数量大幅减少。

Conclusion: 通过将优化算法展开为网络，可以在保持可解释性的同时实现轻量化的高效时空预测模型。

Abstract: Unlike conventional "black-box" transformers with classical self-attention mechanism, we build a lightweight and interpretable transformer-like neural net by unrolling a mixed-graph-based optimization algorithm to forecast traffic with spatial and temporal dimensions. We construct two graphs: an undirected graph $\mathcal{G}^u$ capturing spatial correlations across geography, and a directed graph $\mathcal{G}^d$ capturing sequential relationships over time. We predict future samples of signal $\mathbf{x}$, assuming it is "smooth" with respect to both $\mathcal{G}^u$ and $\mathcal{G}^d$, where we design new $\ell_2$ and $\ell_1$-norm variational terms to quantify and promote signal smoothness (low-frequency reconstruction) on a directed graph. We design an iterative algorithm based on alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM), and unroll it into a feed-forward network for data-driven parameter learning. We periodically insert graph learning modules for $\mathcal{G}^u$ and $\mathcal{G}^d$ that play the role of self-attention. Experiments show that our unrolled networks achieve competitive traffic forecast performance as state-of-the-art prediction schemes, while reducing parameter counts drastically.

</details>


### [379] [ExPLAIND: Unifying Model, Data, and Training Attribution to Study Model Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20076)
*Florian Eichin,Yupei Du,Philipp Mondorf,Maria Matveev,Barbara Plank,Michael A. Hedderich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ExPLAIND是一个统一的理论框架，通过梯度路径核将AdamW训练的模型转化为核机器，从而集成模型组件、数据和训练轨迹，支持跨粒度解释模型行为与训练动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事后可解释性方法通常孤立地归因模型行为到组件、数据或训练轨迹，且局限于局部到全局频谱中的单一粒度，缺乏统一视角，可能遗漏关键交互信息。

Method: 基于梯度路径核的理论推广，将AdamW训练的模型重新表述为核机器，从核特征映射中推导出新的参数级和步级影响分数，实现模型行为的多粒度分解。

Result: 在Transformer的Grokking现象中支持了先前提出的学习阶段划分，并细化最终阶段为外层围绕记忆后形成的表示流水线进行对齐；在EuroLLM预训练中揭示了两个阶段动态：第一阶段以外层MLP学习为特征，第二阶段中间注意力层影响相对增加。

Conclusion: ExPLAIND建立了统一框架用于解释模型行为和训练动态，实验验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Post-hoc interpretability methods typically attribute a model's behavior to its components, data, or training trajectory in isolation, and are often tied to a particular level of granularity along the local-to-global spectrum. This leads to explanations that lack a unified view and may miss key interactions. We present ExPLAIND, a theoretically grounded, unified framework that integrates model components, data, and training trajectory while supporting explanations across granularities. We generalize recent work on gradient path kernels, reformulating models trained by AdamW as kernel machines. From the resulting kernel feature maps, we derive novel parameter-wise and step-wise influence scores. We empirically validate the resulting decomposition of model behavior in several settings and apply ExPLAIND to two case studies. Our findings on a Transformer exhibiting Grokking support previously proposed learning phases, while refining the final phase as one in which outer layers align around a representation pipeline learned after memorization. For EuroLLM pretraining, ExPLAIND reveals a two-phase dynamic, with the first characterized by outer-layer MLP learning and the second by increased relative influence of intermediate attention layers. These results establish ExPLAIND as a unified framework for interpreting model behavior and training dynamics.

</details>


### [380] [LLM-ODDR: A Large Language Model Framework for Joint Order Dispatching and Driver Repositioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.22695)
*Tengfei Lyu,Siyuan Feng,Hao Liu,Hai Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM-ODDR框架，利用大语言模型联合优化网约车平台的订单分配与司机重定位，通过多目标订单价值评估、公平性感知分配和时空需求感知重定位三个核心组件，在真实数据上取得了超越传统方法的效果，并首次探索了LLM作为决策智能体在网约车任务中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统网约车订单分配与司机重定位方法（组合优化、规则启发式、强化学习）忽略了司机收入公平性、可解释性以及对真实动态环境的适应性，亟需一种能综合多目标、具备可解释性的新型决策框架。

Method: 提出LLM-ODDR框架，包含三个组件：(1) 多目标引导的订单价值精炼模块，综合多个目标评估订单价值；(2) 公平性感知的订单分配模块，平衡平台收益与司机收入公平；(3) 时空需求感知的司机重定位模块，基于历史模式和供需预测优化空闲车辆位置。此外开发了领域知识微调的JointDR-GPT模型。

Result: 在曼哈顿出租车真实数据集上的实验表明，本框架在有效性、对异常条件的适应性以及决策可解释性方面显著优于传统方法。通过并行分解和模型蒸馏可将延迟降低到生产可用水平。

Conclusion: 本研究首次探索了LLM作为决策智能体在网约车订单分配与司机重定位任务中的应用，为将大语言模型集成到智能交通系统中建立了基础性洞见。

Abstract: Ride-hailing platforms face significant challenges in optimizing order dispatching and driver repositioning operations in dynamic urban environments. Traditional approaches based on combinatorial optimization, rule-based heuristics, and reinforcement learning often overlook driver income fairness, interpretability, and adaptability to real-world dynamics. To address these gaps, we propose LLM-ODDR, a novel framework leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) for joint Order Dispatching and Driver Repositioning (ODDR) in ride-hailing services. LLM-ODDR framework comprises three key components: (1) Multi-objective-guided Order Value Refinement, which evaluates orders by considering multiple objectives to determine their overall value; (2) Fairness-aware Order Dispatching, which balances platform revenue with driver income fairness; and (3) Spatiotemporal Demand-Aware Driver Repositioning, which optimizes idle vehicle placement based on historical patterns and projected supply. We also develop JointDR-GPT, a fine-tuned model optimized for ODDR tasks with domain knowledge. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets from Manhattan taxi operations demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms traditional methods in terms of effectiveness, adaptability to anomalous conditions, and decision interpretability. To our knowledge, this is the first exploration of LLMs as decision-making agents in ride-hailing ODDR tasks, establishing foundational insights for integrating advanced language models within intelligent transportation systems. While the current framework incurs higher computational costs than traditional methods, we show that parallel decomposition and model distillation can reduce latency to production-viable levels for deployment.

</details>


### [381] [How Reliable are Fairness Audits with Unreliable Data?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.23033)
*Yash Vardhan Tomar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究公平性审计中受保护标签缺失对审计建议可靠性的影响，提出种子校准压力测试方法，发现标签缺失在多数情况下不会超出完整标签基线，但在零标签访问时模型退化为经验风险最小化基线，阈值优化可将单轴公平性转化为交叉危害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公平性审计是负责任机器学习部署的关键环节，但审计建议在受保护标签信息不完整情况下的可靠性尚不明确，现有研究对标签缺失机制的理解不足。

Method: 提出种子校准压力测试（seed-calibrated stress test），将缺失效应与完整标签下种子随机性效应分离；在ACS/Folktables任务上评估不同缺失率下多种缓解方法的表现。

Result: 部分标签保留时缓解方法未显著偏离完整标签基线；零标签访问时模型退化为经验风险最小化基线；阈值优化能在单轴公平性上产生超过种子基线的交叉群体危害。

Conclusion: 受保护标签缺失的报告应包含种子零校准、候选集上下文以及交叉影响评估，避免将其简单视为审计脆弱的证据。

Abstract: Fairness audits are a key component of responsible machine-learning deployment. Yet, audit-recommendation reliability under incomplete protected-label access is still poorly understood. In this work, we focused on protected-label missingness in fairness mitigation audits. We introduced a seed-calibrated stress test to separate missingness effects from seed-to-seed movement already present under complete labels. Across ACS/Folktables tasks, missingness settings that retain some protected labels usually do not move selected mitigation methods beyond a complete-label seed-to-seed baseline. At $0%$ protected-label access, candidates collapse to an empirical-risk-minimization baseline and deterministic tie-breaking rather than revealing a broad missingness effect. We also found that threshold optimization can turn fairness gains on a single protected axis into intersectional harm above a seed baseline, and this threshold-optimizer finding persists under random-forest validation. Overall, our results highlight that protected-label missingness should be reported with seed-null calibration, candidate-set context, and intersectional consequences before it is treated as evidence of audit fragility.

</details>


### [382] [PlaceRep: Geospatial Place Representation Learning from Large-Scale Point-of-Interest Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.02921)
*Mohammad Hashemi,Hossein Amiri,Andreas Zufle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PlaceRep提出了一种基于POI聚类的地理空间表示学习方法，通过聚合空间和语义相关的兴趣点来构建场所级别的嵌入表示，无需模型预训练即可在多尺度上自动识别场所，在人口密度估计和房价预测任务上优于大多数现有方法，并实现高达100倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地理空间表示学习方法通常将POI聚合到预定义的行政区域（如人口普查单元或邮政编码区），但POI往往形成跨越、包含或超出这些边界的语义上有意义的群组，无法反映真实的人类活动和城市功能。因此需要一种能自动识别语义相关POI组并生成场所级表示的方法。

Method: PlaceRep通过聚类空间和语义相关的POI来构建场所级表示：从大规模Foursquare POI图中对POI进行聚类，自动识别多尺度上的场所，生成通用的城市区域嵌入表示，并消除了模型预训练步骤。

Result: 在人口密度估计和房价预测任务中，PlaceRep优于大多数基于图的SOTA地理空间表示学习方法，并在大规模POI图上生成区域级表示时实现高达100倍的加速。

Conclusion: PlaceRep通过POI聚类实现了无需预训练、多尺度、高效的场所级地理空间表示学习，为城市功能区域分析提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Learning effective representations of urban environments requires capturing spatial structure beyond fixed administrative boundaries. Existing geospatial representation learning approaches typically aggregate Points of Interest (POIs) into pre-defined administrative regions such as census units or ZIP code areas, assigning a single embedding to each region. However, POIs often form semantically meaningful groups that extend across, within, or beyond these boundaries, defining places that better reflect human activity and urban function. To address this limitation, we propose PlaceRep, a geospatial representation learning method that constructs place-level representations by clustering spatially and semantically related POIs. PlaceRep summarizes large-scale POI graphs from U.S. Foursquare data to produce general-purpose urban region embeddings while automatically identifying places across multiple spatial scales. By eliminating model pre-training, PlaceRep provides a scalable and efficient solution for multi-granular geospatial analysis. Experiments using the tasks of population density estimation and housing price prediction as downstream tasks show that PlaceRep outperforms most state-of-the-art graph-based geospatial representation learning methods and achieves up to a x100 speedup in generating region-level representations on large-scale POI graphs. The implementation of PlaceRep is available at https://github.com/mohammadhashemii/PlaceRep.

</details>


### [383] [Single vs. Multiple Branches in DeepONet and S-DeepONet: Network Architecture Follows Coupling in Multiphysics Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.03660)
*Jaewan Park,Kazuma Kobayashi,Qibang Liu,Seid Koric,Diab Abueidda,Syed Bahauddin Alam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度算子网络在强耦合多物理场问题中的架构选择，发现单分支网络在紧耦合场景下优于多分支设计，加速比可达1.8×10⁴倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度算子网络在单物理场问题中表现成功，但其在捕捉多物理场耦合系统中非线性相互作用的效果尚不明确，因此需要探究网络架构是否应反映物理耦合强度。

Method: 对比了单分支与多分支架构，涵盖前馈和序列循环两种形式，在反应-扩散、非线性热电耦合、粘塑性热机械耦合三个代表性系统中进行实验。

Result: 单分支网络在紧耦合场景下通过鼓励共享潜在表征而持续优于多分支变体；多分支设计对于解耦或单物理场任务更有利；训练后的代理模型比基于物理的求解器快高达1.8×10⁴倍。

Conclusion: 神经算子架构的设计应匹配物理耦合强度：强耦合场景优先选择单分支架构以实现共享表征学习，弱耦合场景则可采用多分支设计。

Abstract: `Real-time prediction of complex physical systems requires surrogate models that learn from data while representing strong multiphysics coupling. Deep Operator Networks have shown success in single-physics problems, yet their effectiveness in capturing nonlinear interactions in coupled systems (such as thermo-mechanical or electro-thermal coupling) remains underexplored. Here we pose a practical question: should the architecture of a neural operator reflect the strength of physical coupling it aims to model? We compare single-branch and multi-branch designs, in both feedforward and sequential recurrent forms, across three representative systems: a reaction--diffusion problem with heterogeneous sources, a nonlinear thermo-electrical problem with temperature-dependent conductivity and Joule heating, and a viscoplastic thermo-mechanical model of steel solidification. Single-branch networks consistently outperform multi-branch variants in tightly coupled regimes by encouraging shared latent representations, whereas multi-branch designs remain favorable for decoupled or single-physics tasks. Once trained, these surrogates deliver full-field predictions up to $1.8 \times 10^4$ times faster than physics-based solvers.

</details>


### [384] [Meta-Learning Transformers to Improve In-Context Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.05019)
*Lorenzo Braccaioli,Anna Vettoruzzo,Prabhant Singh,Joaquin Vanschoren,Mohamed-Rafik Bouguelia,Nicola Conci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用多个小型、领域特定的数据集集合来训练上下文学习模型，替代传统的大规模非结构化数据集。实验表明，这种策略提高了模型在训练领域外的泛化能力，同时在模块化和可替换性方面具有优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文学习训练范式依赖大规模非结构化数据集，这些数据集存储成本高、质量与平衡性难以评估，且因包含敏感信息而存在隐私和伦理问题。为克服这些局限，作者提出使用多个小型、领域特定的数据集集合作为替代训练策略。

Method: 采用元学习框架，在Meta-Album数据集集合上训练上下文学习器。在三种设置下进行评估：(1) 受控环境：测试领域完全排除在训练知识之外；(2) 持续学习场景：信息仅在有限时间内可访问，检验模型对遗忘的鲁棒性；(3) 无监督场景：更具挑战性的设置。

Result: 增加数据质量和多样性可提升上下文学习器在训练领域外的泛化能力，且性能与在单个大规模数据集上训练的模型相当。在受控、持续学习和无监督场景下，模型均保持了良好的上下文预测泛化能力。

Conclusion: 使用精选数据集集合训练Transformer仍能实现上下文预测的泛化，同时提供模块化和可替换性的优势。

Abstract: In-context learning enables transformer models to generalize to new tasks based solely on input prompts, without any need for weight updates. However, existing training paradigms typically rely on large, unstructured datasets that are costly to store, difficult to evaluate for quality and balance, and pose privacy and ethical concerns due to the inclusion of sensitive information. Motivated by these limitations and risks, we propose an alternative training strategy where we leverage a collection of multiple, small-scale, and domain-specific datasets. We empirically demonstrate that the increased quality and diversity of such data improve the generalization abilities of in-context learners beyond their training domain, while achieving comparable performance with models trained on a single large-scale dataset. We investigate this paradigm by leveraging meta-learning to train an in-context learner on the Meta-Album collection under several settings. Firstly, we show the performance in a controlled environment, where the test domain is completely excluded from the training knowledge. Secondly, we explore the robustness of these models to forgetting in a continual scenario where the information is accessible for a limited time. Finally, we explore the more challenging unsupervised scenario. Our findings demonstrate that transformers still generalize for in-context prediction when trained on a curated dataset collection while offering advantages in modularity and replaceability.

</details>


### [385] [Reconstructing Template-Memorized Images from Natural Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.07947)
*Sol Yarkoni,Mahmood Sharif,Roi Livni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种低资源、低数据依赖的攻击方法，能够从扩散模型的训练数据中重建图像，揭示了使用爬虫电商数据导致的模板化提示与图像记忆之间的根本漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成模型（如扩散模型）存在隐私、版权和数据管理方面的风险。虽然已有工作展示了训练数据可被重建，但现有方法通常依赖高计算资源、部分训练数据集访问或精心设计的提示词。本研究旨在开发一种低资源、少数据依赖的攻击，揭示更广泛存在的安全隐患。

Method: 基于对先前工作的洞察，利用领域知识，聚焦于爬虫电商数据中模板化布局与模式化文本提示之间的紧密关联，发现并利用这一根本性漏洞。通过低资源条件下的提示查找，识别出看似良性但能导致训练数据重建的提示词。

Result: 发现简单提示如"blue Unisex T-Shirt"即可生成真实个体的面部图像；结合真实世界的提示数据，可以复现记忆的可视化元素。攻击所需资源低，几乎不需要对训练数据的访问权限。

Conclusion: 该攻击揭示了扩散模型在训练数据记忆方面的脆弱性，特别是使用爬虫电商数据时模板化提示带来的风险。这类重建可能无意中发生，即使是没有专业知识的用户也可能触发，需引起对数据管理和模型安全的高度关注。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative models, such as diffusion models, have raised concerns related to privacy, copyright infringement, and data stewardship. To better understand and control these risks, prior work has introduced techniques and attacks that reconstruct images, or parts of images, from training data. While these results demonstrate that training data can be recovered, existing methods often rely on high computational resources, partial access to the training set, or carefully engineered prompts.
  In this work, we present a new attack that requires low resources, assumes little to no access to the training data, and identifies seemingly benign prompts that can lead to potentially risky image reconstruction. We further show that such reconstructions may occur unintentionally, even for users without specialized knowledge. For example, we observe that for one existing model, the prompt ``blue Unisex T-Shirt'' generates the face of a real individual. Moreover, by combining the identified vulnerabilities with real-world prompt data, we discover prompts that reproduce memorized visual elements.
  Our approach builds on insights from prior work and leverages domain knowledge to expose a fundamental vulnerability arising from the use of scraped e-commerce data, where templated layouts and images are closely tied to pattern-like textual prompts.
  The code for our attack is publicly available at https://github.com/TheSolY/lr-tmi.

</details>


### [386] [One Token to Fool LLM-as-a-Judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.08794)
*Yulai Zhao,Haolin Liu,Dian Yu,Sunyuan Kung,Meijia Chen,Haitao Mi,Dong Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了生成式奖励模型在参考依赖范式（如RLVR）中存在的系统性奖励黑客攻击漏洞：浅层输入（如非词符号或通用推理开头语）能持续引发假阳性奖励。作者提出了一种基于截断模型输出的数据增强策略，训练出的Master Reward Models在保持标准评估性能的同时，展现出对这类"万能钥匙"攻击的最优鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为自动化评估器在RLVR等参考依赖场景中日益被信任，但这类基于生成式奖励模型的评估机制存在潜在的奖励黑客攻击漏洞，威胁其可靠性。

Method: 系统评估发现生成式奖励模型对浅层"万能钥匙"输入存在系统性假阳性奖励问题；提出使用截断模型输出作为对抗性负样本的数据增强策略，训练Master Reward Models。

Result: "万能钥匙"输入（如非词符号"：""。"或通用推理开头语）能一致性地引发多种模型（包括GPT-o1和Claude-4）的假阳性奖励；Master-RMs在标准评估中保持高性能的同时，展现出最优鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LLM评判器的鲁棒性假设受到挑战；所提出的Master Reward Models有效缓解了"万能钥匙"攻击；研究提供了跨模型规模、提示变体和推理时策略的全面分析，为未来鲁棒LLM评估研究提供指导。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly trusted as automated judges, assisting evaluation and providing reward signals for training other models, particularly in reference-based settings like Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR). However, we uncover a critical vulnerability even in this reference-based paradigm: generative reward models are systematically susceptible to reward hacking. We find that superficial inputs, which we term ''master keys'' such as non-word symbols (e.g., '':'' or ''.'') or generic reasoning openers (e.g., ''Thought process:'' or ''Let's solve this problem step by step.''), can consistently elicit false positive rewards without any substantive reasoning. Our systematic evaluation demonstrates this is a widespread failure affecting a diverse range of models, including leading proprietary systems such as GPT-o1 and Claude-4. These results challenge the assumed robustness of LLM judges and pose a significant threat to their reliability. To address this, we propose a simple yet effective data augmentation strategy using truncated model outputs as adversarial negative examples. The resulting Master Reward Models (Master-RMs) demonstrate state-of-the-art robustness against these ''master key'' attacks while maintaining high performance in standard evaluation settings. We supplement these findings with a comprehensive analysis of the vulnerability across model scales, prompt variations, and common inference-time strategies, offering insights to guide future research on robust LLM evaluation. We release our robust, general-domain reward models and the synthetic training data at https://huggingface.co/sarosavo/Master-RM and https://huggingface.co/datasets/sarosavo/Master-RM.

</details>


### [387] [Decoding the Multimodal Maze: A Systematic Review on the Adoption of Explainability in Multimodal Attention-based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.04427)
*Md Raisul Kibria,Sébastien Lafond,Janan Arslan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了2020年至2024年初关于多模态模型可解释性的研究，发现现有工作主要集中在视觉-语言和纯语言模型上，注意力机制是最常用的解释方法，但难以充分捕捉模态间交互；评估方法缺乏系统性和标准化。文章据此提出了促进多模态XAI研究规范化的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态学习取得显著进展，尤其是基于注意力的模型在多种任务中表现优异；同时，可解释人工智能（XAI）的需求日益增长。然而，多模态模型的可解释性研究缺乏系统性梳理，现有评估方法不成熟，亟需对该领域进行综合分析和标准化建议。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，分析2020年1月至2024年初发表的多模态模型可解释性相关研究，从模型架构、涉及模态、解释算法和评估方法等多个维度进行考察，并整合最新的前沿进展进行补充分析。

Result: 大多数研究集中于视觉-语言和纯语言模型；注意力机制是最常用的解释方法，但难以充分捕捉模态间交互；多模态XAI的评估方法普遍缺乏系统性、一致性、鲁棒性，且未考虑模态特定的认知和上下文因素。

Conclusion: 提出了促进多模态XAI研究规范化、透明化和标准化评估与报告实践的综合建议，旨在推动更具可解释性、可问责性和负责任的多模态AI系统的发展。

Abstract: Multimodal learning has witnessed remarkable advancements in recent years, particularly with the integration of attention-based models, leading to significant performance gains across a variety of tasks. Parallel to this progress, the demand for explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) has spurred a growing body of research aimed at interpreting the complex decision-making processes of these models. This systematic literature review analyzes research published between January 2020 and early 2024 that focuses on the explainability of multimodal models. Framed within the broader goals of XAI, we examine the literature across multiple dimensions, including model architecture, modalities involved, explanation algorithms and evaluation methodologies. Our analysis reveals that most studies are concentrated on vision-language and language-only models, with attention-based techniques being the most commonly employed for explanation. However, these methods often fall short in capturing the full spectrum of interactions between modalities, a challenge further compounded by the architectural heterogeneity across domains. Importantly, we find that evaluation methods for XAI in multimodal settings are largely non-systematic, lacking consistency, robustness, and consideration for modality-specific cognitive and contextual factors. To address these gaps, we not only synthesize findings from the surveyed works but also incorporate a complementary analysis that integrates recent and emerging advances driving multimodal explainability. Based on these insights, we provide a comprehensive set of recommendations aimed at promoting rigorous, transparent, and standardized evaluation and reporting practices in multimodal XAI research. Our goal is to support future research in more interpretable, accountable, and responsible multimodal AI systems, with explainability at their core.

</details>


### [388] [Retrieval-Augmented Foundation Models for Water Level Prediction in the Everglades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.04888)
*Rahuul Rangaraj,Jimeng Shi,Rajendra Paudel,Giri Narasimhan,Yanzhao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WaterRAF框架，利用检索增强机制（统计相似性和互信息检索）为预训练时间序列基础模型提供历史水文情景上下文，显著提升了Everglades地区长期水位预测精度，尤其在极端事件中表现更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前先进的时间序列基础模型在通用任务上表现良好，但在水文学等特定领域的水位预测中性能仍有限，亟需提升其在领域特定任务上的预测能力。

Method: 构建了Everglades地区水位预测专用数据集；采用检索增强机制，从历史观测档案中检索类似的多变量水文事件，通过统计相似性检索和互信息检索两种策略为预训练模型提供增强的输入上下文。

Result: 检索增强一致性地改善了长期水位预测，在极端事件期间获得了更显著的性能提升；两种检索策略均有效，其中互信息检索策略表现更优。

Conclusion: 基于类比的历史情景检索能够有效增强预训练时间序列基础模型在环境科学中的应用；该框架具有通用性，可推广至其他水文系统；代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Accurate water level forecasting in the Everglades is essential for flood mitigation, drought management, water resource planning, and biodiversity conservation. While recent time-series foundation models have shown strong performance on generic tasks (represented in their pre-training), their effectiveness in domain-specific applications remains insufficiently understood. In this work, we curate a domain-specific dataset for water-level forecasting in the Everglades and observe that the performance of current state-of-the-art models remains limited. To address this gap, we leverage a retrieval-augmented mechanism that retrieves analogous multivariate hydrological episodes from an external archive of historical observations to enrich the input context of those pre-trained models. We study two retrieval strategies, statistical similarity-based retrieval and mutual information-based retrieval, and analyze how incorporating retrieved historical contexts affects predictive performance. Extensive experiments show that retrieval augmentation consistently improves long-horizon water level forecasts and yields disproportionately larger gains during extreme events, which is particularly critical for environmental decision-making. Our study provides empirical evidence that analog-based retrieval can benefit pretrained time-series foundation models in environmental science, offering practical insights into their strengths, limitations, and failure modes when applied to hydrological forecasting in the Everglades. Although evaluated in the Everglades, the proposed framework is general and can be applied to other hydrological systems given time series data. The code and data have been made publicly available at https://github.com/rahuul2992000/WaterRAF.

</details>


### [389] [Equivariant Flow Matching for Symmetry-Breaking Bifurcation Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.03340)
*Fleur Hendriks,Ondřej Rokoš,Martin Doškář,Marc G. D. Geers,Vlado Menkovski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将生成式AI（流匹配）与等变架构及最优传输耦合机制相结合，作为对非线性动力系统分岔现象中多稳态解的概率分布进行建模的原则性方法，在弯曲梁、Allen-Cahn方程等系统中准确捕捉多模态分布和对称性破缺分岔，显著优于非概率和变分方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性动力系统中的分岔现象常导致多个共存稳定解，尤其在对称性破缺情况下。确定性机器学习模型无法捕捉这种多重性，会平均化不同解并丢失低对称性结果。需要一种能对分岔结果全概率分布进行建模的原则性方法。

Method: 将流匹配（flow matching）与等变架构（equivariant architectures）及基于最优传输的耦合机制相结合。进一步将等变流匹配泛化为对称耦合策略，使预测与目标输出在群作用下对齐，从而在等变设置中实现准确学习。

Result: 在从简单概念系统到弯曲梁、Allen-Cahn方程等物理问题的多个系统上验证了该方法，能准确捕捉多模态分布和对称性破缺分岔。流匹配显著优于非概率方法和变分方法。

Conclusion: 该方法为高维系统中多稳态建模提供了原则性且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Bifurcation phenomena in nonlinear dynamical systems often lead to multiple coexisting stable solutions, particularly in the presence of symmetry breaking. Deterministic machine learning models are unable to capture this multiplicity, averaging over solutions and failing to represent lower-symmetry outcomes. In this work, we formalize the use of generative AI, specifically flow matching, as a principled way to model the full probability distribution over bifurcation outcomes. Our approach builds on existing techniques by combining flow matching with equivariant architectures and an optimal-transport-based coupling mechanism. We generalize equivariant flow matching to a symmetric coupling strategy that aligns predicted and target outputs under group actions, allowing accurate learning in equivariant settings. We validate our approach on a range of systems, from simple conceptual systems to physical problems such as buckling beams and the Allen--Cahn equation. The results demonstrate that the approach accurately captures multimodal distributions and symmetry-breaking bifurcations. Moreover, our results demonstrate that flow matching significantly outperforms non-probabilistic and variational methods. This offers a principled and scalable solution for modeling multistability in high-dimensional systems.

</details>


### [390] [PLaID++: A Preference Aligned Language Model for Targeted Inorganic Materials Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.07150)
*Andy Xu,Rohan Desai,Larry Wang,Ethan Ritz,Gabriel Hope*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对材料生成中约束满足与多样性的需求，提出PLaID++，利用对称性感知的Wyckoff文本表示和温度缩放熵正则化，通过RLVR后训练实现稳定、独特且新颖的晶体结构生成，相比先前方法效率提升约50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在科学问题（如材料生成）中，目标并非产生单一正确答案，而是生成满足约束的多样候选集合。现有的RLVR方法主要关注正确性，缺乏对多样性和约束满足的适配。

Method: 1）提出紧凑、对称性感知的Wyckoff文本表示，提升计算效率并鼓励从物理先验中泛化；2）采用温度缩放作为熵正则化项，对抗模式坍缩并促进探索；3）将对称性约束直接编码到文本中，引导模型输出至目标化学空间。

Result: PLaID++生成热力学稳定、独特且新颖的结构，相比先前方法的效率提升约50%，并能按条件生成具有所需空间群性质的晶体。

Conclusion: 展示了将自然语言处理中的后训练技术（如RLVR）适配到材料设计的可行性，为定向高效发现新材料铺平了道路。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a promising approach to improve correctness in LLMs, however, in many scientific problems, the objective is not necessarily to produce the correct answer, but instead to produce a diverse array of candidates which satisfy a set of constraints. We study this challenge in the context of materials generation. To this end, we introduce PLaID++, an LLM post-trained for stable and property-guided crystal generation. We find that performance hinges on our crystallographic representation and reward formulation. First, we introduce a compact, symmetry-informed Wyckoff text representation which improves computational efficiency and encourages generalization from physical priors. Second, we demonstrate that temperature scaling acts as an entropy regularizer which counteracts mode collapse and encourages exploration. By encoding symmetry constraints directly into text and guiding model outputs towards desirable chemical space, PLaID++ generates structures that are thermodynamically stable, unique, and novel at a $\sim$50\% greater rate than prior methods and conditionally generates structures with desired space group properties. Our work demonstrates the potential of adapting post-training techniques from natural language processing to materials design, paving the way for targeted and efficient discovery of novel materials.

</details>


### [391] [Structuring The Future: Diffusion LLM Speculative Decoding via Calibrated Draft Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.18085)
*Sudhanshu Agrawal,Risheek Garrepalli,Raghavv Goel,Christopher Lott,Fatih Porikli,Mingu Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Spiffy是一种专为扩散LLM设计的推测解码算法，通过自推测、有向草图图和动态剪枝等技术，在保证输出分布不变的前提下，实现了高达8.6倍的模型推理减少和6.3倍的token速率加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散LLM（dLLM）相比自回归LLM（AR-LLM）具有显著更高的token生成速率潜力，但缺乏针对dLLM独特生成特性的推理加速方法，现有AR-LLM的推测解码技术无法直接应用。

Method: Spiffy采用自推测（auto-speculation）消除独立草稿模型的开销，设计新颖的有向草图图（directed draft graph）结构以利用dLLM双向、块状生成特性，离线校准草图图以最大化接受率，并在推理时动态剪枝以提高计算效率。同时结合KV缓存和基于阈值的动态去掩码技术。

Result: 在LLaDA、Dream和SDAR模型上，Spiffy实现了最多8.6倍的模型推理次数减少和6.3倍的token速率加速。

Conclusion: Spiffy成功将推测解码思想适配到dLLM，在严格保持输出分布的前提下大幅提升推理效率，为扩散LLM的高效部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Diffusion LLMs (dLLMs) have recently emerged as a powerful alternative to autoregressive LLMs (AR-LLMs) with the potential to operate at significantly higher token-generation rates. To unlock this potential, we present Spiffy, a speculative decoding algorithm to accelerate dLLM inference while provably preserving the model's output distribution.
